### [1] [Spin Hamiltonian for Quantum Simulation of Minimally Truncated $3+1$D SU(2) Lattice Gauge Theory and Testing Eigenstate Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36608)
*Ziyuan Li,Xiaojun Yao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文构建了在最小截断j_max=1/2下，3+1维纯SU(2)格点规范理论的空间-规范单态哈密顿量，并在量子比特上实现；通过精确对角化小尺寸晶格，验证了本征态热化假说（ETH）成立，为未来非平衡动力学的量子模拟提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证3+1维截断SU(2)格点规范理论是否满足本征态热化假说（ETH），为其在量子计算机上的实时模拟提供理论基础和基准。

Method: 在j_max=1/2最小截断下，利用高斯定律约束与规范不变环算符的冗余性，构造空间-规范单态哈密顿量并映射到量子比特；对2×3×3晶格进行精确对角化，分析能级统计、局域能量密度算符矩阵元及本征态纠缠熵。

Result: 在耦合强度g=0.3的遍历区，局域能量密度算符矩阵元和所有本征态的纠缠熵均符合ETH预期；n×n×n晶格需2(n−1)^3+3(n−1)^2个量子比特。

Conclusion: 3+1维截断SU(2)格点规范理论满足ETH，具备量子热化行为，可作为未来非平衡动力学量子模拟的可靠基准。

Abstract: We construct the spatial-gauge-singlet Hamiltonian of pure SU(2) lattice gauge theory in $3+1$ dimensions ($3+1$D) on qubits under the minimal truncation $j_{\rm max}=1/2$, building on previous work on the hyperhoneycomb lattice, and test the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). By enforcing Gauss's law and exploiting the redundancy among gauge-invariant loop operators, we find the minimal set of independent loop variables that can be bijectively mapped onto qubits, and write the Hamiltonian as a sum of Pauli strings. An $n\times n\times n$ lattice with fixed boundary conditions requires $2(n-1)^3+3(n-1)^2$ qubits. We exactly diagonalize the Hamiltonian on a $2\times 3\times 3$ lattice and identify the coupling range of quantum ergodicity from the level statistics. At the ergodic coupling $g=0.3$, we calculate the matrix elements of local energy density operators and the entanglement entropies of all eigenstates and find them consistent with the ETH. These results show that the $3+1$D truncated SU(2) lattice gauge theory thermalizes in accordance with the ETH and provide a benchmark for future real-time quantum simulations of non-equilibrium dynamics.

</details>


### [2] [Easier parameter scans with hmcdj](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37120)
*Alexis Verney-Provatas,Ed Bennett,Gaurav Ray*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: hmcdj is a new open-source tool built on the Grid library to simplify and automate the generation of large-scale lattice gauge theory ensembles, featuring YAML-driven configuration, automatic MD step-size tuning, ILDG-compliant output, and HPC scheduler integration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To ease the process of generating large numbers of gauge ensembles for lattice gauge theories, reducing boilerplate and improving job management in high-performance computing environments.

Method: Developed hmcdj as a C++ tool atop the Grid library, enabling concise theory specification and runtime control via YAML; incorporates automatic molecular dynamics step-size tuning, ILDG-compliant I/O, and dynamic trajectory adjustment based on HPC job time limits.

Result: A flexible, production-ready tool supporting efficient, scalable, and reproducible ensemble generation with robust HPC integration and quality-of-life features.

Conclusion: hmcdj significantly lowers the barrier to large-scale lattice QCD simulations and is openly available (GPLv2) for community use and extension.

Abstract: We present hmcdj, a new tool developed to ease the process of generating large numbers of gauge ensembles for a given theory. Building on top of the Grid library, hmcdj allows quick specification of lattice gauge theories in C++ with minimal boilerplate, and precise specification of per-ensemble parameters to be controlled at run time from a YAML input file. Additionally, it supports many quality-of-life features for managing large numbers of running jobs, including automatic tuning of the molecular dynamics step size to control the Metropolis acceptance rate; output of ILDG-compliant data; and integration with HPC schedulers to dynamically adapt the target trajectory count of a job to match its time limits. hmcdj is openly available under the GPLv2 license, and welcomes new contributions.

</details>

Nedkova,Zoran Ninkov,Ken Osato,Casey Papovich,Jack Piotrowski,Lucia Pozzetti,Alvise Raccanelli,Jason Rhodes,Shun Saito,Rebecca Schindhelm,Hee-Jong Seo,Zachary Slepian,Steve Smee,Francesco Spezzati,Lee Spitler,Scott Streetman*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: SIRMOS 是一项拟议的 NASA 天体物理探索任务，旨在通过前所未有的广度和细节测绘星系大尺度结构，以探究宇宙起源与演化，核心目标包括揭示暴胀如何播下大尺度结构种子、理解宇宙加速膨胀本质、高精度测量中微子质量总和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为深入理解宇宙起源与演化，特别是暴胀机制、暗能量性质及中微子质量，亟需对高红移星系进行大规模、高精度光谱巡天。

Method: SIRMOS 将开展为期3年的宇宙学巡天，覆盖欧几里得（Euclid）项目约14000平方度天区，利用空间望远镜搭载数字微镜器件（DMD），对红移1<z<4的约1亿个星系获取分辨率为R=1300、波长覆盖1.25–2.5 μm的连续 slit 光谱。

Result: 实现迄今最广域、最深的高红移星系光谱巡天，显著降低系统误差，有望在宇宙暴胀、暗能量和中微子质量等前沿问题上取得突破性成果。

Conclusion: SIRMOS 作为首个专为协同 Euclid 近红外成像而设计的空间光谱任务，将极大推动精确宇宙学与星系演化研究，具备极高的科学价值与可行性。

Abstract: SIRMOS is a mission concept for a future NASA Astrophysics Explorer opportunity that aims to revolutionize our understanding of the universe by mapping the large-scale structure of galaxies in unprecedented breadth and detail to explore cosmic origins and evolution. Specifically, the primary science goals of SIRMOS are to discover how cosmic inflation seeded large-scale structure, reveal the nature of the accelerating expansion of the universe, and measure the sum of neutrino masses to exceptionally high precision. Its secondary science goals include determining how environment shapes galaxy evolution at cosmic noon and beyond, and probing the Milky Way's dust-enshrouded regions, reaching the far side of our Galaxy.
  To accomplish its transformative science goals, SIRMOS executes a 3-year cosmology survey over the Euclid footprint of ~14,000 deg$^2$, obtaining slit spectra for ~100 million galaxies at 1<z<4. SIRMOS is the first space mission designed specifically to leverage Euclid's wide near-infrared (IR) imaging survey for target selection and employs a digital micro-mirror device (DMD) to acquire up to 4,000 simultaneous spectra over a wide 0.9 deg$^2$ field of view. The instrument's excellent spectral resolution of $R=1300$ and continuous wavelength coverage from 1.25--2.5 $μ$m capture the redshifts of distant galaxies with no gaps, reducing systematic biases to achieve ground-breaking science.

</details>


### [4] [A High Precision Metacalibration Response Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35998)
*Erin S. Sheldon*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为'fusion correction'的新方法，用于校正弱引力透镜测量中Metacalibration技术因人工剪切引入的自旋-2噪声调制偏差，在保持无偏响应的同时显著降低额外噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Metacalibration中的人工剪切操作会引入自旋-2噪声功率调制，导致剪切响应测量偏差，而传统修正方法虽能消除偏差但使噪声方差翻倍（噪声增加√2倍），亟需更优的低噪声修正方案。

Method: fusion correction方法通过将脉冲信号输入Metacal流程来测量噪声功率传递特性，识别并量化各类Metacal图像中自旋-2（m=2）与自旋-6（m=6）调制的方位角共同缺失量，并仅将该缺失量经滤波后的旋转噪声场加入原始图像。

Result: fusion correction在圆形点扩散函数（PSF）下仅使像素级噪声增加约2%，远低于传统方法的√2（≈41%）；即使对高度椭圆PSF，其噪声增幅也仅渐近趋近√2。

Conclusion: fusion correction在保证Metacalibration剪切响应无偏性的同时，大幅降低了附加噪声，尤其适用于PSF较圆的观测数据，提升了弱引力透镜测量的信噪比和精度。

Abstract: In Metacalibration the shear response of a weak lensing measurement is estimated by applying an artificial shear to the image, but the shear operation imprints a spin-2 modulation on the image noise power, which biases the measured response. The standard fix adds a sheared noise field that symmetrizes the anisotropy, at the cost of doubling the noise variance ($\sqrt2$ in the noise). The fusion correction presented in this note reaches the same unbiased response while adding only the minimum power needed. It measures the noise power transfer by passing an impulse through the Metacal process, forms the common-level azimuthal deficit that equalizes the spin-2 ($m{=}2$) and spin-6 ($m{=}6$) modulations across the metacal types, and adds just that deficit by filtering the rotated noise field. The fusion correction raises the per-pixel noise by only $\sim2\%$ for a round PSF (versus $\sqrt2$ for the standard correction), rising toward the $\sqrt2$ level only for highly elliptical PSFs.

</details>


### [5] [ASTRA: An Autonomous Framework for Physics-Validated Analysis and Hypothesis Generation in Astrophysics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36144)
*Glenn J. White*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: ASTRA是一个完全自动化的天体物理分析框架，无需人工干预，整合了量纲分析、结构因果模型、贝叶斯证据计算和多波段数据融合，成功复现多个已知天文规律，并在物理有效性验证上显著优于LLM和纯机器学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决天体物理学中依赖人工干预、缺乏物理一致性验证的自动化分析瓶颈，实现可解释、可验证、端到端自主的科学发现流程。

Method: 构建ASTRA框架，集成量纲分析、结构因果模型（SCM）、贝叶斯证据计算与多波段数据融合；采用知识隔离消融实验评估领域知识作用；对比测试agentic-LLM与纯ML基线在物理验证阶段的表现。

Result: 在5个真实天文数据用例中复现已知结果；第6个合成数据用例无监督恢复主序关系与质量-金属丰度关系；异常检测器在10个密封数据集上纯度>0.99，且在3000星系样本中零误报；相比基线方法，ASTRA唯一稳定通过物理验证（如平衡基准判定），而基线常采纳错误基准；在SDSS DR16实测数据中识别出197个极端异常星系。

Conclusion: ASTRA证明了全自动化、物理约束驱动的天体物理分析可行且鲁棒；领域知识非模式识别所必需，仅影响解释层面；其核心价值在于保障假设生成的物理合理性，而非单纯统计拟合。

Abstract: We present ASTRA, an autonomous framework for physics-validated analysis and hypothesis generation in astrophysics without per-step human intervention. It integrates dimensional analysis, structural causal models, Bayesian evidence computation, and multi-wavelength data fusion in a single automated workflow. Five test cases on real observational data recover known results: Herschel filament scaling, Chandra Deep Field South cross-matching, SDSS galaxy correlations, Gaia causal inference, and Bayesian virial model selection. A sixth case runs unsupervised on synthetic galaxy data. The main sequence and mass-metallicity relation are recovered, and the outlier detector, with a false-discovery-rate threshold and no contamination parameter, recovers the blind injected fraction on ten sealed datasets (purity > 0.99) and flags nothing on the 3,000-galaxy sample. A knowledge-isolation ablation shows pattern detection and physical validation are invariant to removal of domain knowledge, which affects only interpretation. In a direct comparison, agentic-LLM and pure-ML baselines match the statistical fitting but not the physics-validation stage. They rarely derive the equilibrium benchmark, split evenly on the physical verdict, and adopt a supplied erroneous benchmark in all six trap runs, an astrophysics-specific fine-tuned model completes zero of six runs. On live SDSS DR16 data it flags 197 of 4,999 galaxies at a data-determined threshold, ranked extremes classifiable from withheld spectroscopy. These are recovery validations and capability demonstrations rather than discoveries, with hypothesis generation demonstrated qualitatively.

</details>


### [6] [Propulsion Systems for a Starshade Rendezvous Mission with the Habitable World Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36200)
*Mario Damiano,Rhonda Morgan,Michael Turmon,Dmitry Savransky,Armen Tokadjian*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文评估了推进系统和运行约束对太阳系外类地行星表征任务的影响，重点分析了不同推进器（AEPS Hall、NEXT离子、Starlink级Hall）在星冕仪伴飞任务中的性能表现，并探讨了燃料容量、轨道重定位策略及太阳规避角等关键参数对观测产出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持宜居世界天文台（HWO）类望远镜在日-地L2点开展系外行星表征，需明确星冕仪任务中推进系统与运行约束对科学产出的限制作用。

Method: 采用EXOSIMS平台进行端到端蒙特卡洛仿真，建模60米（可见光波段）和35米（近紫外波段）两种星冕仪，并对比三种电推进器性能；分析初始推进剂质量、加速/减速时间分配、在轨加注及太阳规避角等参数的影响。

Result: NEXT离子推进器在无加注条件下提供最高表征产出（比AEPS高9–13%，比Starlink级高13–16%）；有加注时各推进器产出趋近约120次/10年；35米星冕仪产出主要受任务时长与几何可达性限制；太阳规避角超过65°显著降低产出，近80°时严重恶化。

Conclusion: 研究划分了燃料受限与时间/太阳规避角受限两类任务区间，为HWO星冕仪任务的推进选型、贮箱设计、加注策略及太阳规避约束设定提供了定量依据。

Abstract: We assess how propulsion and operational constraints affect exo-Earth characterization with a starshade rendezvous mission for a Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO)-like telescope at Sun-Earth L2. Using end-to-end Monte Carlo simulations with EXOSIMS, we model a 60 m starshade for visible-band spectroscopy (500-1000 nm) and a 35 m starshade for near-ultraviolet characterization (250-550 nm). For the 60 m design, we compare AEPS Hall, NEXT ion, and Starlink-class Hall thrusters and examine sensitivity to propellant capacity, the fraction of each retargeting slew spent accelerating and decelerating, and on-orbit refueling. With 1000-3000 kg of initial propellant and no refueling, NEXT provides the highest characterization yield: 9-13% more characterizations than AEPS and 13-16% more than the Starlink-class option. Its higher specific impulse enables more retargeting slews per kilogram of propellant. With refueling, yields converge across thruster choices to about 120 cumulative characterizations over 10 years, while propulsion choice mainly affects the number of refueling events. For the 35 m starshade with AEPS, yields are less sensitive to fuel and are dominated by mission duration and geometric accessibility. Increasing the minimum solar keepout angle from 40 to 50 degrees has little effect on yield; increases from 50 to 65 degrees have a modest effect, while angles above 65 degrees rapidly reduce yield, with severe degradation near 80 degrees. These results distinguish fuel-limited and time- or keepout-limited regimes and guide propulsion selection, tank sizing, refueling policy, and keepout constraints for HWO starshade missions.

</details>


### [7] [The Great Chat in the Sky: Aligning Exotic Astronomical Data to Language with AstroBridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36375)
*Ibrahim Habib,Kevin Li,Trung Nguyen,Gabriel A. Patron,Gaurav Sekar,Nolan Koblischke,Matthieu Le Lain,Shashwat Sourav,Aman Kumar,Kartheik G. Iyer,Joshua S. Speagle,Itai Sfaradi,Michael J. Smith,Ioana Ciucă*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: AstroBridge 是一个将天文观测数据（图像、光谱、光变曲线）与大语言模型（LLM）连接的框架，通过文献辅助生成描述性字幕，无需人工标注；它利用冻结的模态编码器和可训练的融合网络对齐多源表征与LLM嵌入空间，在光谱分类任务中显著提升准确率（91.5% vs 69.0%），但仍受限于星系形态识别、稀有超新星分类及生成字幕的事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合天文观测数据（图像、光谱、光变曲线）与自然语言描述的科学文献之间的鸿沟，实现多模态天文数据与大语言模型的有效对齐。

Method: 构建 AstroBridge 框架：利用星表元数据匹配观测与相关科学文本；用视觉-语言模型基于观测表征、匹配段落和元数据自动生成观察-字幕对；训练模型直接从原始观测预测字幕；采用模态专用冻结编码器提取表征，并通过共享可训练融合网络将其对齐到 LLM 的嵌入空间。

Result: 在基于字幕的光谱分类评估中，AstroBridge 对类星体与星系的区分准确率达 91.5%，显著高于微调前基线模型的 69.0%；但在星系形态识别、少数超新星子类分类及生成字幕的事实性方面仍存在局限。

Conclusion: AstroBridge 为统一处理天文学中多样且奇特的多模态数据提供了可行路径，是迈向以文本为通用接口全面对齐天文数据的重要一步。

Abstract: Astronomical observations include images, spectra, and light curves, while the scientific literature describes these objects in natural language. We present AstroBridge, a framework that connects these observations to an LLM through a literature-assisted caption generation pipeline. We used catalog metadata to match observations with scientific texts that describe the same objects. A vision-language model generates descriptive captions from representations of observations, matched passages, and available metadata, creating observation-caption pairs without manual captioning. We train AstroBridge to predict captions directly from raw observations. Modality-specific frozen encoders extract representations that a shared, trainable fusion network aligns with the embedding space of an LLM. In caption-based evaluation of spectra, AstroBridge achieves 91.5% accuracy in distinguishing quasars from galaxies, compared to 69.0% from the base model prior to fine-tuning. Limitations remain in galaxy morphology, classification of minority supernova classes, and the factual quality of generated captions. We believe that AstroBridge's framework is a step towards the goal of aligning the totality of astronomy's diverse, exotic data through the lingua franca of text.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对新型低成本光学巡天设备ILMT的认知，验证其天体测量与测光性能，并推动公众科普教育。

Method: 基于ILMT自2022年4月首光以来的实际观测数据，开展系统性的天体测量与测光性能分析；同时提出构建缩比模型的技术指导方案。

Conclusion: ILMT作为专用巡天望远镜已进入常规运行阶段，性能达标，具备重要科学价值；配套科普模型方案有助于扩大其社会影响力。

Abstract: The International Liquid Mirror Telescope (ILMT) is an inexpensive optical instrument with a diameter of 4 metres, entirely dedicated to astronomical surveys. Its scientific programmes include the detection and study of transients, variable objects, asteroids, comets, low surface brightness galaxies, space debris, etc. The telescope achieved its first light on 29 April 2022 and was officially inaugurated on 21 March 2023. It has been operating regularly since October 2023 and continuously observes the sky passing through the zenith using the SDSS $g^{\prime}$, $r^{\prime}$, and $i^{\prime}$ filters in a narrow band of the sky ($22.3^{\prime}$) centred around the equatorial declination of $+29°21^{\prime}41^{\prime \prime}$. Here we report on the astrometric and photometric performance of the ILMT and summarise the main results obtained since the BINA3 workshop. A great way to raise public awareness is to build a scale model of a liquid mirror telescope. General guidelines for doing so are provided.

</details>

Motivation: 为快速射电暴（FRB）数据分析提供统一、交互式、可复现且易用的开源工具，解决现有流程分散、缺乏标准化及可追溯性的问题。

Method: 开发FLITS软件包，集成数据读取（SIGPROC/PSRFITS/CHIME HDF5）、可视化检查、自动/半自动干扰剔除、多准则DM评分（信噪比、结构特征、平滑结构度量）、fitburst模型拟合、会话持久化（含校准信息与软件溯源）及命令行批量处理能力。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are brief flashes of radio emission whose time, frequency, and polarization structure constrain their sources and propagation environments. FLITS (Fast-Look Interactive Transient Suite) is an open-source Python package with a browser interface for analysing detected bursts. It combines data inspection, interference mitigation, measurement, and modelling, including fits of the fitburst burst model, in a shared session, and reads SIGPROC filterbank, PSRFITS, and CHIME/FRB HDF5 data. The same routines measure larger samples from the command line and flag bursts whose automatic selections need checking. Trial dispersion measures can be scored by signal-to-noise, by burst structure, or with a smoothed-structure metric that also reports the range of dispersion measures consistent with the best one. Portable session files retain the selected data regions, excluded channels, calibration inputs, available results, input-data checksum, and software provenance, and supported measurements can be replayed from the command line and compared with their saved values. Uncertainty labels identify missing calibration information. A public reference workflow provides downloadable data and numerical checks.

</details>

Abstract: Satellite emissions at low frequencies can interfere with astronomical observations. Observations with the EDA2 have revealed recurrent Starlink emissions near 137~MHz, attributed to very high frequency (VHF) telemetry, tracking, and command (TT\&C) transmissions. We aim to characterise Starlink VHF emissions detected with the 21 CentiMeter Array (21CMA) to better assess their potential impact on future low-frequency radio astronomical observations and inform the development of methods to mitigate the resulting interference. We searched five coordinated VHF channels in observations obtained on 15 April 2025 (UTC) and demodulated and decoded candidate signals. Two valid packets are detected at 137.055~MHz, each lasting 1.027584~s and separated by 99.987~s. A blind comparison with 11\,305 active satellites identifies STARLINK-32838 (NORAD ID 62827) as the transmitter, with differential Doppler measurements linking both events to the same spacecraft. Orbital analysis shows that the satellite was undergoing orbit raising and that the limited number of detections was partly attributable to its being below the horizon for most of the observing time. Re-analysis of EDA2 detections indicates that such emissions are not confined to orbit raising. The stronger event's estimated flux density is 9.23~MJy at 1000~km, consistent with previous measurements at the order-of-magnitude level. Signal demodulation and decoding, combined with orbital analysis, can improve the identification and characterisation of satellite-related radio frequency interference. The detection of similar VHF emissions across different orbital phases indicates that their potential interference with low-frequency radio astronomy should not be considered solely in the context of orbit raising.

Abstract: We present a deep low-frequency radio point-source catalogue centred on the North Celestial Pole (NCP) and extending $5^\circ$ in radius, derived from a single 24-h observation with the 21 CentiMeter Array (21CMA). Covering 75-175 MHz, the catalogue characterises extragalactic foreground point sources for Epoch of Reionization (EoR) studies. Previous 21CMA imaging has been limited by reduced dynamic range caused by direction-dependent effects, particularly the severe grating lobes of ultra-bright sources such as 3C 61.1 and ionospheric phase distortions. Here we apply 21CMAcali, a direction-dependent calibration and imaging pipeline, to mitigate these effects. The angular resolution ranges from 2.3 arcmin at 100 MHz to 1.4 arcmin at 168.75 MHz. The resulting maps show reduced artefacts and background noise, enabling more reliable recovery of faint sources. We construct a catalogue of 1246 radio point sources. Cross-matching with LoTSS-DR3, WENSS, and NVSS enables broad-band spectral fitting, while an independent in-band analysis yields a median spectral index of $α=-0.825$ (where $S \propto ν^α$). The Euclidean-normalised differential source counts at 150 MHz, derived from a dedicated 50-MHz-wide multi-frequency synthesis (MFS) image, are broadly consistent with existing measurements and semi-empirical models at the bright end, whereas the deficit at lower flux densities ($S \lesssim 0.04$ Jy) mainly reflects limited sensitivity and incompleteness. This catalogue improves foreground point-source characterisation in the 21CMA NCP field, demonstrates the value of direction-dependent calibration, and provides a reference for deeper catalogues based on multi-day observations.

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型高效算法，用于降低合成跟踪（synthetic tracking）的计算复杂度与内存占用，使其可在普通工作站上实时运行，而无需GPU服务器或CPU集群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合成跟踪方法在处理大尺寸图像和大量短曝光帧时，面临计算量大（O(N_x N_y N_f^3)）和内存需求高（O(N_x N_y N_f^2)）的问题，难以实用化。

Method: 提出一种基于二进制分解与分层累加的近似对齐算法：将帧间位移表示为帧索引二进制位的线性组合，逐位向上取整后，通过共享中间和实现多速度并行积分，内存降至O(N_x N_y N_f)，计算效率接近动态规划。

Result: 显著降低计算资源需求，在仅引入对数级微小跟踪误差的前提下，使合成跟踪可在标准工作站高效运行，能量效率大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该算法在精度、速度与内存之间取得良好平衡，推动合成跟踪从理论方法走向实际天文观测部署。

Abstract: Detecting unknown faint moving objects using optical imaging is challenging. Long exposure times are infeasible for such targets, as the resulting image streaking causes a loss of signal-to-noise ratio due to energy dispersal across multiple pixels. To address this, synthetic tracking, a track-before-detect method, replaces long exposures with many short exposures, integrating them in post-processing to recover the moving signal. Enabled by modern CMOS detectors with fast readout and low read noise, this technique synthesizes telescope tracking by searching over a grid of velocities. However, the conventional brute-force implementation imposes a significant computational burden: integrating N_f frames of size (N_x,N_y) requires O(N_x N_y N_f^3) operations. This cost becomes prohibitive for megapixel-scale frames with N_f~100. Although dynamic programming can reduce the computational complexity by a factor of N_f, it requires a prohibitive memory of O(N_x N_y N_f^2), unsuitable for large-format detectors. This article presents a novel algorithm to achieve the computational efficiency of dynamic programming while maintaining a memory footprint of just O(N_x N_y N_f) by slightly approximating the computation of frame shifts for alignment. By expressing the shift as a sum of terms proportional to the digits frame index in binary and rounding up each of these terms, frames can be integrated efficiently by hierarchically adding intermediate sums that are shared across different velocities with the same higher bits in binary. The approximation only introduces a small tracking error that increases logarithmically with the number of frames. This advancement enables synthetic tracking to be executed with high energy efficiency, making it practical for deployment on standard workstations rather than requiring high-performance computing infrastructure such as GPU servers or CPU clusters.

</details>

Abstract: We compute torus partition functions of orbifolds of two-dimensional chiral fermions by huge non-Abelian groups involving the Conway group, one of the sporadic finite simple groups. We describe in detail how they can be overcome.
  Although the methods described in this paper are generally applicable, the choice of the particular orbifolds we consider in this paper was motivated by the search for the periodicity element of topological modular forms. We will provide a brief introduction to this question, to explain why all the techniques in this paper had to be developed. Unfortunately, the orbifolds we considered turned out not to be the periodicity element.

Abstract: The high order behavior of perturbation theory provides insight into non-perturbative phenomenon in quantum field theory. While extensively studied in simplified models, high order behavior is challenging to access in more realistic field theories due to the complexity of the standard Feynman diagrammatic expansion. Building on recent progress in global sampling on the moduli space of tropical curves, we introduce an annealed importance sampling framework for tropical field theory. Our approach overcomes the exponential weight-variance barrier, allowing precise numerical calculations to high orders. We apply our technique to compute the perturbative series for the two-point function in the massive three-dimensional $φ^3$ theory through 325 loops. These coefficients determine the instanton action in $φ^3$ theory to 0.03\% precision, confirm the collective-coordinate exponent, and extract the one-loop determinant and the instanton fluctuation corrections through four loops, in remarkable agreement with direct semiclassical calculations. Our approach is applicable to a variety of related field theories, facilitating the quantitative exploration of their high loop behavior.

Compared with the corrections the tower makes to low-energy amplitudes, which are dominated by the lightest levels and captured by local operators, the mutual information with the tower is not dominated by any finite set of levels and arises from the time-nonlocal part of the effective action. Abstract: It has been proposed that consistency limits how slowly black holes can ring down in extensions of General Relativity: effective-field-theory (EFT) corrections may lengthen the decay time of a quasinormal mode by at most about one oscillation period, $\Re[ω_{\rm GR}]\,δτ\lesssim1$. We argue that, under standard power counting, this condition yields no new, model-independent constraint on gravitational EFTs. First, its motivation does not extend straightforwardly to black holes: the inverse oscillation frequency is not a universal, detector-independent uncertainty in the damping time of a classical waveform. Our main argument, however, rests on eikonal power counting alone. The decay-time shift grows with the angular momentum $\ell$ of the mode, but so does the weight of higher-derivative operators. When curvature and derivatives are controlled by a single scale $Λ$, as in every known ultraviolet completion, the EFT around a black hole of radius $r_s$ remains predictive up to $\ell\sim(r_sΛ)^2$, and the shift becomes of order one only there, where an infinite tower of operators contributes at the same order. A resolvable shift within the predictive regime requires either large numerical coefficients or a two-scale power counting, which has no known ultraviolet completion. In both cases the quartic-curvature operators dominate, and the condition coincides parametrically with the positivity bounds that flat-space graviton amplitudes impose under standard assumptions on the ultraviolet completion. Abstract: The study of galaxies outside the Milky Way using observations of resolved stellar populations has experienced extraordinary growth over the past two decades. There now exist panchromatic photometry and precise astrometry for hundreds of millions of individually resolved stars in hundreds of galaxies that span $\sim10^9$ in luminosity, $\sim10^3$ in metallicity, and sample a wide range of environments. Here, we review advances in extragalactic astronomy enabled by resolved stellar population imaging. We summarize major observational programs and focus on four interconnected science areas: the star formation histories of galaxies, 'bursty' star formation, the stellar initial mass function, and the orbital histories of Local Group galaxies. We review the literature in each area, synthesize key findings, identify emerging trends, and discuss how new and future facilities (e.g., JWST, LSST, Euclid, Roman, CSST, ELTs, HWO) will enable further progress. Finally, we highlight shortcomings in current datasets and discuss promising directions for new areas of investigation.

Abstract: Characterising the assembly history of the inner Milky Way is a key goal of upcoming spectroscopic surveys, yet disentangling its overlapping stellar components remains challenging. We classify stars within the inner 4 kpc into proto-galaxy, subsequent in-situ, and accreted components to assess their distinguishability using kinematics, dynamics, ages, and metallicities. Stellar net rotation declines sharply between [Fe/H]=0 and [Fe/H]=-1, marking the transition from disc-like in-situ stars to the proto-galaxy. While significant chemical and dynamical overlaps complicate the search for structures in energy-angular momentum (E-Lz) space, metal-poor stars ([Fe/H]<-1) at low energy form a remarkably pure (>97%) sample of proto-galaxy stars. Conversely, the high-energy, metal-poor tail offers the best chance of detecting accreted debris. However, at high energies the inner accreted halo is dominated by low-mass systems (M_star ~ 10^7 - 10^9 M _sun), not the earliest massive mergers. Dynamically, few clues remain from early mergers, and early enrichment is rapid and similar across progenitors. In-situ contamination is an issue for [Fe/H]>-1 throughout E-Lz space. Overall, while accurate stellar ages and high-precision chemical abundances may help further constrain the Milky Way's history, we should be cautious when associating stars with individual merger events in the inner galaxy.

In NGC 3411, we detect extended radio emission surrounding the central AGN over scales of ~70 kpc, and resolve previously undetected arc-shaped substructures on scales of ~20-30 kpc. This extended component exhibits a relatively uniform steep spectrum ($α$ ~ -1.7). We discuss possible scenarios for its origin, including mini-halos, aged AGN plasma, weak shocks, and the possibility of a radio galaxy observed along the line of sight. In NGC 5903, MeerKAT reveals a complex filamentary morphology, including a previously unknown ring-like structure surrounding the central AGN. Together with the disturbed X-ray morphology, these results support a scenario in which fossil AGN plasma has been redistributed and locally reenergized by group-scale dynamical processes such as turbulence, shear, and weak compression. The shortest timescale we estimated is only 1.1~hours, measured between MRO 37~GHz and JVLA 33~GHz detections. This is the fastest intrinsic radio variability reported in an AGN so far. Abstract: The escape velocity is a key probe of the Milky Way (MW) potential, but standard estimates can be biased by unrelaxed substructures. The $\sim 2\%$ underestimation of the escape velocity corresponds to an $\sim 6\%$ underestimation of the total mass. This improvement persists in LAMOST-like magnitude and footprint selection. Abstract: Open clusters exhibit diverse spatial structures that provide insight into their formation and dynamical evolution. For each measure, we define a network-based mass segregation ratio (MSR) by comparing the mean centrality of the most massive stars with that of all cluster members. We test the stability of these measures across connectivity parameters $k=5$--20 and compare them with the classical 3D MSR, $Λ_{\rm MSR,3D}$, and dynamical age. All three network ratios show positive correlations with $Λ_{\rm MSR,3D}$, strongest for closeness centrality, while their correlations with dynamical age are weak. Abstract: Blue cores in brightest cluster galaxies (BCGs) trace recent central activity associated with cooling cluster atmospheres. We identify 379 blue-core BCGs among 11352 systems (3.3%), although contamination increases toward higher redshift. Of these, 98 (0.86%) are X-ray bright. Their defining signature is a central $g-r$ color at least 0.2 mag bluer than the reference population within $a=20$ kpc before returning to the normal BCG envelope. Ha emission equivalent width correlates strongly with the blue continuum excess, consistent with young stellar components comprising ~0.1-1% of pre-existing stellar mass. Line ratios indicate widespread LINER-like excitation, with a larger star-forming contribution in blue Ha emitters but substantial non-stellar excitation. This activity is closely linked to the thermodynamics of the host-cluster gas. At $0.1R_{500}$, cooling times decrease from BCGs without significant Ha emission to red and then blue Ha emitters. Blue BCGs preferentially occur in clusters with $L_{\rm X,300}>10^{44}$ erg s$^{-1}$; red Ha emitters also favor higher X-ray luminosities. The blue-core fraction rises with optical richness. For systems with $t_{\rm cool}(0.1R_{500})<20$ Gyr, doubling richness increases the odds of hosting a blue-core BCG by $2.45\pm0.39$. This indicates that cluster scale carries information beyond cooling time, plausibly through the amount of gas available for cooling and condensation. The scarcity of blue BCGs without significant Ha emission disfavors isolated short bursts followed by long Ha-quiet fading phases. It can instead be explained by sustained or closely recurrent star formation over timescales much longer than 100 Myr, or by Ha persisting through additional excitation mechanisms as the young stellar component fades.

However, these have focused on single hydrodynamical codes with a number of limitations, making it unclear how generic this result is. We compare satellite disruption in four models: two cosmological hydrodynamical simulations (Auriga and FIRE), cosmological $N$-body simulations with stellar particle tagging (Aquarius), and a semi-analytic model with varying host disk properties (SatGen). For all systems, we compute the fraction of stellar mass retained by the progenitor ($f_\text{bound}$) at the present day. We find a remarkable agreement between FIRE, Aquarius, and SatGen; all predict that $\sim$20 to 30 per cent of classical-mass satellites have $f_\text{bound} < 0.9$ ($\sim$5 to 10 per cent have $f_\text{bound} < 0.5$). In the ultra-faint regime captured by Aquarius and SatGen, disruption rates are comparable to or exceeding those for classical satellites. Abstract: Compact radio sources are occasionally observed to undergo extreme scattering events (ESEs): large, chromatic flux-density variations, often by more than a factor of two, over a few months. As such, the nature of the plasma lenses generating such events remains elusive. In this article, we present the most comprehensive sample of ESEs to date, using the ASKAP Variables and Slow Transients (VAST) survey. We show that the light curves demonstrate varied morphologies, which can be interpreted in terms of the source size and the strength of the lens in the paradigm of a simple one-dimensional Gaussian lens model. Abstract: Helium abundance is a key input to stellar models that are used to infer accurate ages, masses, and compositions for individually resolved stars in the Milky Way (MW), underpinning Galactic archaeology. Since spectroscopically measuring He abundances is difficult, stellar models often assume a fixed "chemical enrichment law" dY/dZ (the slope linearly relating a star's helium mass fraction Y to its metal mass fraction Z). If incorrect, this assumption can introduce significant systematics in inferring stellar properties. Using the FIRE-3 cosmological baryonic zoom-in simulations, we investigate helium versus metal enrichment in the stars of MW-mass galaxies. Given the nucleosynthetic yields in FIRE-3, overall metal production is dominated by core-collapse supernovae (CCSN) at all progenitor metallicities. He production is dominated by CCSN for progenitor metallicities of Z <= 0.1 Zsun, but by stellar winds for progenitors near Zsun. Our simulations produce a range of dY/dZ slopes slopes with best-fit values of ~0.75-2.1 depending on the age of the stars considered at present day; on average consistent with the range of values from MW observations but with no single "universal" enrichment law. The dY/dZ slope also shows galaxy-to-galaxy variance in our simulations, and to leading order increases over cosmic time within the same galaxy, based on the peculiarities of the formation histories of each MW-mass system. Despite choosing a fixed set of nucleosynthetic yields for stellar feedback processes in FIRE-3, we predict variance in dY/dZ for stars in MW-mass galaxies, and observations of MW stars must be interpreted in this context.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移星系中质量-金属丰度关系（MZR）如何受恒星形成活动和形态结构影响，揭示其背后物理机制（如气体循环、吸积与反馈）。

At log(M*/M)<9.5, we find that larger galaxies, and those having lower SFR surface densities, bulges, and/or spiral features are significantly metal rich relative to their smaller, higher SFR surface density, bulge-less, and/or non-spiral counterparts. Abstract: Dwarf galaxies are dark matter-dominated systems that are sensitive to feedback and display bursty star formation histories. This makes them ideal probes of the processes that regulate star formation. This work investigates the timescales of star formation rate (SFR) variability in isolated dwarf galaxies using high-resolution cosmological zoom-in simulations of $50$ dwarf galaxies drawn from the Marvelous Massive Dwarfs and Extended Marvelous Dwarfs simulation suites ($7.3 <$ \lmstar $< 9.1$). We measure star formation histories in $10$ Myr bins and characterize their variability with power spectral densities (PSDs). At the population level, the PSDs of both disk and non-disk galaxies follow a broken power-law, with a break timescale near $100$ Myr. Individually, our galaxies show both smooth and bursty star formation. A subset of disk galaxies show quasi-periodic SFR oscillations about their mean, which we refer to as cyclic star formation. The dominant timescale of these oscillations scales linearly with half-light radius and is approximated by the orbital period at that radius. Cyclic galaxies have Magellanic Spiral (Sm) morphologies and host lopsided dipole gas disks. In these systems, the bulk of the gas in the lopsided disk moves between its maximum and minimum distance from the galactic center, compressing the star-forming gas once per radial orbit and driving the SFR cycle. Cyclic star formation adds a narrow Lorentzian component to the PSD, peaked at radial orbital frequency of the disk ($\sim 200-500$ Myr), which is comparable to burst timescales inferred from observations.

Abstract: JWST surveys find more UV-bright galaxies at $z>10$ than galaxy-formation models predict, but the roles of recent star formation, stellar mass and dust remain uncertain. We analyse 1365 spectroscopically confirmed JADES galaxies at $3\leq z\leq9$, fitting NIRCam photometry and NIRSpec emission lines with \ceri. Our model includes flexible star-formation histories (SFHs) under three priors, two-component dust attenuation and nebular emission. We account for spectroscopic selection through population reweighting and assess the reliability of inferred trends. At $z=3$--5, rising SFHs are more common in low-mass galaxies, whereas most galaxies above $10^{9.5}\,{\rm M}_{\odot}$ have declining SFHs. The apparent increase in rising fractions with redshift cannot be distinguished from the adopted SFH prior alone. At fixed stellar mass, recent star formation is the strongest predictor of UV brightness. UV-brighter galaxies have higher recent SFRs, younger stellar populations and less attenuation at fixed SFR. Below $10^9\,{\rm M}_{\odot}$, they are also more likely to have rising SFHs, with odds increasing by up to a factor of 2.3 per magnitude brighter, although the strongest trend requires caution. In contrast, galaxies with $M_{\mathrm{UV}}<-20$ at $z=3$--5 are predominantly massive, and approximately half have declining SFHs. Dust partly offsets burst-driven UV brightening: attenuation increases with recent SFR and declines gradually after the inferred SFR peak. In model sensitivity tests, dust has the largest effect on bright-end counts, whereas removing galaxy-to-galaxy SFH scatter changes them by at most 0.17~dex. Thus, bursts can make low-mass galaxies unusually UV-bright for their mass, while the absolute bright end of the UVLF is primarily shaped by stellar mass and dust attenuation.

TL;DR: 本文通过结合法拉第旋转测量（RM）、快速射电暴色散量（FRB DM）和运动学SZ效应（kSZ），对星系群和星系团中的磁场强度和等离子体β参数设定了新的上限和下限，并预测未来Deep Synoptic Array（DSA）观测将实现首次直接探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场在大质量晕中对重子物理状态起关键作用，但难以直接观测，影响星系形成理论及tSZ功率谱振幅的宇宙学推断。

Conclusion: 当前结果为限制性结论，但未来DSA和SKA观测有望通过偏振分数剖面和RM结构函数解决湍流消偏振与相干长度不确定等问题，实现磁场的精确测量。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are vital in determining the physical conditions of baryons in massive halos. They are, however, notoriously difficult to observe. This impacts problems from our understanding of galaxy formation, to the observed deficit in the amplitude of thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (tSZ) power spectrum that affects cosmological inferences. Although Faraday rotation measures (RMs) offer a direct probe of magnetic fields, it weights magnetic fields by the free-electron density. Several probes of free electrons, including fast radio burst (FRB) dispersion measures (DMs) and kinematic SZ (kSZ) effect, are now maturing into precision tools. We combine RM$^2$ stacks from SPICE-RACS around DESI galaxy groups and ROSAT clusters with optical depth profiles constrained jointly by the FRB DM-redshift relation and the kSZ effect, and obtain 95% upper limits on the rms line-of-sight magnetic field of $<3.9$-$11.6~μ$G ($<2.3$-$6.4~μ$G) in groups (clusters) for a 10~kpc coherence length. Combining these with thermal pressure profiles inferred from tSZ-shear cross-correlations, we obtain 95% lower limits on the plasma $β$ of $>0.31$ ($>11$) in groups (clusters). Although current constraints are only limits, we forecast a first detection with larger datasets from the Deep Synoptic Array (DSA). We present the first forecast of the DSA RM grid density ($\sim365$ RMs deg$^{-2}$), which when combined with 10$^4$ FRBs would measure the field in groups to $3$-$13\%$ precision, compared to current constraints of $38$-$75\%$. Although the interpretation of current measurements remains challenging owing to depolarization along turbulent halo sightlines and the unknown coherence length, larger samples and DSA/Square Kilometer Array observations may resolve these uncertainties by measuring polarization-fraction profiles and the RM structure function using extended radio sources, respectively.

</details>

Conclusion: M81的恒星形成主要受长期、稳定的内禀过程主导，近期潮汐扰动影响微弱；其盘面不对称性揭示了外部相互作用的遗留印记，但未显著扰动整体SFH；该工作凸显了多波段、空间分辨恒星考古对理解星系演化的重要性。

Abstract: The nearby M81 galaxy---a tidally interacting $L^*$ spiral disk---offers a unique perspective on the interplay of secular and external processes in shaping galaxy properties. We present the most detailed, panoramic map of M81's stellar populations to date, using archival Hubble Space Telescope ACS photometry from the ANGST survey spanning the full optical extent of its disk. From color-magnitude diagram fitting, we recover spatially-resolved star formation histories (SFHs) over the last $\sim180\,$Myr. M81's global SFH is remarkably steady in the last $\sim180\,$Myr with slight enhancements seen $\lesssim15\,$Myr ago. The average SFR surface density rises toward the center before falling off within the innermost $\sim4\,$kpc. The disk is also notably lopsided, with the eastern half having formed $\sim50\%$ more stars than the western half over the same area---a trend missed by FUV-based SFR indicators due to dust systematics. Finally, the long-term SFHs of both M81 and M82 point to a pericenter passage $\sim1\,$Gyr ago. By identifying M81-M82 analogs in the TNG50 cosmological simulation, we rule out the more recent pericenter times of $150-220\,$Myr predicted by some works, as such passages are inconsistent with M81's undisturbed SFH over the last $\sim180\,$Myr. With this work, M81 becomes only the third $L^*$ spiral---after M31 and NGC 6946---to have its SFH spatially resolved across large areas of its disk.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦理论紧致化可能产生轻标量场（如稀释子或模场），其运动改变基本常数，进而影响等效原理（WEP）和时钟速率；需建立从高能模型到低能可观测量的定量联系。

Result: WEP单独拟合得D_m̂=(-6.06±34.91)×10⁻¹²、D_e=(8.80±46.46)×10⁻¹²（强负相关）；加入α-漂移约束后，当|ζ|=2.37×10⁻⁸ yr⁻¹时，WEP与钟漂移敏感度匹配；跑动稀释子估计η~10⁻¹²，显著高于MICROSCOPE当前精度。

Conclusion: 该分析为弦论紧致化模型提供了可检验的低能预言框架：只要模型给出斜率比r=D_m̂/D_e、标量振幅及ζ，即可与当前和未来WEP/钟实验直接比对；MICROSCOPE级新实验（如Be-Ti重复）可大幅提升约束能力。

Abstract: String compactifications and runaway-dilaton models can contain light scalar directions. Motion along these directions changes gauge couplings, the QCD scale, quark masses, and Yukawa parameters. A given string-derived sector then predicts low-energy slopes. Its gauge kinetic functions, matter-metric or Yukawa slopes, source charge, scalar trajectory, and scalar range determine a Damour--Donoghue weak-equivalence-principle (WEP) vector and a clock-drift vector. The fit assumes one long-range scalar or an aligned multi-field direction. With that assumption, WEP tests and secular clock drifts probe related projections of the same scalar-slope vector. The minimal two-charge Damour--Donoghue fit uses Ti--Pt MICROSCOPE, Be--Ti torsion-balance, and $^{85}$Rb--$^{87}$Rb atom-interferometer data, then adds the Yb$^+$ E3/E2 secular $α$-drift row. The WEP-only fit gives $D_{\hat m}=(-6.06\pm34.91)\times10^{-12}$ and $D_e=(8.80\pm46.46)\times10^{-12}$, with correlation $-0.99958$. The clock input is $\dotα/α=1.0(1.1)\times10^{-18}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$. It matches the WEP marginal reach when $|ζ|=2.37\times10^{-8}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$, with $ζ=\dot\varphi/α_E$. For the benchmark $\dot\varphi=ξH_0$ and $α_E=10^{-3}α_0$, this gives $ξ/α_0\simeq0.34$. A model becomes testable after it supplies $r=D_{\hat m}/D_e$, the scalar amplitude, and $ζ$. The Damour--Piazza--Veneziano runaway-dilaton estimate lies near $η\sim10^{-12}$ for order-unity $n=2$ parameters, well above the MICROSCOPE-level WEP scale. In the representative-isotope two-charge model, a MICROSCOPE-level Be--Ti-like row would reduce the long WEP axis by a factor $27.8$; a repeated Ti--Pt-like row would barely change it. The calculation tests compactification-level predictions for string-derived dilaton or modulus sectors after the model supplies its low-energy data.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We analytically evaluate in terms of finite sums, the following definite improper integral, which appears in the quantum field theoretical calculation involving elementary charged particles having charge $\pm e$ in presence of a constant external magnetic field $\mathbf{B}=B\hat{\mathbf{z}}$ $$ I_{abln}^{μ_1\dotsμ_r}(\mathbf{q}_\perp,|eB|) = \int\!\!\dfrac{d^2k_\perp}{(2π)^2}k_\perp^{μ_1} \dots k_\perp^{μ_r} \exp \bigg(-\frac{\mathbf{k}_\perp^2+\mathbf{p}_\perp^2}{|eB|}\bigg) L^a_l \bigg(\frac{2\mathbf{k}_\perp^2}{|eB|}\bigg) L^b_n \bigg(\frac{2\mathbf{p}_\perp^2}{|eB|}\bigg)\bigg|_{\mathbf{p}_\perp=(\mathbf{q}_\perp+\mathbf{k}_\perp)} $$ where $μ_i \in \{1,2\}$, $l\ge 0$, $n\ge 0$ and $L_l^a(x)$ are the generalized Laguerre polynomials. We also provide explicit expressions of the result up to the fourth moment i.e. $r=4$, which are sufficient for most of the field theoretical calculations.

Abstract: We study how magnetic fields affect the onset of quark saturation, which limits the domain of applicability of a purely baryonic description. From the baryon wave functions, we construct the one-body reduced density matrix and derive the corresponding single-quark distributions in longitudinal momentum and Landau-level space. We then use these distributions to evaluate quark occupation probabilities in an ideal baryon gas and determine the onset of quark saturation. The resulting saturation density depends nonmonotonically on the magnetic-field strength: it decreases at weak fields and increases at stronger fields. This behavior reflects the competition between spin-flavor polarization, which promotes saturation, and transverse spatial squeezing, which delays saturation by broadening of the quark transverse momentum distribution.

Applying the exact differential recoil spectra within an effective propagation model for cosmic-ray electron boosted dark matter (CRE-BDM), we derive $90\%$ C.L. exclusion bounds in the 1 keV -- 10 MeV mass range from Super-Kamiokande, XENONnT, and PandaX-4T, extending experimental sensitivity into the sub-MeV window.

Abstract: Recent years have seen a surge of interest in adapting non-classical states to search for physics beyond the Standard Model, including both qubit- and boson-based systems. These two classes of systems are closely related through the Holstein--Primakoff transformation (HPT). In this work, we show that the HPT offers novel insights into inherently quantum-mechanical detection of new physics. We begin by discussing how the HPT can be used to unify certain existing qubit- and boson-based proposals in a single framework. We then show how it naturally reveals a novel generalization of GHZ states which offer superior performance for the detection of rare interactions. We demonstrate that these novel states can be readily prepared, and combine the coherent advantage of GHZ states with superior error rejection. Finally, we show that the HPT also reveals a natural extension of the Hamiltonian-Not-in-Lindblad-Span theorem for collections of bosonic modes.

</details>

Abstract: Statistics is a well-established discipline, yet its application can be confusing, particularly in fields where the language of statistics is unfamiliar and when making projections, where real data is not yet available. Both of these factors are commonplace in Beyond the Standard Model physics. In this work, we clarify the distinction between goodness-of-fit tests, which assess the compatibility of an observation with a single hypothesis, and hypothesis tests, which quantify how strongly some data prefer one hypothesis over another. We emphasise that for Beyond the Standard Model sensitivity projections, hypothesis testing should be used. We give detailed examples using both Poisson and multinomially distributed data, deriving exact $p$-values and discussing Monte Carlo and asymptotic approximations. We further demonstrate that for the Asimov data set all the test statistics discussed, for both goodness-of-fit tests and hypothesis tests, can be approximated by Pearson's $χ^2$ statistic, and the difference in approaches comes down to the distribution of the test statistics. We also compare goodness-of-fit and hypothesis test $p$-values in two realistic examples. We (1) consider the reach of a muon collider on heavy spin-1 resonances and (2) provide updated projections for the potential of current and future gamma ray telescopes to probe new dark sectors via the observation of Hawking radiation from the explosion of a black hole. Finally, we discuss the CLs method, the significance and systematic errors.

</details>

Abstract: The relic abundance of light stable axion dark matter is usually set by misalignment. We study whether the inflationary fluctuations of the axion can dominate instead, and we follow the modes analytically through an instantaneous transition from de Sitter expansion to radiation domination, neglecting the axion mass during inflation and keeping it afterward. Since the energy density is quadratic in the field, a fluctuation-dominated abundance carries an isocurvature perturbation even when the mean displacement vanishes. For a canonical minimally coupled axion the spectrum is scale invariant, and fluctuation-dominated dark matter exceeds the Planck isocurvature limit by about eight orders of magnitude. An inflaton-dependent kinetic function or a nonminimal coupling to the Ricci scalar tilts the spectrum blue, moving the fluctuation power from CMB scales to small scales. Imposing the Planck limit at the pivot scale, we find that fluctuation-dominated dark matter with a constant kinetic slope requires the spectral tilt $n_η-1\gtrsim0.27$ and the axion mass $m_η \gtrsim200 \rm{GeV}$ at $H_I=10^{10}\rm{GeV}$, and $n_η-1\gtrsim0.34$ and $m_η\gtrsim4.5\,$keV at $H_I=10^{13}\rm{GeV}$. A kinetic function with a running slope lowers these masses to $1.4~\rm{GeV}$ and $0.7\,$eV, respectively. For the QCD axion, whose oscillations start late, we estimate that any single power-law spectrum blue enough to satisfy the pivot limit gives too little fluctuation abundance, and misalignment dominates.

Abstract: We study the training dynamics of a feed-forward neural network (NN) through the analysis of the neural tangent kernel (NTK) in a phenomenologically relevant fit of parton distribution functions (PDFs) from deep inelastic scattering (DIS) data. The exact integration of the gradient flow derived in a previous single-flavour analysis is generalised to the multi-flavour case, yielding an analytical perspective on the relationship between data and methodology during training. The Shapley value recently used in the context of PDFs is derived within our statistical framework for observables that depend linearly on the PDFs, and we outline a geometric interpretation of training. We expose the role of gradient-based optimisation algorithms and of the parametrisation in the regularisation of the ill-posed inverse problem that affects the determination of PDFs from experimental data, demonstrating that the regularisation amounts to the incremental inclusion of NTK modes, each learned at the timescale set by the corresponding eigenvalue. We find that only a few leading modes of the NTK are necessary to reconstruct the target PDFs, while the noise of the data is carried by the sub-leading ones learned at later stages of training. Finally, we consider the impact of the initialisation on the training dynamics, and recover a transient behaviour observed in the previous analysis and in the machine learning (ML) literature. Beyond sharpening our understanding of the training dynamics, our framework can be applied to other parametrisations (such as fixed functional forms) and ML applications in physics, as well as to stress tests in the presence of inconsistent data.

</details>

Method: 将干涉仪视为开放量子系统，暗物质作为环境，利用Schwinger-Keldysh形式从微观理论推导出干涉仪的有效作用量，并投影到原子与复合粒子干涉仪相关的多体世界线扇区；区分 retarded 和 Keldysh 核以分别刻画因果响应与环境涨落。

Result: （i）在四点相互作用下，核函数对应DM气泡图；（ii）在长程相互作用下，对应被DM介质修饰的传播子；进一步发现玻色增强效应使CPI可探测<1 eV Higgs-portal DM；且轻强子亲和标量耦合下DM背景可致媒介子介质质量失稳（tachyonic）。

Conclusion: 该EFT框架统一描述了MWI对暗物质的相位与退相干响应，揭示了复合粒子干涉仪在极轻暗物质参数空间中的不可替代探测能力，尤其拓展了Higgs-portal及强子亲和模型的实验敏感度。

Abstract: Matter-wave interferometers (MWIs) are a genuine quantum probe of dark matter (DM), being sensitive to DM-induced phase shifts and loss of contrast. Treating the interferometer as an open quantum system and the dark sector as its environment, we derive an open EFT to study these signatures using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism. Starting from a microscopic theory, we derive the interferometer's effective action, which we then project onto the many-body worldline sectors relevant for atom interferometers and MWIs employing mesoscopic objects, i.e., composite-particle interferometers (CPIs). This construction clarifies the heavy-probe limit and organizes phase shifts and decoherence as distinct real-time components of the influence action: the retarded kernel controls the causal response of the environment, while the Keldysh kernel controls environmental fluctuations and hence loss of contrast. At leading order, these kernels reduce to: (i) DM bubble diagrams in the case of four-point interactions, and (ii) dressed in-medium mediator propagators in the case of long-range interactions, where the dressing is provided by the DM environment. Within this framework, we study the signatures of elastic spin-independent sub-GeV DM scattering off nucleons and find that: (i) owing to Bose enhancements below the DM wave-particle boundary, ambitious CPIs could probe Higgs-portal DM with mass below 1 eV in an unexplored region of model space; (ii) for DM coupled to nucleons via a light hadrophilic scalar, the DM background can drive the mediator's in-medium mass tachyonic over much of parameter space; outside these regions, CPIs could probe provide powerful probes of light DM.

</details>

*Steven Ferrante,Lillian Luo,Maxim Perelstein,Bingrong Yu*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种同时包含WIMP和ALP暗物质的新型宇宙学场景，通过微弱的普朗克压制耦合实现二者相互作用，导致‘协同冻结’与‘ALP奇迹’机制，可自然解释观测到的暗物质丰度。

Result: 发现即使极小的普朗克压制耦合也能显著改变二者宇宙学演化：WIMP通过‘协同冻结’机制在远晚于标准冻结时间后退耦，允许远高于热目标值的湮灭截面；ALP振荡起始于相变之后，其遗迹密度由WIMP质量与耦合唯一确定，并在弱标与普朗克标自然取值下自动匹配观测值（即‘ALP奇迹’），且预测ALP质量 > 10^{-3} eV。

Conclusion: ‘ALP奇迹’提供了一种自洽、自然且可检验的双组分暗物质框架，其参数空间与当前QCD轴子带部分重叠，有望被IAXO等实验探测。

Abstract: Both Weakly-Interacting Massive Particle (WIMP) and Axion-Like Particle (ALP) are attractive particle dark matter candidates. In this paper we consider a scenario in which both WIMP and ALP are present. The two species interact via a non-derivative quadratic coupling modeled on the axion-nucleon interaction in QCD. We show that even a small (Planck-suppressed) coupling can drastically modify the cosmological history of both species. In the very early Universe, the ALP field acquires a vacuum expectation value, and the system undergoes an inverse phase transition at a temperature around or below the weak scale. WIMPs may remain in equilibrium far beyond the usual freeze-out time, eventually decoupling via a novel "coherent freeze-out" mechanism. WIMP annihilation cross sections several orders of magnitude above the canonical thermal target value can be consistent with the observed relic density in the coherent freeze-out scenario. At the same time, coherent oscillations of the ALP field do not begin until after the phase transition, and the initial conditions for these oscillations are set by the thermal physics of the phase transition. The ALP relic density is then predicted unambiguously in terms of the WIMP mass and coupling. We term this scenario "ALP miracle", since the observed relic density is obtained when these parameters are around their natural values, the weak and the Planck scale. The ALP miracle scenario prefers the ALP mass above about $10^{-3}\,\mathrm{eV}$, far larger than other popular ALP dark matter models. The ALP miracle parameter space overlaps with a currently allowed region of the QCD axion band, a large fraction of which is expected to be probed by the proposed IAXO experiment.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a projection formalism for four-dimensional massless scattering amplitudes based on the construction of harmonics on multi-particle phase space. This provides a generalization of the partial-wave decomposition valid for any number of particles. Applying this formalism to calculations of on-shell cuts, we show that any cut calculation can be rewritten as a sum of products of projected coefficients and derive a condition for this sum to vanish. Using the on-shell renormalization group equation in combination with our projection formalism, we derive new non-renormalization theorems for effective field theories valid at general loop orders.

Abstract: We study neutrino mass generation in a model based on the $SU(3)_c \times SU(2)_1 \times SU(2)_2 \times U(1)_Y$ gauge symmetry, in which the Standard Model $SU(2)_L$ arises at low energies as the diagonal subgroup of the two $SU(2)$ factors. The symmetry is enlarged with a global $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry, spontaneously broken by the vacuum expectation value of a scalar bidoublet. Light neutrino masses are generated at tree level through a hybrid of the type-III, inverse and linear seesaw mechanisms. A distinctive feature of the construction is the existence of a Nambu-Goldstone boson, the majoron, which transforms non-trivially under the original gauge symmetry while being mostly singlet under the Standard Model gauge group. We explore the phenomenological consequences of this setup, including electroweak precision constraints, the production of the nearly degenerate $W'$ and $Z'$ bosons at colliders and lepton flavor violating decays with a majoron in the final state. The latter constitute the most promising signature of the model.

Abstract: We study the minimum cosmic reheating temperature allowed by recent measurements of primordial nuclear abundances and of the cosmic microwave background. We find that reheating by decays of a light particle into electrons and neutrinos is compatible with both at temperatures around 0.4 MeV, an order of magnitude below the bound for purely electromagnetic channels. This scenario has implications for cosmology and laboratory searches. For example, nearly standard neutrino radiation can coexist with a smaller active neutrino mass density at unchanged physical masses, by up to a factor of five for normal ordering. The weaker suppression of clustering changes matter power at the percent level, which forthcoming galaxy surveys and lensing measurements may probe. Late reheating also suppresses sterile neutrino production at mixings within the projected reach of laboratory experiments.

Abstract: We study the infrared sensitivity of heavy-quark pair production by a color-neutral vector current in perturbation theory. We work in a spontaneously broken SU(2) gauge theory where, by tuning parameters to make the gauge-boson mass $m_g$ small, we use it as a probe of infrared physics. We find that, through next-to-next-to-leading order, terms linear in $m_g$ cancel in the differential cross section, provided that it is expressed through a short-distance heavy-quark mass. Interestingly, we establish this cancellation without evaluating any integrals explicitly, by relating virtual integrals to real-emission ones through loop-energy residues, with a suitable redefinition of the real-emission momenta. Our result is the first strong indication that the cancellation of non-perturbative linear power corrections in the corresponding QCD process extends to the non-Abelian sector. It is also a concrete non-Abelian example of the extension of the Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg theorem to linear infrared terms for sufficiently inclusive observables.

</details>

Abstract: Machine learning tasks in particle physics are typically expressed in terms of regression, or relaxation toward some optimal solution as defined by an objective or loss function. In this paper, we introduce a large multiplicity expansion for analyzing regression tasks, which enables significant analytic understanding. For concreteness, we study regression with energy flow polynomials (EFPs), a complete linear basis of infrared and collinear safe observables on Lorentz invariant phase space. While the expectation value of any EFP scales like a constant in the large multiplicity limit, we prove that the covariance on uniform phase space of any two EFPs is highly suppressed and scales like the inverse of the squared multiplicity. This very unintuitive result relies on detailed cancellation guaranteed by the interplay of permutation invariance, momentum conservation, and rotational invariance. This scaling of the covariance with multiplicity further implies that the coefficients in an expansion of EFPs must scale quadratically in multiplicity. We present an example of regression with EFPs as applied to $e^+e^-\to$ hadrons events, and show how the regression can be solved analytically through the complementary expansions of the large multiplicity limit and of familiar fixed-order perturbation theory of QCD.

Abstract: We show that, for most gauged scalar fields, the scalar potential can be systematically organised into an ordered tree of increasingly symmetric operators. The intermediary global symmetries represented in the tree allow at tree-level a subset of the scalar field components, the custodial scalars, to be technically lighter than the scalar mass scale, or even exactly massless (so-called accidents). We further demonstrate, using the notion of moment maps, that such structure can be related to the Higgs branch of supersymmetric theories, allowing the use of powerful symplectic geometry tools in studying the vacuum of non-supersymmetric scalar potentials. We present various applications, from an example of a non-abelian accident to a Standard Model-like Higgs sector generated from custodial scalars.
