<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电子俘获超新星早期中微子爆发中谱分裂的形成机制，特别是其背后共进动解的稳定性问题。

Method: 推导非对角味背景下的显式色散关系，分析共进动解的慢相位调制行为，并在流体力学极限下研究其不稳定性起源；类比磁化等离子体，建立味等离子体物理图像。

Result: 发现共进动中微子等离子体存在由慢相位调制引发的Goldstone型不稳定性，导致共进动平面发生振幅增大的‘摆动’；该不稳定性是流体力学极限下的本征行为，对应于磁化等离子体中火软不稳定性在味空间的类比。

Conclusion: 共进动中微子等离子体具有类似磁化等离子体的动力学特性，其相位扭曲不稳定性为理解超新星中微子味演化提供了新视角，且无需依赖微观共振细节。

Abstract: Dense neutrino plasmas support collective flavor motion. We derive the first explicit dispersion relation about a flavor-nondiagonal background, focusing on the coprecession solution underlying spectral-split formation during the prompt neutrino burst of an electron-capture supernova. We show that slow phase modulations are unstable Goldstone modes, causing the coprecession plane to wobble with increasing amplitude. The instability emerges in the hydrodynamic limit, governed by conservation laws and without resolving flavor-precession resonances. Coprecessing neutrino plasmas resemble magnetized plasmas: a coherent flavor field drives flavor isospins to precess, and the phase-twist instability is the flavor analog of the firehose instability.

</details>


### [103] [Projected sensitivity to the ADD large extra dimensions model in the monojet plus missing transverse momentum final state at HL-LHC, HE-LHC, and FCC-hh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36107)
*Emine Gurpinar Guler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对ADD大额外维度模型在单喷注+缺失横动量末态下的探测灵敏度进行了统一投影，覆盖HL-LHC、HE-LHC和FCC-hh三个未来对撞机，并通过基于Run-2数据的n依赖校准确保结果可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确评估未来高能对撞机对ADD模型的探测能力，需统一分析框架并锚定现有实验限制，避免模型假设偏差。

Method: 使用Pythia 8生成信号样本，结合ATLAS/CMS发布的标准模型背景数据；采用基于Z(νν̄)+jets和W(ℓν)+jets样本实测的E_T^miss依赖背景能量标度；引入n依赖的Run-2锚定校准，并验证选择效率与ATLAS截断流一致。

Result: 对n=2，95% CL排除上限M_D分别达12.5 TeV（HL-LHC）、15.2 TeV（HE-LHC）、22.0 TeV（FCC-hh）；5σ发现上限分别为9.8、12.0、17.0 TeV，较Run-2结果提升1.1–2.0倍；背景能量标度实测使FCC-hh灵敏度下降39%。

Conclusion: 该统一框架具备可推广性，其校准方案为未来对撞机研究提供了模板；所有投影均建立在Run-2锚定的探测器与不确定性假设之上，结果稳健可靠。

Abstract: We present a unified projection of the sensitivity to the Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali (ADD) model of large extra dimensions in the monojet plus missing transverse momentum final state at the HL-LHC ($\sqrt{s}=14$ TeV, 3 ab$^{-1}$), the HE-LHC (27 TeV, 15 ab$^{-1}$) and the FCC-hh (100 TeV, 30 ab$^{-1}$), for $n=2,3,4,6$ extra dimensions, with one selection, significance estimator and calibration throughout. Signal samples are generated with Pythia 8 and combined with the post-fit Standard-Model background yields released by ATLAS and CMS on HEPData; the expected significance combines the thirteen exclusive $E_{\mathrm{T}}^{\mathrm{miss}}$ bins of the ATLAS signal region, matching the binning of the published likelihood fit. Two elements are new. First, the energy scaling of the background to higher $\sqrt{s}$, including the $13\to14$ TeV step, is measured as a function of $E_{\mathrm{T}}^{\mathrm{miss}}$ from dedicated $Z(ν\barν)$+jets and $W(\ellν)$+jets samples rather than assumed constant; this reduces the projected FCC-hh reach by 39% relative to a constant-factor treatment. Second, the framework is anchored to the published ATLAS Run-2 expected 95% CL limits through a transparent $n$-dependent calibration, determined directly for all five values of $n$ and found consistent with unity ($1.001\pm1.4\%$), so that it acts as a consistency check rather than a sizeable correction; the selection response is validated against the published ATLAS cutflow. The projected 95% CL exclusion reach in $M_D$ for $n=2$ is 12.5, 15.2 and 22.0 TeV at the three colliders, with $5σ$ discovery reach of 9.8, 12.0 and 17.0 TeV, extending the ATLAS Run-2 bound of $M_D>11.2$ TeV by factors of 1.1-2.0. The calibration scheme provides a template for analogous future-collider studies; the projections hold within the stated Run-2-anchored detector and uncertainty assumptions.

</details>


### [104] [How particle physics stops ultra-relativistic bubbles and shapes their gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36160)
*Alessandro Strumia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙一级相变中超相对论性气泡产生的引力波谱，指出其主要由粒子散射主导，并受粒子物理耦合、相互作用类型及粒子数守恒影响；全局对称性破缺情形下则呈现弹性和非弹性碰撞混合特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一级相变中引力波谱的产生机制，特别是超相对论性气泡的动力学及其能量耗散过程对GW谱的影响。

Method: 理论建模与分析，重点考虑气泡碰撞、经典后续演化及粒子散射（尤其是LPM效应）对能量密度分布和GW谱的贡献。

Result: 气泡碰撞及后续经典演化对GW谱影响可忽略；主导能量损失来自粒子散射（常处于LPM区域）；GW谱依赖于耦合强度、相互作用类型和粒子数是否守恒；全局对称性破缺导致弹性与非弹性碰撞共存。

Conclusion: 引力波谱本质上由微观粒子物理过程决定，不能仅用流体力学近似描述；需结合具体粒子模型才能准确预测观测信号。

Abstract: The gravitational-wave spectrum produced by ultra-relativistic bubbles in a first-order cosmological phase transition can be approximated in terms of their energy density as a function of bubble size. Bubble collisions and post-collision classical evolution negligibly change it. The dominant energy loss is particle scattering, often in the Landau--Pomeranchuk--Migdal regime. So the GW spectrum depends on the particle-physics coupling (interpolating between the `envelope' and `bulk flow' limits), on the interaction type (gauge, Yukawa, scalar quartic, ...), and on whether particle number is conserved. Finally, we consider broken global symmetries, which give a mixture of `elastic' and `inelastic' collisions.

</details>


### [105] [Asymmetric Reheating of a Dark Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36161)
*Catarina Cosme,João G. Rosa,Inês Sequeira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀后不对称再加热机制，提出一个包含可见与暗两个隔离MSSM类扇区的模型，两扇区超对称破缺能标不同，暗物质为最轻的暗重子；通过各自独立的Affleck-Dine机制自然解释可见与暗物质丰度相近，并分析不同衰变道（2/3/4体）对温度梯度、暗扇区热历史及ΔN_eff的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释可见物质与暗物质丰度相近这一宇宙学疑难，并构建一个具有物理自洽性的双扇区超对称框架。

Method: 构建含两个隔离MSSM类扇区（可见与暗）的模型，引入不同超对称破缺尺度；分析暴胀子通过2体、3体和4体衰变向暗扇区输运能量的动力学；计算各衰变模式下暗扇区再加热温度、热历史演化及对有效中微子自由度ΔN_eff的贡献。

Result: 4体衰变导致最强的再加热不对称性；3体衰变在暗Higgs对产生阈值附近使ΔN_eff达最大值约0.078；当前BBN+CMB+BAO联合限制仅允许2体衰变略高于阈值的一个窄区间；下一代CMB实验有望探测阈值略下方区域。

Conclusion: 该双扇区模型为可见与暗物质丰度提供自然解释，且其可检验的ΔN_eff信号为未来CMB观测提供了关键探针，凸显了非标准再加热动力学在暗物质与早期宇宙研究中的重要性。

Abstract: We study asymmetric post-inflationary reheating in a framework with two sequestered MSSM-like sectors (the ``visible'' and ``dark'' sectors) with different supersymmetry-breaking scales, with dark matter corresponding to the lightest dark baryons. In this setup, independent Affleck-Dine dynamics in the two sectors offers a natural explanation for the comparable abundances of visible and dark matter. We relate the resulting temperature hierarchy to the inflaton's partial decay widths in different kinematical regimes, where it may decay into dark sector particles via 2-, 3- or 4-body processes. The latter case yields the most asymmetric reheating scenario, and we discuss the implications of this regime for the dark sector's thermal history, namely regarding the corresponding strong and weak interactions. While in this kinematical regime the contribution of light dark sector degrees of freedom to $ΔN_{\rm eff}$ is negligible, we show that it increases significantly in the regime where 3-body decays are possible, particularly near the threshold for dark Higgs pair-production where $ΔN_{\rm eff}^{\max} \simeq 0.078$. The current combined BBN+CMB+BAO bound leaves only a narrow allowed interval of the 2-body decay regime immediately above threshold, while next-generation CMB experiments could probe slightly below it.

</details>


### [106] [A GOOFy Three Higgs Doublet Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36163)
*I. de Medeiros Varzielas,P. M. Ferreira,S. Oliveira*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个具有GOOFy对称性的三Higgs双峰模型，并通过直接计算β函数证明了其重整化群不变性，同时分析了其现象学特征，表明该模型与当前LHC观测结果兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在双Higgs双峰模型中发现了一些无法用已知对称性描述但具有全阶微扰论重整化群不变性的参数区域，被称为GOOFy对称性；本文旨在将此类对称性推广至三Higgs双峰模型并拓展至费米子扇区。

Method: 构建具有GOOFy对称性的三Higgs双峰模型，包含费米子扇区扩展，并通过直接计算模型的β函数来证明其重整化群不变性。

Result: 证明了所构建模型具有全阶重整化群不变性，且其现象学预测与当前LHC实验观测兼容，并展现出可在近未来探测的新特征。

Conclusion: GOOFy对称性可成功推广至更复杂的三Higgs双峰模型，为探索超越标准模型的新物理提供了新方向和可行框架。

Abstract: Recent developments in two-Higgs doublet models identified regions of parameter space which are renormalization-group invariant to all orders of perturbation theory but cannot be described by any of the known symmetries of those models. These were dubbed GOOFy symmetries, as they involve scalar doublets and their complex conjugates transforming independently, and imaginary scaling of spacetime coordinates is needed to ensure invariance of kinetic terms. We build a three-Higgs doublet model with a GOOFy symmetry including extensions to the fermion sector. We prove renormalization group invariance through a direct calculation of the model's beta functions and analyse the corresponding phenomenology. The model is shown to be compatible with current LHC observations, displaying interesting features which may be probed in the near future.

</details>


### [107] [Quark mixing and CP violation due to torsional interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36611)
*Arnab Chakraborty,Amitabha Lahiri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了时空中的费米子动力学如何产生挠率，进而导致一种独立于引力的四费米子电流-电流相互作用；该相互作用具有手征性和非普适性，并在夸克部分引入类CKM矩阵，影响CP破坏和介子衰变等过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由费米子动力学引发的挠率效应及其对粒子物理标准模型外潜在可观测现象的影响，特别是对味物理和CP破坏的修正。

Method: 通过分析挠率诱导的四费米子相互作用，推导其在夸克扇区的结构，对角化该相互作用以获得新的幺正混合矩阵（类CKM矩阵），并研究其对CP破坏、介子衰变及重相位不变量（如Jarlskog不变量）的影响。

Result: 发现挠率相互作用即使在味基下对角，仍因耦合常数非普适性而影响味改变过程；引入新CKM型矩阵和额外CP相位，修改CP破坏行为；Jarlskog不变量在挠率基与味基对齐时仍被修正。

Conclusion: 挠率诱导的四费米子相互作用为超越标准模型的新物理提供了可观测窗口，尤其在味物理和CP破坏方面具有重要理论与实验意义。

Abstract: The dynamics of fermions in spacetime generates torsion, ultimately leading to a current-current interaction independent of gravity. This torsional four-fermion interaction is generically chiral and non-universal and may have observable consequences. Furthermore, the fermion basis in which this torsional interaction is diagonal may be different from the mass basis and the flavor basis. We work out the structure of the quark sector, where new CKM-like matrices appear as a result of diagonalizing the torsional interaction. CP violation is modified in general due to the appearance of additional phases. We also consider various processes which are affected by it such as kaon decays in particular and show that even when the torsional interaction is taken to be diagonal in the flavor basis, flavor-changing processes are still affected due to the non-universality of the torsional coupling constants. Finally, we discuss rephasing invariants in the presence of the torsional interaction and show how the Jarlskog invariant is modified even when the torsion basis is aligned with the flavor basis.

</details>


### [108] [Energy density in color string clusters and its implications for heavy-quark energy loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36723)
*Daria Prokhorova,Shuzhe Shi,Evgeny Andronov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了相对论性离子和质子-质子碰撞初期由色通量管形成的介质特性，提出了两种表征该介质的方法：基于色电场强度（与簇中色源数的平方根成正比）和基于受限色场能量密度（与色源数线性相关），并比较二者对所提取介质有效温度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能核碰撞初期由色通量管形成的非微扰强相互作用介质的性质，特别是其有效温度，这对计算探针粒子的能量损失至关重要。

Method: 在完全可穿透色弦体积近似下，通过分析高密度弦重叠形成的随机取向色场簇，分别基于色电场强度和能量密度两种标度律来表征介质，并据此推导有效温度。

Result: 两种表征方式导致所提取的有效温度具有不同标度行为：一种随色源数平方根变化，另一种随色源数线性变化，从而显著影响后续能量损失计算。

Conclusion: 介质的有效温度依赖于所采用的物理量（色电场强度或能量密度）作为表征基准，这提示在建模早期阶段介质性质时需谨慎选择基础物理量。

Abstract: Initial stages of relativistic ion and proton-proton collisions can be described within the framework of color flux tube formation between the receding color charges. In the approximation of fully penetrable color string volumes, regions of high string density yield overlaps of randomly oriented color fields and create string clusters. The paper discusses two ways of characterizing the medium formed by the resulting field configurations. One is based on the chromo-electric field strength that scales as the square root of the number of color sources in the cluster. The other is defined by the energy density of the confined color field and thus scales linearly with that number. We compare the effect of the two approaches on the extracted effective temperature of the medium, which is the input for calculations of collisional and radiative energy loss of a probe traversing it.

</details>


### [109] [Testing the Gaussian Ansatz for TMD PDFs Through CMS Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36772)
*Shuailiang Yang,Yingqi Hou,Xiaoyu Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在横动量依赖（TMD）因子化框架下研究质子-质子Drell-Yan过程的微分截面，比较CSS演化方法与高斯假设对TMD PDF横动量依赖的描述能力，发现CSS方法在全能量范围与CMS 13 TeV数据吻合良好，而高斯假设仅适用于低能区；结果验证了TMD因子化在高能过程中的有效性，并支持对核子内部结构和非微扰QCD动力学的深入研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证TMD因子化定理在高能过程中的适用性，并比较不同TMD PDF横动量依赖模型（CSS演化 vs 高斯假设）对Drell-Yan过程微分截面的描述能力，以深化对核子内部结构和非微扰QCD的理解。

Method: 基于TMD因子化框架，计算pp Drell-Yan过程关于横动量q_T的微分截面，在不同双轻子不变质量区间内，分别采用Collins-Soper-Sterman（CSS）演化方法和高斯Ansatz两种TMD PDF横动量依赖形式，并与CMS 13 TeV实验数据对比。

Result: CSS演化方法在全部q_T和不变质量区间均与CMS数据高度一致；高斯Ansatz仅在低q_T（低能）区域有效，在高q_T（高能）区域显著偏离数据。

Conclusion: TMD因子化定理在高能Drell-Yan过程中是有效的；CSS演化方法具有更强的普适性和预测能力，是描述TMD分布函数演化行为的更优选择；该研究为核子三维结构及非微扰QCD研究提供了可靠的理论支撑。

Abstract: We investigate the differential cross section of the $pp$ Drell-Yan process within the framework of Transverse Momentum Dependent (TMD) factorization. We perform phenomenological calculations of the differential cross section as a function of $q_T$ in different dilepton invariant mass bins and compare it with the measured data from the CMS experiment at $13~$TeV, employing two distinct transverse momentum dependencies of the parton distribution functions: the Collins-Soper-Sterman (CSS) evolution method and the Gaussian Ansatz. The results indicate that the CSS evolution method agrees well with experimental data across the entire energy range, demonstrating its strong predictive capability for the evolution behavior of TMD distribution functions. In contrast, the Gaussian Ansatz of the TMD PDFs is only applicable in the low-energy region and exhibits significant limitations at high energies. This study verifies the effectiveness of the TMD factorization theorem in describing high-energy processes and provides theoretical support for an in-depth exploration of the nucleon's internal structure and non-perturbative QCD dynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Probing Charged Higgs Bosons through $H^\pm \to W^\pm A$ Decay in the Type-I Two-Higgs Doublet Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36780)
*Abdesslam Arhrib,Mohamed Krab,Tanmoy Mondal,Prasenjit Sanyal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上通过H±→W±(*)A衰变道，搜寻类型I双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）中的带电Higgs玻色子，该道在中等至大tanβ区域具有高灵敏度，并可显著拓展参数空间探测范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在类型I 2HDM中，轻赝标量A（mA < mh125/2）仍被现有实验约束所允许；而传统H±→τν和H±→tb搜寻受限，需探索新衰变道以提升探测能力。

Method: 聚焦于主导的玻色子衰变H±→W±(*)A，并设计4b+2j+ℓν末态进行LHC探测分析，结合h125→AA产生机制与当前信号强度及直接搜寻限制。

Result: 该新搜寻通道可在高亮度LHC上有效探测类型I 2HDM中此前未被充分覆盖的大tanβ、中等质量H±参数区域。

Conclusion: H±→W±(*)A是类型I 2HDM中极具潜力的互补探针，能显著增强LHC对带电Higgs玻色子的探测能力，尤其在传统费米子衰变受限的情形下。

Abstract: A light pseudoscalar lighter than the 125 GeV Higgs boson remains allowed within the type-I Two-Higgs Doublet Model (2HDM), consistent with current collider constraints, including Higgs signal strength measurements and direct searches for additional Higgs bosons. At the Large Hadron Collider (LHC), the light pseudoscalar $A$ can be pair-produced through the undetected decay of the Standard Model-like Higgs boson, $h_{125} \to AA$, when this channel is kinematically accessible (i.e., $m_A < m_{h_{125}}/2$). For the charged scalar $H^\pm$, once open, the bosonic decay channel $H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A$ naturally dominates over the fermionic $H^\pm \to τ^\pmν$ and $H^\pm \to tb$ channels, while relaxing the existing $H^\pm$ limits. As a result, we propose searching for the charged Higgs boson through $H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A$ in the $4b+2j+\ellν$ final state. This channel is particularly sensitive to moderate-to-large $\tanβ$ values and provides an excellent complementary probe to searches based on fermionic $H^\pm$ decays at the LHC. Our collider analysis demonstrates that this channel can probe a substantial region of the type-I 2HDM parameter space at the high-luminosity LHC.

</details>


### [111] [Two-body strong decays of the $P_{cs}(4338)$ as a hidden-charm pentaquark state via the QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36861)
*Zhi-Gang Wang,Jia-Wei Zhu,Yang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用三点QCD求和规则，研究了自旋宇称为1/2^-的Pcs(4338)五夸克态的强子耦合常数，并计算其部分衰变宽度和总宽度，结果与LHCb实验数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过计算Pcs(4338)的强子耦合常数和衰变宽度，以区分五夸克态与分子态等不同结构解释。

Method: 采用三点QCD求和规则，并依据严格的夸克-强子对偶性，选取最优参数化方案以消除高激发态和连续谱的污染。

Result: 得到Pcs(4338)总宽度为Γ=10.48±1.79 MeV，与LHCb实验值7.0±1.2±1.3 MeV一致；部分衰变宽度满足Γ(J/ψΛ) > Γ(D̄_sΛ_c) > Γ(η_cΛ)的层次关系。

Conclusion: Pcs(4338)可被合理描述为[us][dc]c̄−[ds][uc]c̄型五夸克态，其理论预测的衰变宽度层次可在未来实验中用于鉴别其内部结构。

Abstract: In this work, we study the hadronic coupling constants of the $P_{cs}(4338)$ as a $[us][dc]\bar{c}-[ds][uc]\bar{c}$ type pentaquark state with the spin-parity $J^P={\frac{1}{2}}^-$ via the three-point QCD sum rules according to rigorous quark-hadron quality. We select the optimal parametrization to subtract contaminations from the higher resonances and continuum states and obtain ideal hadronic coupling constants therefore the partial decay widths and total width $Γ(P_{cs}(4338))=10.48\pm 1.79\,\rm{MeV}$, which are consistent with the experimental data $Γ(P_{cs}(4338)) = 7.0\pm1.2\pm1.3 \mbox{ MeV}$ from the LHCb collaboration. The partial decay widths have the hierarchy $Γ(J/ψΛ)> Γ(\bar{D}_sΛ_c)> Γ(η_cΛ)$, which can be compared to the experimental data in the future to distinguish the pentaquark and molecule scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [Nucleon decays into three leptons plus a kaon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36902)
*Jin-Han Liang,Xiao-Dong Ma,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在低能有效场论（LEFT）框架下系统研究了重子数破坏（BNV）的核子四体衰变（到三个轻子加一个K介子），通过分类维度9的局域算符、匹配到手征微扰理论，计算了衰变宽度，并利用现有实验数据给出了严格的寿命限和Wilson系数约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个有效的EFT框架，将底层重子数破坏相互作用与实验上对奇异核子四体衰变的搜寻联系起来。

Method: 基于低能有效场论（LEFT），对手征对称群下含三个轻子与三个夸克（含一个奇异夸克）的维度9局域算符进行分类，并匹配到手征微扰理论，从而导出K⁺或K⁰参与的四体核子衰变宽度；再通过重新分析现有实验数据，提取寿命限制及对应Wilson系数约束。

Result: 得到了所有运动学允许的四体衰变模式（N→ℓℓℓK）的衰变宽度表达式，并基于实验数据给出了各通道部分寿命的严格下限及相应维度9 Wilson系数的上限约束。

Conclusion: 本工作为连接新物理模型中的重子数破坏机制与当前/未来核子衰变实验提供了系统、实用的有效场论工具。

Abstract: We systematically investigate baryon-number-violating (BNV) nucleon decays into three leptons and a kaon within the framework of low-energy effective field theory (LEFT). We identify all kinematically allowed four-body decay modes involving a $K^+$ or $K^0$ meson and derive their decay widths induced by local dimension-9 operators in terms of the LEFT Wilson coefficients. These results are obtained by classifying the relevant operators, which contain three lepton and three quark fields, including one strange quark, according to their transformation properties under the chiral symmetry group, matching them onto chiral perturbation theory. By reanalyzing existing experimental data, we establish stringent bounds on the partial lifetimes of these four-body decay modes and derive corresponding constraints on the dimension-9 Wilson coefficients. Our results provide a convenient EFT framework for connecting the underlying BNV interactions to experimental searches for these exotic nucleon decays.

</details>


### [113] [Quasiparticle Modeling of Strange Quark Stars with In-Medium QCD Effects at Finite Temperature and Strong Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36953)
*Yogesh Kumar,Mihir Arora,Manas Lohani,Poonam Jain,Pargin Bangotra,Rajeev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自旋极化奇异夸克物质（SQM）的性质及其对奇异夸克星（SQSs）结构的影响，提出了一种热力学自洽的准粒子模型，通过引入与介质相关的动态袋常数和夸克有效质量修正，显著提升了高密区状态方程刚度，成功解释了超大质量脉冲星（如PSR J0740−6620和PSR J0952−0607）的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测到的超大质量脉冲星（如PSR J0740−6620和PSR J0952−0607），需构建更合理的奇异夸克物质状态方程，避免不现实的热或结构假设。

Method: 在热力学自洽的准粒子框架下，构建动态袋常数模型，该常数内在耦合于介质依赖的夸克有效质量减小，从而保证严格热力学自洽，并分析其对声速和物态方程的影响。

Result: 模型预言最大稳定引力质量可达2.19倍太阳质量，且声速呈现显著非单调行为，使高密度区物态方程明显变硬。

Conclusion: 热且磁化的奇异夸克物质是一种稳健可行的机制，可自然解释超大质量脉冲星，无需引入极端物理假设。

Abstract: This work investigates the properties of spin polarized strange quark matter SQM and the resulting structure of strange quark stars SQSs within a thermodynamically self consistent quasiparticle framework. By developing a dynamic bag constant intrinsically coupled to the medium dependent reduction of quark effective masses we ensure strict internal thermodynamic consistency. This coupling gives rise to a distinct non monotonic behavior in the speed of sound significantly stiffening the Equation of State EoS at high densities. Consequently our model predicts maximum stable gravitational masses up to 2.19 Mo. These results demonstrate that hot magnetized SQM provides a robust and viable mechanism to explain ultra massive pulsars such as PSR J0740 6620 and PSR J0952 0607 without requiring unrealistic thermal or structural assumptions.

</details>


### [114] [Non-linear effects in coherent photoproduction of heavy quarkonia off nuclei: Green's function formalism vs Balitsky-Kovchegov equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36992)
*I. Koval,J. Nemchik,J. Óbertová*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了高能核光致产生粲偶素过程中QCD非线性效应，比较了Green函数形式与Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）方程在能量演化中的差异，指出BK方程当前形式不适用于核靶，需引入量子相干效应的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解高能核光致产生粲偶素过程中的QCD非线性效应，并评估不同理论框架（Green函数 vs BK方程）对非线性效应能量演化的预测差异。

Method: 采用严格的Green函数（GF）形式，并与基于LO/NLO BK演化方程的计算结果进行对比分析。

Result: 发现GF与BK两种形式对非线性效应的预测存在显著差异，NLO BK虽减小差异但仍导致比GF更强的领头扭阴影效应；BK方程当前形式无法直接用于核靶。

Conclusion: BK方程需非平凡地修改以纳入量子相干效应，才能适用于核靶的非线性QCD动力学描述。

Abstract: We present an analysis of QCD non-linear (NL) effects in the coherent photoproduction of charmonia off nuclei at high energies corresponding to future measurements at the LHC. For the first time, we confront the energy evolution of such effects within our rigorous Green's function (GF) formalism with results from Refs.~\cite{Cepila:2025ujv,Cepila:2024qge}, which are based on the solution of the leading-order (LO) and next-to-leading-order (NLO) Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) evolution equations. We demonstrate a huge difference in the NL effects between these two formalisms, exhibiting a rather strong rise with photon energy. Our analysis shows that NLO calculations of the BK equation from Ref.~\cite{Cepila:2024qge} slow down the rate of the high-energy LO evolution of NL effects. Although these calculations reduce the differences between the GF and BK predictions, they still lead to a much stronger onset of leading-twist shadowing effects compared to the GF formalism. The BK equation in its present form cannot be applied to nuclear targets and thus requires a non-trivial modification that properly incorporates quantum coherence effects.

</details>


### [115] [Constraining New Physics by Studying Single Top Quark Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37039)
*Maryam Roknabadi,Saeid Paktinat Mehdiabadi,Seyed Mohammad Reza Tabatabaei*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种超越标准模型的新框架，引入一个类2HDM带电标量粒子H_c，但不施加2HDM常规约束；通过分析其对单顶夸克s-和t-道产生截面及s-道自旋关联的影响，结合二者可有效限制该模型的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越标准模型的新物理，特别是通过引入一个不受2HDM传统约束限制的带电标量粒子H_c，以检验其对单顶夸克产生过程的可观测效应。

Method: 构建一个简化的唯象模型，引入带电标量H_c；计算并比较s-道和t-道单顶夸克产生截面随模型参数的变化；分析s-道中传播子粒子自旋导致的顶夸克自旋关联差异。

Result: 在所考虑的简化模型中，s-和t-道截面变化与自旋关联分析的联合使用，能够显著约束H_c模型的参数空间。

Conclusion: H_c粒子对单顶夸克产生具有可观测影响，截面与自旋关联的联合分析是探测此类新物理的有效策略。

Abstract: This study introduces a beyond the Standard Model framework by adding a charged scalar particle, H_c, analogous to the charged Higgs in the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM), but analyzed without the restrictive constraints typically applied in 2HDM scenarios. To assess the impact of H_c on single top quark production, two approaches are taken. First, variations in the production cross-section via the s- and t-channels are examined under different values of the model parameters, compared to the SM predictions. Second, spin correlations are analyzed, focusing on differences in the spin of the mediated particle between the new model and SM in the single top quark production via the s-channel. Within the framework of the simplified phenomenological model considered here, a significant fraction of the parameter space can be constrained by combining the two approaches.

</details>


### [116] [Higher-order strongly intensive observables for forward-backward multiplicity correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37051)
*Evgeny Andronov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的强强度观测量层级，用于消除粒子产生中源数涨落的影响，并在所有累积量阶数上保持对源数涨落的不敏感性；该方法在pp碰撞实验数据（如PYTHIA模拟和ALICE测量）中验证了其对窗口几何、事件选择及多粒子关联的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向-后向多重数关联可探测粒子产生的纵向结构，但发射源数量的涨落会干扰其物理解释；为消除这种干扰，需构建对源数涨落高阶不敏感的观测量。

Method: 构建一个归一化的强强度观测量层级，每阶n引入n个代数独立的新观测量；要求其对统计独立粒子归一化为1、当两窗多重数固定时为0、且在窗平均多重数相等时保持有限；给出显式表达式（至四阶）与全阶递推关系。

Result: 该层级观测量在解析粒子产生模型和PYTHIA pp碰撞模拟中展现出对窗口几何、事件筛选及连通多粒子关联的显著敏感性；结合低阶观测量拟合可提供高阶条件基准，用以检验关联层次假设；初步ALICE 13 TeV数据支持相应模型依赖场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的全阶强强度观测量层级有效消除了源数涨落影响，为高精度探测QCD初态与粒子产生机制提供了新工具，尤其适用于小型碰撞系统中的关联结构分析。

Abstract: Forward-backward multiplicity correlations probe the longitudinal structure of particle production, but fluctuations in the number of emitting sources complicate their interpretation. Strongly intensive observables remove the contribution of source-number fluctuations at second order. Here we extend the construction to all cumulant orders. For independent identical sources whose emission law does not depend on the source count, we build a normalized hierarchy of strongly intensive observables with $n$ new algebraically independent quantities at order $n$. Every observable is normalized to unity for statistically independent particles, vanishes when both window multiplicities are fixed, and remains finite at equal positive window means. We give explicit expressions through fourth order and an all-order recursion. Analytical particle production models and PYTHIA calculations for $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=0.9$ and 13 TeV demonstrate the sensitivity to window geometry, event selection, and connected multiparticle correlations. Fits constrained by the mean multiplicities and second-order observables provide conditional higher-order benchmarks. Deviations of these benchmarks from the PYTHIA results test the assumed correlation hierarchy, subject to the quality of the lower-order fit. Preliminary ALICE measurements at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV are used to illustrate the corresponding model-dependent scenarios.

</details>


### [117] [Probing new $C\!P$-violating sources in $Λ_b\toΛ_c\ell^-\barν_\ell$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37106)
*Dong-Hao Li,Meng-Chao Gao,Qin Qin,Meng-Han Wu,Fu-Sheng Yu,Xing-Bo Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过比较Λ_b→Λ_cℓν和B̄→D*ℓν的角分布，利用格点QCD形状因子和R(D(*))等全局拟合，研究重子CP破坏对新物理的敏感性，发现重子衰变可探测介子衰变无法触及的新物理威尔逊系数组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管重子CP破坏已被观测到，但其揭示超出介子衰变灵敏度的新物理潜力仍不明确。

Method: 基于格点QCD计算的形状因子，对比Λ_b→Λ_c(→Λπ)ℓν与B̄→D*(→Dπ)ℓν的角分布；结合R(D(*))及其它CP偶测量进行全局拟合，确定新物理威尔逊系数的可行参数空间，并评估二者对弱相位的新物理敏感性。

Result: 重子衰变Λ_b→Λ_cℓν可探测介子衰变B̄→D*ℓν角分布无法覆盖的威尔逊系数组合，提供识别新物理的关键互补信息。

Conclusion: 重子半轻子衰变在探索新物理方面具有不可替代的补充作用，尤其在探测特定组合的威尔逊系数和弱相位方面优于传统介子衰变。

Abstract: While baryonic $C\!P$ violation has been observed, its potential to reveal new physics beyond the sensitivity of mesonic decays remains an open question. We investigate this potential within semileptonic $b\to c\ell\barν_\ell$ transitions by comparing the angular distributions of $Λ_b\toΛ_c(\toΛπ)\ell^-\barν_\ell$ and $\bar B\to D^*(\to Dπ)\ell^-\barν_\ell$ utilizing lattice-QCD form factors. We perform a global fit to $R(D^{(*)})$ alongside auxiliary $C\!P$-even measurements to delineate the phenomenologically viable parameter space for new-physics Wilson coefficients. Leveraging these allowed ranges and the lattice inputs, we evaluate the complementary sensitivity of both angular distributions to novel weak phases. We demonstrate that the baryonic decay mode probes combinations of Wilson coefficients that remain inaccessible in mesonic angular distributions, thereby providing critical supplementary information for identifying new physics.

</details>


### [118] [Reggeized $K*(892)$ exchange in $π^- p \to K^0 Λ$ and implications for fixed-target measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37109)
*Hao-Nan Wang,Cheng Chen,Yre Ma,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了π⁻p→K⁰Λ反应的总截面，采用单个Regge化t-道K*(892)交换模型，在π⁻束流动量2.6–6.0 GeV/c范围内进行描述，并分析其对固定靶实验的启示；同时利用理想化事例样本给出Kₛ⁰和Λ衰变产物的运动学分布，为后续探测器接受度与追踪研究提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: π⁻p→K⁰Λ反应是研究奇异粒子产生及获取标记Λ超子样本的理想通道，需建立可靠的截面模型并提供关键衰变产物的运动学分布以支撑未来固定靶实验设计。

Method: 采用无极化单Regge化t-道K*(892)交换模型描述总截面；使用理想化蒙特卡洛事例样本模拟Kₛ⁰→π⁺π⁻和Λ→pπ⁻衰变，提取实验室系中各带电粒子的动量与极角分布。

Result: 成功拟合了2.6–6.0 GeV/c动量区间的总截面；发现Kₛ⁰衰变双π介子高度前向，而Λ衰变的质子与π⁻覆盖更大角度、更低动量区域。

Conclusion: 该模型与运动学分布可作为未来固定靶实验（如PANDA、J-PARC）中探测器设计、接受度评估与粒子识别策略的重要基准。

Abstract: The two-body \(π^-p\to K^0Λ\) reaction provides a clean channel for studying strangeness production and producing tagged hyperon \(Λ\) samples. We describe its total cross section over the $π^-$ beam momentum \(2.6 \leq p_{π,\lab}\leq6.0~\gev/c\) using a single Reggeized \(t\)-channel \(K^*(892)\) exchange without polarization. We also examine the implications for future fixed-target measurements. An idealized event sample is used to determine the laboratory momentum and polar-angle distributions of the charged particles from \(K_S^0\toπ^+π^-\) and \(Λ\to pπ^-\). The \(K_S^0\) decayed two pions are strongly forward, whereas the proton and pion from the \(Λ\) decay extend to larger angles and lower momenta. These distributions provide a kinematic baseline for future acceptance and tracking studies.

</details>


### [119] [What the Primary Lund Plane Omits: A Model-Stitching Diagnosis of a Jet-Tagger Representation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37137)
*Saurabh Rai,Sanmay Ganguly*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过模型拼接（model stitching）技术分析了Lund-plane和Particle Transformer（ParT）两种喷注标记器性能差异的根源，发现Lund表示在顶夸克喷注识别中存在固有表征缺陷，而非下游计算能力不足；修复完整C/A declustering树表征可显著缩小性能差距，且关键信息集中在主分解分支的宽角分裂上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分Jet tagging性能差异是源于输入表征不足还是下游模型计算能力弱——仅靠独立模型性能无法判断，需解耦表征与计算贡献。

Method: 采用模型拼接方法：将一个网络的冻结前部层与另一网络的冻结后部层用单个可训练线性映射连接；以已知缺陷的Lund主分解分支为正对照，结合超图分裂三元组读取器、AUC与背景拒绝率分析、节点遮蔽实验及完整C/A树重建等进行归因分析。

Result: Lund主分支表征缺陷导致顶夸克标记背景拒绝率大幅下降（远低于ParT），该差距在AUC中不可见但显著体现在低信号效率下的背景拒绝率；仅0.6%高质量背景喷注暴露此缺陷；重建完整C/A树使独立拒绝率提升，填补72%性能缺口；主分支仍占主导，次级分裂主要提升可学习性而非提供新信息；最宽角分裂信息价值约为随机分裂的两倍；对q/g标记该缺陷较轻（保留77.6%拒绝率），完整树仅补33%缺口。

Conclusion: Lund-plane在顶夸克喷注识别中的性能瓶颈主要来自表征能力不足，尤其缺乏完整C/A树结构信息；提升表征设计（如纳入次级分裂结构）比增强下游计算更关键；不同物理任务（top vs q/g）对表征完备性要求差异显著。

Abstract: Lund-plane and Particle Transformer (ParT) jet taggers can differ in performance because one representation is weaker, or because a weaker downstream computation reads an adequate one. Standalone performance cannot separate these. Model stitching can: it links the frozen early layers of one network to the frozen late layers of another through a single trained linear map. As a positive control we use an incomplete Lund representation, the primary declustering branch, whose deficit is known independently. Stitched into ParT's downstream, it reaches a background rejection at least as much as its own downstream recovers, yet far below ParT's background rejection: the deficit is representational, not computational. A hypergraph reader over splitting triplets does no better, ruling out higher-order correlations as the cause. The gap is invisible in AUC, which stays within 1\% of its target value, and appears only in background rejection at low signal efficiency. It localizes to 0.6\% of background jets, at and above the top mass, with more constituents but fewer primary splittings than typical background. Rebuilding the representation on the full Cambridge/Aachen declustering tree repairs the deficit as predicted: standalone rejection rises, closing 72\% of the gap. Masking nodes at inference shows the repaired network still relies almost entirely on the primary branch; the secondary splittings make the representation learnable rather than supplying information it reads. Along the primary branch, the widest-angle splitting, near the top and $W$ opening angles, is worth nearly twice a random one. For quark/gluon tagging the primary branch is far less deficient, retaining 77.6\% of ParT's rejection (46.9\% for top), and the full tree closes only 33\% of the gap (72\% for top).

</details>


### [120] [Searches for new particle physics by methods of classical and neutrino astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37198)
*Sergey Troitsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用天体物理方法检验粒子物理模型的多种途径，包括恒星结构演化、高能光子传播及大型中微子望远镜，并结合最新观测数据（如红巨星支尖、伽马暴GRB 221009A、Baikal-GVD和IceCube）进行参数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用天体物理环境作为天然实验室，探测超出标准模型的新粒子与新相互作用，弥补地面实验在能量与耦合强度上的限制。

Method: 基于前向建模（forward modeling），整合新物理效应、粒子传播、探测响应及系统误差等环节，对恒星演化、高能光子传播、中微子/磁单极子探测等多类天体物理信号进行参数约束。

Result: 通过红巨星支尖亮度、GRB 221009A光子谱、Baikal-GVD与IceCube数据，对能量损失机制、光子-新粒子混合、超重暗物质衰变、非标准中微子传播等粒子物理参数给出了新限制。

Conclusion: 天体物理探针是检验弱耦合、长寿命或高能标新物理的重要互补手段，其约束能力随观测精度与模型精细化程度持续提升。

Abstract: Selected astrophysical methods for testing particle-physics models are reviewed. Stellar structure and evolution constrain additional channels of energy loss and transport; high-energy photon propagation probes photon mixing with new particles and modifications of electron-positron pair production; large-volume neutrino telescopes are used to search for magnetic monopoles, neutrinos from superheavy dark-matter decay, and non-standard neutrino propagation. Recent applications of these methods are discussed, involving the tip of the red-giant branch in the color-luminosity diagram, gamma-ray burst 221009A, and data from the Baikal Gigaton Volume Detector (Baikal-GVD) and the IceCube experiment. In each case, particle-physics parameters are inferred through a forward model that accounts for the response to new physics, propagation, detection, and nuisance parameters.

</details>


### [121] [Exclusive production of doubly charmed baryon pair at B Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37210)
*Xiao Liang,Jun Jiang,Chun-Yuan Li,Meng-Li Liu,Xing-Hua Yang,Zong-Guo Si*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD框架下计算了B工厂中e+e-湮灭产生双粲重子对Ξcc+Ξ¯cc的排他性过程，系统讨论了总截面、理论不确定性、微分分布和事例产额，结果表明BaBar和Belle II实验探测该过程前景不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双粲重子对在e+e-湮灭中的排他性产生机制，评估B工厂实验（如BaBar和Belle II）探测该过程的可行性。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）理论框架进行理论计算，系统分析总截面、理论不确定性、微分分布及事件产额。

Result: 计算表明，BaBar和Belle II实验中该过程的总截面小、事件产额低，探测前景不利。

Conclusion: 在当前B工厂能量和亮度条件下，通过e+e-湮灭排他性产生Ξcc+Ξ¯cc对的实验观测难度大，不具现实可行性。

Abstract: The exclusive production of doubly charmed baryon pairs $Ξ_{cc}+\barΞ_{cc}$ via $e^+e^-$ annihilation at B factories are calculated in the framework of non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD). The total cross sections, theoretical uncertainties, differential distributions and event yields for the BaBar and Belle II experiments are systematically discussed. According to our study, the detection prospects for both experiments are unfavorable.

</details>


### [122] [Global Analysis of Nuclear Parton Distribution Functions using Markov Chain Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37254)
*Nasim Derakhshanian*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架与马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法的核部分子分布函数（nPDFs）全局分析新范式，克服了传统Hessian方法在非高斯不确定性下的局限性，并首次实现了仅基于铅数据的单核素铅PDF确定及多核素联合分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hessian方法依赖χ²函数的局部二次近似（高斯假设），在实验约束有限、参数强相关时难以刻画非对称误差、平缓方向或多极小值等非高斯特征。

Method: 在nCTEQ框架内构建贝叶斯nPDF分析模型，采用自适应Metropolis-Hastings MCMC算法直接采样后验分布；开展单核素（仅Pb数据）和多核素（含轻核）两类全局拟合。

Result: 发现价夸克分布呈现显著非高斯行为和次级极小值；多核素分析显著约束夸克分布并改变铅PDF形状，而胶子分布相对稳定；与Hessian结果对比凸显其在强非线性/多峰区域的不足。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯MCMC方法为nPDF确定提供了更灵活、鲁棒的不确定性量化框架，能更全面刻画参数空间结构与不确定性本质。

Abstract: Global analyses of nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) have traditionally used the Hessian method for uncertainty estimation, relying on a local quadratic approximation of the chi-squared function. This Gaussian approximation can be inadequate when experimental constraints are limited and parameter correlations are strong, as it cannot fully capture asymmetric uncertainties, flat directions, or multiple minima. This thesis develops a Bayesian framework for nPDF determination using Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) techniques within the nCTEQ analysis framework. An adaptive Metropolis-Hastings algorithm enables direct sampling of the posterior distribution, revealing pronounced non-Gaussian behavior and secondary minima, particularly in the valence distributions. The analysis presents the first global single-nucleus determination of lead (Pb) PDFs based exclusively on lead data, complemented by a multi-nuclei analysis incorporating different nuclear targets through a standard analytic dependence on the mass number. Including lighter nuclei significantly constrains quark distributions and changes the extracted lead PDFs, while the gluon distribution remains comparatively stable. A comparison with Hessian-based results highlights the limitations of the Gaussian approximation in regions with strong nonlinearity or multimodality. Overall, this work establishes Bayesian MCMC inference as a flexible framework for nPDF determination, providing a more comprehensive characterization of parameter uncertainties and the underlying parameter space.

</details>


### [123] [Cosmic-string calorimetry with a 220\,PeV neutrino](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37282)
*Bo-Qiang Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了KM3NeT观测到的220 PeV高能中微子事件KM3-230213A可能起源于宇宙弦网络，并利用能量守恒将其视为‘量热计’，从而约束宇宙弦张力Gμ与粒子辐射效率ε_p的乘积；单次事件给出的约束已接近脉冲星计时阵列当前灵敏度，且结合引力波辐射分支，可进一步推断宇宙弦能量在粒子与引力波辐射间的分配比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释极高能中微子事件KM3-230213A的可能天体物理起源，并检验宇宙弦作为其源的可能性，同时利用该事件对宇宙弦基本参数（如张力Gμ和粒子辐射效率）施加新约束。

Method: 基于能量守恒原理，将中微子事件视为宇宙弦网络释放能量的‘量热计’；假设临界电流处的弦尖峰（cusp）以单位效率辐射粒子，由此反推宇宙弦张力Gμ与粒子辐射效率ε_p的乘积；结合已有的脉冲星计时阵列对Gμ的限制，联合分析粒子辐射与引力波辐射分支的能量分配。

Result: 由KM3-230213A事件得到Gμε_p = (2.8–4.3)×10⁻¹¹（取中微子分数f_ν=0.1）；该约束与当前脉冲星计时阵列（达10⁻¹⁰量级）具有可比性；若宇宙弦仍存在引力波辐射分支，则粒子辐射需占总能量约三分之一，未来更多>100 PeV事件可精确测量该份额。

Conclusion: 单个极高能中微子事件可成为探测宇宙弦物理的重要探针，其提供的约束已具备与传统引力波方法（如脉冲星计时）相竞争的潜力；粒子与引力波辐射的联合分析为理解宇宙弦能量耗散机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: KM3NeT has reported KM3-230213A, a $220$\,PeV neutrino with no source identified. If it comes from a cosmic-string network, energy conservation makes the event a calorimeter. The flux measures the power the network has spent, not how it emits, and a cusp at critical current can supply that power at unit efficiency. The event gives $Gμε_p=(2.8\text{--}4.3)\times10^{-11}$ for $f_ν=0.1$. Pulsar timing already reaches $10^{-10}$, so one event competes with the arrays. For the branch that still radiates gravitationally, flux and timing together require a third of the network's energy in particles, a share that further events above $100$\,PeV would measure.

</details>


### [124] [Nonradial oscillation modes of axion condensed neutron stars in the Cowling approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37308)
*Antonio Gómez-Bañón,Pantelis Pnigouras,José A. Pons*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻QCD轴子模型中，中子星内轴子凝聚态对非径向（l=2）振荡谱的影响，发现轴子凝聚改变了中子星平衡结构并引入与轴子场的动力学耦合，从而影响f模和p模频率及阻尼特性，并揭示了轴子主导的准正规模和类原子束缚态及其与流体模的避免交叉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轻QCD轴子模型中，中子星内部有限密度修正可能导致轴子场非零凝聚，进而影响其动力学行为；此前工作仅研究了径向振荡，本文旨在拓展至更物理相关的非径向振荡，并探究轴子-流体耦合对振荡谱的完整影响。

Method: 在相对论Cowling近似下，对轴子凝聚中子星进行非径向（l=2）振荡谱分析，同时将流体扰动与轴子场扰动完全耦合并视为动力学自由度。

Result: 轴子凝聚使f模和p模频率最高提升约20%，但动力学耦合部分抵消该效应（尤其对f模几乎完全抵消）；高于轴子质量的流体模可通过轴子辐射新通道衰减；f模始终低于该阈值，而p模衰减时标可短至~10 ms；还发现强阻尼的轴子主导准正规模（Im~kHz）及类引力原子的束缚态，且在传播阈值以下，束缚p模与这些轴子态发生避免交叉并交换主导成分。

Conclusion: 轴子凝聚显著重塑中子星非径向振荡谱，引入新的动力学耦合机制、衰减通道及量子化束缚结构，为通过引力波观测探测轴子提供理论依据和特征信号。

Abstract: In light QCD axion models, finite-density corrections to the axion potential can source a nonvanishing axion field inside neutron stars. Extending our previous analysis of radial oscillations, we study the nonradial ($l=2$) oscillation spectrum of such axion condensed neutron stars in the relativistic Cowling approximation, keeping the fluid and axion perturbations fully coupled and dynamical. The condensate modifies the fluid spectrum in two ways: the altered equilibrium structure raises the $f$- and $p$-mode frequencies by up to $\sim20\%$, while the dynamical coupling to the axion field partially compensates this shift, almost entirely for the $f$-mode. Fluid modes with frequencies above the axion mass acquire a new damping channel through axion emission. The $f$-mode remains below this threshold for all configurations supporting a condensate, whereas the $p$-modes are damped on timescales that can be as short as $\sim10$ ms, shorter than their gravitational-wave damping times, except for certain axion masses where the outgoing axion amplitude nearly vanishes. The spectrum also contains a family of strongly damped axion-dominated quasinormal modes, with imaginary parts of order kHz, and a sequence of gravitationally bound axion states analogous to gravitational atom levels. Below the propagation threshold, the bound $p$-modes undergo avoided crossings with these levels, exchanging their fluid- and axion-dominated character.

</details>


### [125] [Color-Reconnection Effects on the nMPI-Nch Conditional Multiplicity Fluctuations in PYTHIA 8 pp Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37511)
*Subir Sarkar,Kalyan Dey*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定多部分子相互作用（MPI）数量下，不同质心能量（2.76、7、13 TeV）质子-质子碰撞中带电粒子多重数涨落的条件行为，并考察了色重连接（CR）效应对涨落宽度（绝对与相对）的影响；发现CR开启时条件平均值减小比例大于标准差减小比例，即绝对宽度变小但变异系数变大，该现象在多种模型调参（CMS CP5、Monash、A14）中一致存在，但无法确认CR是唯一成因或其能量依赖性的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子对撞中色重连接（CR）机制对末态粒子产额和涨落的影响，尤其是其在不同MPI数下的条件行为及能量依赖性，以厘清CR在蒙特卡洛模型中的物理作用与建模可靠性。

Method: 基于PYTHIA生成的质子-质子碰撞事件样本（2.76/7/13 TeV），限定带电粒子|η|<0.8、0.15<pT<20 GeV/c，按真实MPI数分 bin 分析条件带电多重数涨落；对比CR-ON/OFF配置（含CMS CP5、Monash、A14等不同 tunes），并结合ALICE实验的⟨pT⟩数据与模拟活性估计器进行末态交叉验证。

Result: 在所有保留的MPI bin 中，CR-ON 配置下条件平均值的下降幅度大于标准差的下降幅度，导致绝对宽度减小而变异系数增大；该趋势在13 TeV CMS CP5 tune（CR严格ON/OFF对照）、Monash和A14样本中均重现；但因历史CR参数设置未标定且有效寿命/衰变设定未经验证，无法归因于CR本身或确立其能量依赖机制。

Conclusion: 观察到的绝对与相对涨落宽度的反向变化模式具有模型稳健性，提示其可能源于MPI与CR之外的共性建模特征或相空间约束；需更严格的CR参数控制与实验可观测量联合分析，才能确立CR对条件涨落的真实物理影响。

Abstract: We examine conditional charged-multiplicity fluctuations at fixed multiparton interaction (MPI) count in retained PYTHIA proton-proton samples at centre-of-mass energies of 2.76, 7, and 13 TeV. Charged particles satisfy |eta| < 0.8 and 0.15 < pT < 20 GeV/c. In the historical colour-reconnection (CR)-labelled baseline samples, the CR-ON conditional mean is smaller by a larger fraction than the standard deviation: the absolute width is smaller, while the coefficient of variation is larger in every retained matched MPI bin. Effective lifetime/decay settings and their per-run correspondence are unverified for this baseline, so these comparisons do not establish that CR alone causes the observed differences or their energy dependence. A separately documented 13 TeV comparison using the Compact Muon Solenoid (CMS) CP5 tune, with equal non-CR physics settings and particle data across the ON/OFF pair, exhibits the same absolute-versus-relative width ordering. Retained independent Monash and A14 samples also show this pattern, subject to historical production-record limitations. A comparison with mean-transverse-momentum data from A Large Ion Collider Experiment (ALICE) and simulated activity estimators provides final-state context within the stated particle-definition and provenance limits.

</details>


### [126] [Electron-to-Muon Forward Annihilation at High Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37531)
*Maximilian Delto,Alexander A. Penin,Lorenzo Tancredi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了QED中双轻子质量体系下电子-缪子正向湮灭的高能行为，利用有效场论重整化群方法推导了其散射振幅的全阶渐近行为（至次领头对数精度），并计算了一圈和两圈固定阶修正（含幂次修正），旨在补充先前预印本arXiv:2510.04914中省略的技术细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补充先前工作中省略的关键技术细节，完善高能极限下正向电子-缪子湮灭过程的理论描述。

Method: 采用有效场论（EFT）与重整化群（RG）技术推导全阶渐近行为（至NLL精度）；同时在高能极限下直接计算一圈和两圈固定阶费曼图修正，并保留幂次修正项。

Result: 得到了正向电子-缪子湮灭振幅的完整次领头对数（NLL）渐近形式，并给出了精确到幂次修正的一圈和两圈固定阶结果。

Conclusion: 该工作系统建立了双质量轻子体系中正向湮灭过程的高能渐近理论框架，为高精度QED计算和未来高能实验中的背景分析提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We study the high-energy behavior of electron-to-muon annihilation in forward kinematics in QED with two massive lepton species. Using effective field theory renormalization group techniques we derive the all-order asymptotic behavior of the annihilation amplitude through the next-to-leading logarithmic approximation. Furthermore, we consider one- and two-loop fixed-order corrections to the forward-annihilation amplitude in the high-energy limit, which we compute up to power corrections. The goal of this paper is to elaborate on various technical details that we omitted in the original presentation of our results in arXiv:2510.04914.

</details>


### [127] [On-shell Mass Renormalisation and RG-improved Effective Potential in Vector-like Lepton Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37541)
*Gregor Daberstiel,Kilian Möhling,Dominik Stöckinger,Hyejung Stöckinger-Kim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了六种向量型轻子（VLL）扩展标准模型中的壳质量重整化方案，该方案能吸收由SM与VLL混合引起的大辐射修正，并支持高效计算可观测量及低能尺度下的MS-bar参数；同时推导并求解了无冗余的两圈重整化群方程，计算了RG改进的有效势，并分析了各VLL模型的真空不稳定性标度，发现存在低至10^5 GeV的不稳定性标度区域以及一阶修正可使真空稳定的参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决向量型轻子扩展模型中因SM与VLL混合导致的SM轻子质量大辐射修正问题，提升低能参数计算与真空稳定性分析的精度和效率。

Method: 采用壳质量重整化方案处理最多5×5的混合矩阵；推导并求解冗余自由的两圈重整化群方程；计算RG改进的有效势；系统分析各VLL模型的真空不稳定性标度与相行为。

Result: 发现了丰富的相行为；识别出真空不稳定性标度可低至10^5 GeV的参数区域；也发现某些区域中一阶修正足以维持真空稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的壳质量重整化方案适用于多维VLL模型，显著改善了低能可观测量与RG演化计算的可靠性；真空稳定性分析揭示了模型参数空间中关键的稳定/不稳定区域，对新物理能标与实验探测具有指导意义。

Abstract: We discuss the on-shell mass renormalisation in a class of six vector-like lepton (VLL) extensions of the Standard Model with seesaw-like contributions to the SM lepton masses. The schemes absorb large radiative corrections to the SM lepton masses arising from mixing between the SM and VLLs with up to $5\times5$ mixing matrices and allows for efficient computation of observables and the low-scale $\overline{\text{MS}}$ values of model parameters. We determine and solve the redundancy-free 2-loop renormalisation-group (RG) equations, compute the RG-improved effective potential and study the vacuum instability scales of each of the VLL models. We find rich phase behaviour and parameter regions that show instability scales as low as $10^5$ GeV as well as regions where the one-loop corrections to the input parameters can lead to a stable vacuum.

</details>


### [128] [Using Higgs Boson Production in Association with Three Jets via Gluon Fusion at the LHC to Investigate the CP Properties of the Higgs Boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37627)
*Arunima Bhattacharya,Francisco Campanario,Michael Kubocz,Terrance Figy,Tunde Kushimo,Ivan Rosario*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了在13.6 TeV LHC上，中性Higgs玻色子Φ与三喷注（pp→Φjjj）联合产生的完整质量依赖的领头阶强子截面和运动学分布，包含顶夸克和底夸克圈图的相干干涉；发现夸克参与过程对赝标量三喷注截面贡献近一半；底夸克环在2HDM-II模型中随tanβ增大而主导，导致截面非单调；有效理论在横动量和二喷注质量分布高尾失效，但第三喷注中心度z*和前两喷注快度差较鲁棒；多喷注方位角关联Φ₂受底夸克贡献及CP混合干涉调制，sin2Φ₂项可作为CP混合的新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测通用中性Higgs（含标量/赝标量混合耦合）与多喷注联合产生过程，尤其在底夸克主导区域（如大tanβ的2HDM），需超越重顶夸克有效理论，保留完整夸克质量效应与干涉；同时探索新CP敏感观测量以区分CP本征态与混合态。

Method: 基于QCD微扰理论，在α_s^5阶下计算pp→Φjjj的完整质量依赖截面，包含gg/gq/q\bar{q}等全部初态部分子过程；严格处理顶、底夸克圈图的相干干涉；对比重顶有效理论（HEFT）及其形式因子改进版本（FF-HEFT）；构造并分析新型多喷注方位角关联观测量Φ₂及其傅里叶分量。

Result: 1）夸克初态通道贡献约50%赝标量Φ+3j截面（tanβ=1）；2）2HDM-II中Φ+3j截面随tanβ增大先升后降，呈非单调行为；3）HEFT在p_T^Φ和m_jj高尾显著失效，z*和Δy_{jj}则稳健；4）Φ₂分布受底夸克环及CP混合干涉影响，出现独特sin2Φ₂调制项；5）提出基于Φ₂的简单计数不对称性，可干净提取该CP-odd信号。

Conclusion: 完整夸克质量效应和干涉对Φ+多喷注物理至关重要，尤其在大tanβ区域；Φ₂及其sin2Φ₂分量是探测Higgs CP性质（特别是CP混合）的强有力新工具，建议LHC实验开展相关测量。

Abstract: A neutral Higgs boson $Φ$ with general scalar and pseudoscalar couplings and accompanying jets can be efficiently produced via gluon fusion through heavy-quark loops in high-energy hadronic collisions. We present integrated cross sections and kinematic distributions for the production of a generic Higgs boson $Φ$ in association with three jets, $pp \to Φjjj$, at order $α_{s}^{5}$. We include the full mass dependence of the top- and bottom-quark loop contributions, combined coherently so that their interference is retained. Earlier finite-mass calculations of $Φ+3$ jets with bottom-quark loops and $CP$-odd or $CP$-mixed couplings were restricted to $gg \to gggΦ$; we include the complete set of partonic subprocesses, and find that the quark-containing channels supply close to one half of the pseudoscalar three-jet cross section at $\tanβ=1$. In a Type-II two-Higgs-doublet model the bottom-quark loop comes to dominate the rate as $\tanβ$ is increased, so that the cross section is non-monotonic in $\tanβ$. Comparing against the heavy-top effective theory and its form-factor-improved version, we show that the effective description fails in the hard tails of the transverse-momentum and dijet-mass distributions, while the third-jet centrality $z^\star$ and the rapidity separation of the two leading jets are largely insensitive to the heavy-quark treatment. We further study the multi-jet azimuthal correlation $Φ_{2}$, built from the jet momenta on the two sides of the Higgs boson in rapidity, and find that the bottom-quark contribution modifies its shape even for a state of definite $CP$. For a $CP$-mixed state the interference of the two couplings adds a $\sin2Φ_2$ term, which a simple counting asymmetry isolates and which no $CP$ eigenstate can produce. Numerical results are presented for the LHC at $\sqrt{s}=13.6~$TeV and $m_Φ=125.2~$GeV.

</details>
