### [129] [Closure of Traceful Radiation-Gauge Reconstruction with Matter: General Kerr Conditions, a Thin-Shell Benchmark, and a Conserved Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35938)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kerr背景下的首阶、迹辐射规范度规重构的闭合问题，将其分解为Weyl曲率、Ricci迹和质量-角动量补全部分，并建立了局部存在唯一性；通过薄壳与流体环两个例子验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Kerr黑洞背景下，为含物质的引力波建模提供严格、可计算的度规重构框架，解决辐射规范下迹类度规重建的闭合性问题。

Method: 将度规重构分解为Weyl曲率、Ricci迹和质量-角动量补全部分，构建三角形输运系统；结合初始条件、规范选择与补全数据，在正则Kerr区域上分析其局部适定性，并检验两类具体物质源（薄球壳与稳态流体环）。

Result: 证明了兼容数据下三角输运系统的局部存在唯一性；对Schwarzschild薄壳，导出ADM质量修正为√(1−2M/R)倍静止质量，轴向部分决定角动量、转动惯量和内禀参考系拖曳；对Kerr上的恒角动量流体环，计算其Newman–Penrose Ricci投影，揭示非球对称源激发耦合Ricci分量。

Conclusion: 该框架为Kerr背景中含物质的辐射规范度规重构提供了结构化路径；薄壳构成封闭基准，流体环凸显一般Kerr源所需的额外耦合结构；但未实现全局存在性证明。

Abstract: We formulate the closure problem for first-order, traceful radiation-gauge metric reconstruction on a Kerr background with matter. The reconstruction is decomposed into Weyl-curvature, Ricci-trace, and mass--angular-momentum completion sectors. For compatible initial, gauge, and completion data, we establish local existence and uniqueness of the resulting triangular transport system on a regular Kerr domain, and identify the additional conditions needed for a closed global formulation. This is not a global existence theorem. We test the framework using two conserved matter sources. For a thin shell around a Schwarzschild black hole, Israel matching, regularity, asymptotic flatness, a barotropic equation of state, and fixed rest mass determine the static, axial, and leading rotational sectors. The ADM-mass shift is $\sqrt{1-2M/R}$ times the shell rest mass, while the axial sector determines the angular momentum, moment of inertia, and interior frame dragging. We also construct a stationary constant-angular-momentum fluid torus on Kerr and compute its Newman--Penrose Ricci projections, showing that nonspherical sources generally excite coupled Ricci sectors. The shell provides a closed benchmark, while the torus illustrates the additional structure required for general Kerr sources.

</details>


### [130] [Can a Wormhole Induce a Monopole?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35969)
*Sergei Slobodov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出，围绕虫洞一端运行的恒星并不会导致另一端观测到的质量发生周期性变化；此前针对银河系中心Sgr A*提出的此类信号源于对静态解的误用，而实际上，在广义相对论框架下，源运动在首阶近似下不会改变观测到的史瓦西质量，邦迪质量亦无法增加（仅可因引力辐射而减少）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清关于虫洞两端质量观测是否受轨道天体影响的误解，特别是回应此前针对Sgr A*提出的周期性质量变化搜寻方案。

Method: 通过类比电磁学（高斯定律与电流关系）分析引力中的质量定义，结合史瓦西质量与邦迪质量的概念，从广义相对论基本原理出发进行理论推导与物理诠释。

Result: 证明轨道恒星运动不会引起虫洞另一端观测质量的一阶变化；邦迪质量在真空观测端不能增加，仅可能因引力辐射而减小；远距离双口可等效为单外部时空。

Conclusion: 先前提出的基于虫洞两端质量周期性变化的观测信号在理论上不成立，其根源在于混淆了不同质量参数的物理含义及动力学演化条件。

Abstract: Could a star orbiting one mouth of a wormhole make the mass seen near the other rise and fall? We show that the periodic mass change proposed for searches around Sgr A* does not occur. The predicted signal comes from treating static solutions with different masses as successive states of the same orbiting system. In the Maxwell analogue, charge measured around a mouth changes only when current crosses the measuring surface. Similarly, in gravity, source motion cannot change the observed Schwarzschild mass at first order. Furthermore, the Bondi mass of a vacuum observer end cannot increase even beyond first order, though gravitational radiation can reduce it. The same observer-side dynamics can be reproduced with both mouths in one exterior at sufficiently large separation.

</details>


### [131] [First-order corrections to equatorial periodic orbits around rapidly rotating black holes in dynamical Chern--Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35983)
*Hao-Jie Lin,Tao Zhu,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了动态Chern-Simons（dCS）引力中快速旋转黑洞周围赤道周期轨道的能量、径向周期和周向转向半径的一阶修正，并分析了这些修正对引力波形相位累积和谱线频率偏移的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强场极限下的有效性，通过研究dCS修正引力理论中黑洞周围轨道运动的可观测偏差，为引力波天文学提供理论基准。

Method: 基于dCS引力中解析拟合的度规修正，将轨道与固定无量纲自旋、比角动量和有理周期性的Kerr参考轨道匹配；结合直接贡献与能量补偿机制分析周期修正；构建数值杂凑（numerical kludge）波形并分析其离散谱。

Result: 发现周期一阶修正存在符号变化，其主导机制在顺行与逆行轨道间不同；定位到周期修正系数C_T=0的边界；周向位移符号可与坐标位移相反；相位修正在一阶下等效于Kerr质量重标度，但在C_T=0轨道上该相位贡献被抑制。

Conclusion: dCS引力中的轨道动力学修正具有丰富的结构特征，尤其在周期和相位响应上呈现非平凡依赖关系，为未来高精度引力波观测区分替代理论提供了关键诊断依据。

Abstract: We calculate first-order corrections to the energy, radial period, and circumferential turning radii of equatorial periodic orbits around rapidly rotating black holes in dynamical Chern--Simons (dCS) gravity. Using an analytic fit to the metric correction, we match each orbit to a Kerr reference at fixed dimensionless spin, dimensionless specific angular momentum, and rational periodicity. The period correction combines a direct contribution at fixed energy with compensation from the energy adjustment that preserves periodicity. Their competition produces sign changes across orbital families and reference paths. In a two-parameter scan, negative period corrections are dominated by energy compensation for sampled prograde orbits and by the direct contribution for sampled retrograde orbits. We locate $C_T=0$ boundaries, where the coefficient of the first-order fractional period correction vanishes. Circumferential shifts can differ in sign from coordinate shifts. Numerical kludge waveforms at leading quadrupole order and their discrete spectra connect these corrections to phase accumulation and harmonic frequency shifts. At fixed dimensionless orbital parameters and physical mass of the small body, this phase contribution can be reproduced to first order by rescaling the Kerr mass. For the $C_T=0$ orbit examined here, this contribution is suppressed at equal black hole masses, while corrections to the waveform and spectral amplitudes remain nonzero.

</details>


### [132] [Attractors and initial-condition sensitivity in Horndeski cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36036)
*Yulia A. Ageeva,Andrey K. Sharofeev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了邻近的均匀宇宙学历史是否趋近或远离Genesis和收缩解，并探讨了后期标量速度的不确定性如何影响初始速度的推断。结果表明，即使在扰动不稳定的背景下，均匀解仍可能收敛；因此，背景吸引性不足以证明宇宙学解的物理可行性。在收缩模型中，一个幂律解吸引邻近轨迹，另一个则排斥，其强度依赖于宇宙状态的参数化方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Genesis和收缩宇宙学模型中背景解的稳定性与物理可行性，特别是均匀解的收敛性与初始条件敏感性之间的关系。

Method: 通过分析均匀宇宙学历史的动力学行为，研究Genesis和收缩模型中背景解的吸引/排斥特性，并考察标量速度不确定性对初始速度反演的影响。

Result: 发现Genesis模型中引入引力动力学会显著改变速度关系；即使在扰动不稳定的背景下，均匀解仍可收敛；收缩模型中存在一个吸引性和一个排斥性的幂律解，其强度依赖于参数化方式。

Conclusion: 背景解的吸引性并非物理可行性的充分判据；Genesis和收缩模型的初始条件敏感性及动力学稳定性需结合具体参数化和完整演化（如反弹）进一步分析。

Abstract: We study whether nearby homogeneous cosmological histories approach or move away from the Genesis and contracting solutions. We also determine how an uncertainty in the scalar velocity at a later stage affects the inferred initial velocity. In the Genesis models, including the gravitational dynamics can substantially change this relation. We find that convergence of homogeneous solutions can occur even around backgrounds with unstable perturbations, and therefore attraction of the background is not sufficient to establish that a cosmological solution is physically viable. In the contracting model, one power-law solution attracts nearby trajectories, whereas another repels them. The strength of these effects depends on how the cosmological state is parametrized. Their evolution through the subsequent bounce is not considered here.

</details>


### [133] [Semi-agnostic inspiral modeling: the massless scalar case at leading post-Newtonian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36037)
*Dario Rossi,Leonardo Gualtieri,Thomas P. Sotiriou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半不可知的黑洞双星旋近建模框架，不预设具体引力理论，而是通过后牛顿展开和l/L（偏离广义相对论的特征长度与系统特征长度之比）展开，聚焦于质量为零的标量场与度规耦合的情形，在领头阶后牛顿近似下导出仅含少量普适参数的 inspiral 描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在广义相对论之外对黑洞双星旋近进行建模，避免对特定替代理论的先验假设，同时兼顾可观测性和普适性约束能力。

Method: 采用半不可知建模策略，固定场内容（如引入无质量标量场），在后牛顿近似和小参数l/L下展开；系统分析相互作用项与源特征量的尺度依赖性。

Result: 发现旋近过程可由少量普适参数完全刻画，该参数化形式适用于多源联合约束及针对特定新场的搜寻。

Conclusion: 该框架为超越广义相对论的引力波数据分析提供了灵活、通用且可观测的理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a semi-agnostic modeling framework for black hole binary inspirals beyond general relativity that fixes the field content without selecting a particular theory. We expand in the post-Newtonian order and in powers of $l/L$, where $l$ is the characteristic length-scale that controls deviations from General Relativity and $L$ is the characteristic length-scale of the system. We focus on the case of a massless scalar field coupled with the metric, at leading post-Newtonian order. Taking into account the scaling with $l/L$ of both the interaction terms and the quantities that characterise the sources, we find that the inspiral can be described in terms of a small number of universal parameters. Such a parametrisation is suitable for obtaining combined bounds from multiple sources and performing searches for specific fields.

</details>


### [134] [Fresh Look at Shift Symmetric Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36063)
*Rodrigo Calderon,Eric V. Linder*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有平移对称性的Horndeski引力理论可以穿越幽灵分界（w=-1），并揭示了两种保持平移对称性下实现该穿越的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统观点——认为平移对称Horndeski引力无法穿越幽灵分界（w=-1），探索其量子修正鲁棒性与宇宙学演化可能性。

Method: 结合解析推导与数值模拟，开展动力学系统分析，刻画无鬼、稳定区域的演化，识别不动点与吸引子结构，并研究w=-1曲线与相空间结构的相互作用。

Result: 严格证明平移对称Horndeski理论可穿越w=-1；明确两类穿越机制；刻画了模型族中稳定与无鬼区域的动力学演化及相空间结构。

Conclusion: 平移对称Horndeski引力不仅是量子稳健的简单理论，还可自然支持穿越幽灵分界的宇宙学演化，拓展了暗能量模型的理论可行性。

Abstract: Shift symmetric Horndeski gravity can be a simple, well posed theory with protection against quantum corrections. Overturning conventional thought that such a theory cannot cross the phantom divide ($w=-1$) we present both analytic and numerical proof that it can. We carry out a detailed dynamical systems analysis showing the evolution of ghost free and stable regions within the model family, identify the fixed point and dynamical attractor structure, and derive the interaction of the $w=-1$ curve with these phase space structures. This reveals two mechanisms for cosmologies crossing the phantom divide while preserving shift symmetry.

</details>


### [135] [Scalarized Einstein-Maxwell black holes in external scalar fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36085)
*Lang Cheng,Guangzhou Guo,Yuhan Li,Peng Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论中在给定外部标量场下的静态黑洞解，分析了其存在域及标量荷随外部场的变化规律，并探讨了双黑洞系统中由伴星激发的标量场导致的相互标量感应现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解外部标量场如何影响黑洞的标量荷及其存在条件，并探究双黑洞系统中因标量场耦合引发的非线性相互作用与平衡行为。

Method: 构建Einstein-Maxwell-scalar理论中受控外部标量场下的静态黑洞解；数值映射存在域；分析标量荷对不同符号外部场的响应；通过领头阶伴星激发标量场建模双黑洞间的标量感应。

Result: 正外部场可使低于零源标量化阈值的RN黑洞获得正标量荷并扩展其电荷-质量允许范围；负外部场引入有限下界并允许多重解；双黑洞间存在相互标量感应，同号电荷时标量荷未必随间距减小而增大，异号电荷时可能出现多平衡态且原平衡支无法连续延拓至更小间距。

Conclusion: 外部标量场显著调控黑洞标量性质与存在性，双黑洞系统的标量相互作用展现出丰富非线性动力学行为，挑战了基于孤立黑洞的简单外推预期。

Abstract: We construct static black holes in Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory under prescribed external scalar fields, mapping their existence domain and determining how their scalar charges vary with the external field. Focusing on solutions with positive scalar charge, we find that a positive external field induces scalar charge in Reissner-Nordström (RN) black holes below the zero-source scalarization threshold and extends the allowed charge-to-mass range. A negative external field instead introduces a finite lower existence boundary and permits distinct black holes under identical external conditions. Coupling these isolated responses through the leading companion-generated scalar fields, we find mutual scalar induction in binaries whose components are RN black holes in isolation. For binaries with scalar charges of the same sign, their scalar charges need not increase as the separation decreases, while some binaries with opposite scalar charges admit multiple equilibria and cannot continue along their original equilibrium branches to smaller separations.

</details>


### [136] [A gaussian-process reconstruction of gravitational-wave friction from dark standard sirens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36087)
*Michele Mancarella,Viola De Renzis,Alessandro Agapito*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用暗标准汽笛（dark standard sirens）通过高斯过程非参数重建引力波传播中异常摩擦项α_M(z)的方法，并结合GWTC-5.0数据检验广义相对论，结果未发现偏离GR的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在宇宙学尺度上对引力波传播的预言，尤其关注扩展引力理论中可能出现的异常摩擦项导致的振幅演化改变及引力波与电磁波光度距离差异。

Method: 采用高斯过程（GP）非参数重建红移依赖的摩擦函数α_M(z)，通过积分得到GW与EM光度距离比Ξ(z)，同时约束GW光度距离单调可逆；联合推断源种群、修正传播效应和背景宇宙学参数；引入函数空间诊断工具（如先验校准、主成分分析等）评估重建可靠性与数据驱动性。

Result: 基于GWTC-5.0数据，α_M=0与Ξ=1在所覆盖红移范围内与后验分布一致，未发现偏离广义相对论的证据；结果对GP核函数、先验设定、分辨率及似然处理变化稳定。

Conclusion: 当前引力波数据支持广义相对论关于引力波传播的预言；所提非参数框架及诊断工具为可解释的引力波传播检验提供了新范式。

Abstract: Standard sirens provide a direct probe of the cosmic distance-redshift relation and therefore a powerful test of whether gravitational waves (GWs) propagate as predicted by general relativity on cosmological scales. In many extensions of general relativity, GW propagation is modified by an anomalous friction term, which alters the amplitude evolution and leads to a GW luminosity distance that differs from its electromagnetic counterpart. In this work, we develop a non-parametric reconstruction of this anomalous friction from dark standard sirens. We reconstruct its redshift dependence, encoded in the friction function $α_M(z)$, with a Gaussian process (GP) and derive the corresponding GW-to-EM luminosity-distance ratio $Ξ(z)$ by integration, explicitly retaining the relation between the local friction modification and its accumulated effect on the observed distance, while enforcing a monotonic and invertible GW luminosity distance. We apply the method to the GWTC-5.0 catalog, jointly inferring the source population, modified propagation, and background cosmological parameters. We find no evidence for departures from GR, with $α_M=0$ and $Ξ=1$ consistent with the posterior over the redshift range probed by the catalog. The result is stable under variations of the GP kernel, smoothness and amplitude priors, resolution prescription, and likelihood treatment. We further introduce function-space diagnostics for comparing flexible and parametric reconstructions, including prior calibration, prior-to-posterior contraction, common summaries of propagation-population degeneracies, and principal-component analysis. These tools make explicit which features are informed by the data and which are imposed by the function-space prior, providing a framework for interpretable non-parametric tests of gravitational-wave propagation.

</details>


### [137] [Improved Gauge Conditions for Large Expansion Cosmological Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36123)
*Fan Zhang,Lee Lindblom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的规范条件，用于在具有非平凡空间拓扑的宇宙学时空上长时间数值求解爱因斯坦方程，显著提升了空间体积演化能力（超百万倍），并在三种典型非平凡拓扑流形上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法难以在具有非平凡空间拓扑的宇宙学时空中进行长时间、大尺度的爱因斯坦方程演化，限制了对相关宇宙模型的研究。

Method: 提出一种新的规范条件，并在第六转空间、透镜空间L(5,2)和Seifert纤维空间SFS[S2:(2,1)(2,1)(2,-1)]等具有非平凡拓扑的流形上，对均匀各向同性初值进行数值演化测试。

Result: 新规范条件成功支持空间体积扩大超一百万倍的稳定、可靠数值演化。

Conclusion: 该新规范条件为研究具有非平凡空间拓扑的宇宙学模型提供了有效的数值工具，拓展了广义相对论数值模拟的应用范围。

Abstract: This study develops a new gauge condition for performing long duration numerical evolutions of Einstein's equation on cosmological spacetimes with non-trivial spatial topologies. This new gauge makes it possible to perform reliable numerical evolutions on these manifolds in which the spatial volumes become more than one million times larger. This new condition was tested for this study by evolving homogeneous and isotropic initial data on exemplar manifolds with three spatial topologies: Sixth-Turn space, the lens space L(5,2), and the Seifert fiber space SFS[S2:(2,1)(2,1)(2,-1)].

</details>


### [138] [Unified Dynamics of Black-Hole Interiors: From Geometric to Scalar-Kinetic Dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36129)
*Fu-Guo Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的动力学图像，将事件视界处的小振幅标量输入与标量-Kasner内部结构联系起来，揭示了面积收缩在几何项与标量动能项主导性转换中的核心作用，并通过数值模拟验证了该机制在多种黑洞解（如Schwarzschild、Kerr–Newman等）中的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内时空奇点附近的动力学行为，特别是标量场如何影响内视界稳定性及奇点结构，是广义相对论与量子引力交叉的关键问题。

Method: 基于边界支撑的定态解族，结合解析分析与全耦合数值模拟（包括四维定态轴对称Kerr–Newman内部解），研究面积收缩对标量梯度和能量项相对强度的影响。

Result: 发现面积收缩是驱动几何项向标量动能项过渡的共同物理机制；在Schwarzschild-like分支中抑制曲率贡献，在Kerr–Newman-like分支中强烈放大标量梯度并导致局域化跃迁；动能与几何项抵消产生度规诊断量的正极小值，动态解释内视界消失。

Conclusion: 面积收缩是连接不同黑洞内部标量动力学的普适机制，而‘准柯西视界零点’决定了梯度放大强度与跃迁定位，为黑洞内奇点普适性提供了新视角。

Abstract: We identify a unified dynamical picture connecting small-amplitude scalar input at the event horizon to scalar--Kasner interiors. Within boundary-supported stationary families, area contraction drives the transition from geometric to scalar kinetic dominance. For the Schwarzschild-like branch, contraction suppresses the relative curvature contribution, so the scalar kinetic term becomes dominant while the gradient remains small. For the Kerr--Newman-like branch, the scalar gradient is strongly amplified as area contraction carries the solution toward the would-be Cauchy-horizon zero, followed by a narrow transition to scalar kinetic dominance. Cancellation between the kinetic and geometric terms produces a positive minimum of a metric diagnostic, explaining inner-horizon loss dynamically. Fully coupled numerical solutions for the Schwarzschild-like branch and four-dimensional stationary axisymmetric Kerr--Newman interiors, including the Reissner--Nordström and Kerr limits, support this picture. Area contraction provides the common physical mechanism, while the would-be Cauchy-horizon zero determines the strength of gradient amplification and the localization of the transition.

</details>


### [139] [Non-Perturbative Approach to Gravitational Particle Production in the Inflation-Kination Transition: Surveying a Wide Range of the Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36137)
*Amirsalar Nikandish,Shiva Rostam Zadeh,Nematollah Riazi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在暴胀-动能主导背景下的引力粒子产生（GPP）作为自然再加热机制，通过改进的非微扰方法分析了标量场质量、引力耦合常数和相变时间三个参数对再加热温度的影响，并数值计算得到足够高的再加热温度以开启热大爆炸时代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽考察了超重标量场质量对再加热温度的影响，但未考虑非共形引力耦合常数对GPP产率的影响；同时需在保持模型普适性前提下分析暴胀向kination过渡背景中的引力再加热。

Method: 采用连续尺度因子函数建模暴胀-kination背景；结合微扰与基于绝热真空概念的非微扰方法，并对其进行修正以同时刻画场质量与非共形引力耦合常数的影响；辅以数值计算与一个解析可解的平行模型作为数学工具。

Result: 识别出影响再加热温度的三个关键参数（场裸质量、引力耦合常数、相变时间），系统分析其单独及联合效应；在三维参数空间中搜寻到最高可能的再加热温度，数值结果表明其足以使宇宙进入热大爆炸时期。

Conclusion: 引力粒子产生可在暴胀-kination背景下高效实现再加热；非共形引力耦合显著影响GPP产率；该机制为无需引入额外相互作用的自然再加热提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate the gravitational particle production (GPP) as a natural reheating channel in time-dependent backgrounds. We focus on reheating in an inflation-kination background. To maintain generality across inflationary models, we use a continuous scale-factor function that produces this transition and has been proposed in the literature. In this scenario, the gravitational reheating channel arises from the gravitational excitation of a superheavy spectator scalar field with Ricci-matter gravitational coupling. Besides all the perturbative approaches, a recently proposed non-perturbative one that uses the notion of the adiabatic vacuum has also been applied to the case of superheavy particles. Although the effect of the mass of the field on the reheating temperature has been investigated, the impact of the non-conformal values of the gravitational coupling constant on the GPP rate is not considered. Hence, we modify the non-perturbative approach in such a way that both effects can be traced. Three parameters affect the reheating temperature in this case: the bare mass of the field, the gravitational coupling constant, and the transition time. In this paper, the effect of each of these parameters alone on the resultant reheating temperature, as well as the combined effects of these parameters, is studied. Taking into account all involved parameters and surveying a wide range of the 3D parameter space, the highest reheating temperature possible is obtained. All of the aforementioned investigations are done based on numerical calculations, and we obtain that the highest possible reheating temperature is quite sufficient for the universe to enter the Hot Big-Bang era. For the sake of completeness, we study the GPP in such a transition using an analytical method in an independent, parallel, solvable model. This analysis is presented only as a mathematical tool.

</details>


### [140] [On the Interpretation of Tests of the Horizon Area Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36162)
*Selma Vangstein,Christopher J. Moore*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了引力波对黑洞视界面积定律的检验，指出这些检验依赖于广义相对论（GR）模型，因此具有模型依赖性；通过模拟玻色子星并合，作者证明无事件视界的奇异致密天体仍可高度拟合面积定律（置信度达~92%），从而质疑此类检验对GR的验证效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力波对黑洞视界面积定律的检验虽能验证广义相对论，但其模型本身预设了广义相对论成立，导致结果解释存在内在模型依赖性，需检验其是否真正具有区分GR与非GR致密天体的能力。

Method: 采用玻色子星并合的数值模拟作为具体例子，分析其引力波信号在面积定律检验中的表现，并计算其通过该检验的置信水平。

Result: 玻色子星（无事件视界）的并合信号可在面积定律检验中达到约92%的置信度，成功‘通过’检验，表明该检验无法可靠区分真实黑洞与某些奇异致密天体。

Conclusion: 基于广义相对论模型的面积定律检验本质上模型依赖，不能作为对广义相对论的独立、强约束检验；需发展更普适、模型无关的引力波检验方法。

Abstract: This Letter investigates gravitational-wave tests of the horizon area law. Although they provide a striking consistency check of general relativity (GR), these tests rely on models that assume GR, making their interpretation inherently model dependent. We show with a specific example---simulations of merging boson stars---that exotic compact objects can mimic merging black holes and pass an area-law test at a confidence as high as $\sim 92\%$ despite not even having event horizons.

</details>


### [141] [The Symbiosis of Vainshtein and Chameleon Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36166)
*Sergi Sirera,Tessa Baker,Clare Burrage,James Hallam,Krishna Naidoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了宇宙结构中Vainshtein与Chameleon屏蔽机制共存的现象，揭示了二者相互作用对第五力屏蔽、结构形成及引力宇宙学检验的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实的暗能量模型可能同时激活多种屏蔽机制，但其相互作用此前被忽视；需理解多机制共存对宇宙结构和引力检验的影响。

Method: 理论分析与模型比较，研究Vainshtein与Chameleon机制在宇宙结构中的耦合效应及其对屏蔽半径和结构形态的影响。

Result: 发现Chameleon机制可解决Galileon模型长期存在的空洞病态问题；抑制或消除Vainshtein半径；Vainshtein项则压缩Chameleon的屏蔽核心。

Conclusion: Vainshtein与Chameleon屏蔽的共存显著重塑引力修改模型的可观测特征，对宇宙学引力检验具有根本性意义。

Abstract: We present the first study of coexisting Vainshtein and Chameleon screening in cosmic structures. Screening hides fifth forces locally, keeping modified gravity viable. Realistic dark-energy models can activate several mechanisms at once, yet this interplay has been overlooked. We find that (i) the Chameleon resolves the long-standing Galileon void pathology; (ii) it suppresses or eliminates the Vainshtein radius around massive sources; and (iii) the Vainshtein term shrinks the Chameleon's screened core. These effects reshape the observable signatures central to cosmological tests of gravity.

</details>


### [142] [Testing Conformal Weyl Gravity with Plasma Modified Black Hole Shadows and EHT Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36562)
*Zhichen Guan,Connor McMillin,Owen Gartlan,William Horner,Leo Rodriguez,Shanshan Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四阶共形Weyl引力（CWG）中带电旋转de Sitter黑洞在等离子体环境中的阴影特征，旨在区分等离子体色散效应与引力理论本身的观测效应；发现等离子体增强会缩小阴影半径，而电荷增强在CWG中扩大阴影、在广义相对论（GR）中缩小阴影，从而为区分CWG与环境效应提供潜在观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分由等离子体环境引起的观测效应与由底层引力理论（如共形Weyl引力）引起的效应，以识别真正的理论特征。

Method: 基于可分离的Hamilton-Jacobi等离子体模型，推导光子径向与角向势、赤道有限距离偏转角及有限观测者处的阴影边界；分析CWG时空下阴影半径、偏转角和能谱峰值对等离子体参数和电荷参数的依赖关系，并与EHT对Sgr A*和M87*的观测尺寸区间比较。

Result: 等离子体增强普遍减小阴影半径、有符号偏转角和能量辐射峰；电荷q在CWG中使阴影增大，在GR中使阴影减小；透镜行为与光子区及阴影观测量一致；CWG中电荷响应与等离子体响应方向相反，可作为区分理论与环境效应的观测依据。

Conclusion: 等离子体效应可部分模拟阴影尺寸变化，但CWG中电荷对阴影的独特响应提供了分离共形引力信号与传播环境效应的潜在观测途径。

Abstract: We study the shadows of a charged rotating de Sitter solution in fourth order conformal Weyl gravity (CWG) immersed in plasma and analyze the joint charge-plasma constraints. Our main goal is to distinguish observational effects caused by the dispersive plasma environment from those caused by the underlying gravitational theory. Within a separable Hamilton-Jacobi plasma model, we derive the radial and angular photon potentials, the equatorial finite-distance deflection angle, and the finite-observer shadow boundary due to the asymptotically non-flat nature of the CWG spacetime. Our results separate two effects. Increasing the plasma strength generally reduces the shadow radius, the signed deflection angle, and the energy emission peak in both CWG and GR. Charge ($q$) produces a stronger theory dependence: for the representative parameters studied, increasing $q$ enlarges the CWG shadow but reduces the GR shadow. The lensing behavior independently supports the geometry-dependent response found from the photon region and shadow observables. We further compare the finite observer dimensionless shadow radius with EHT-inferred size intervals for Sgr~A$^*$ and M87$^*$. Our results indicate that plasma effects can mimic part of the shadow size shift, but the distinct charge response of CWG offers a potential observational route for separating conformal gravity signatures from environmental propagation effects.

</details>


### [143] [Reconstruction of the Riemann tensor from causal order and element counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36695)
*Nathan Madsen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在因果集理论框架下，从离散的因果序和元素计数中重建连续时空几何量（如法坐标、平行移动和黎曼曲率张量）的方法，并在给定维度和采样密度条件下，证明了曲率误差界随采样密度增加而趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将因果集理论这一离散时空模型与广义相对论的连续几何描述相联系，需从离散结构中恢复连续时空的几何量并控制离散化引入的误差。

Method: 在给定维度和采样密度的前提下，构建近似法坐标、估计平行移动，并仅利用因果序和元素计数来恢复完整的黎曼张量；对嵌入固定光滑时空且满足均匀计数假设的样本进行分析。

Result: 证明了在所述假设下，曲率估计具有统一的误差界，且该误差界随采样密度增加而一致趋于零。

Conclusion: 因果集理论具备从纯序和计数信息中稳健恢复连续时空局部几何的能力，为离散量子引力提供了坚实的几何基础。

Abstract: A causal set is a locally finite partially ordered set. Causal set theory proposes such discrete structures as a basis for spacetime physics. Connecting this description with continuum physics requires recovering the geometric quantities used in general relativity and controlling the errors introduced by discreteness. Here, with dimension and sampling density specified, we construct approximate normal coordinates, estimate parallel transport, and recover the full Riemann tensor from causal order and element counts. For samples embedded in a fixed smooth spacetime and satisfying stated uniform counting assumptions, we prove a uniform curvature error bound that tends to zero as the sampling density increases.

</details>


### [144] [Improved constraints on higher-derivative effective field theory extensions of general relativity with GW230627_015337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36728)
*Tao Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA引力波数据，结合SEOBNRv5HM_ROM波形模型与有效场论（EFT）框架，对三种高阶曲率修正（两个三次、一个四次）进行参数估计，结果支持广义相对论，给出特征长度尺度的90%单边上限为11.1–18.3 km。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用双黑洞并合的引力波观测，在强场区域检验广义相对论；通过引入高阶曲率耦合的有效场论扩展，探测可能的偏离GR的新物理。

Method: 在SEOBNRv5HM_ROM波形中嵌入三种EFT高曲率修正（分别对应5PN、6PN、8PN相位修正），采用Fisher矩阵预估约束能力，并对最敏感事件GW230627_015337开展全贝叶斯分析；使用多极矩和完整IMR信号，结合灵活的理论无关框架。

Result: 所有三个EFT耦合在90%置信度下与零一致；对应特征长度尺度的90%单边上限分别为17.1 km、11.1 km和18.3 km；对GR的对数贝叶斯因子为−5.49至−2.51，支持广义相对论；约束精度优于部分以往的旋进与铃宕分析。

Conclusion: 当前引力波数据未发现偏离广义相对论的显著证据，对高曲率修正的约束达到10–20 km量级特征长度，是迄今该类EFT项中最严格的数值约束之一。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) observations of binary black holes provide a powerful probe of general relativity (GR) in the strong-field regime. In effective field theory (EFT) extensions of GR, higher-curvature operators modify the binary dynamics and introduce corrections to the inspiral phase. In this work, we consider two parity-even cubic-curvature couplings and one quartic-curvature coupling, whose leading phase corrections enter at 5PN, 6PN, and 8PN order, respectively. We incorporate these corrections into the individual multipoles of the \texttt{SEOBNRv5HM\_ROM} waveform using the flexible theory-independent framework, allowing higher-order modes and the full inspiral-merger-ringdown signal to be used in the analysis. We first perform a Fisher-matrix analysis of 92 binary-black-hole events selected from a parent sample of 273 events in the public LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA catalogs. GW230627\_015337 provides the tightest forecast constraints on all three EFT couplings and is therefore selected for a full Bayesian analysis. The posterior distributions are consistent with GR, with all three couplings consistent with zero at 90\% credibility. The corresponding one-sided 90\% upper limits on the characteristic length scales are \(17.1\), \(11.1\), and \(18.3\,\mathrm{km}\). The log Bayes factors of the three EFT models relative to GR range from \(-5.49\) to \(-2.51\), favoring GR under the adopted priors. These bounds probe higher-curvature length scales at the level of \(10\)--\(20\,\mathrm{km}\) and are numerically tighter than representative previous inspiral and ringdown constraints, although differences in the models and analysis assumptions should be taken into account.

</details>


### [145] [Impact of deformation parameters on the greybody factor and Joule-Thomson expansions of Schwarzschild-AdS black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36730)
*Faisal Javed,A. Eid,Arfa Waseem,Jiafeng Guo,Abdelmalek Bouzenada,Sulaman Shaukat,Farruh Atamurotov,N. Mustapha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了变形Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的灰体因子和焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀，通过引入扭曲坐标将Klein-Gordon方程转化为类薛定谔波方程，推导出精确有效势，并分析其随变形参数、质量与角动量的变化行为；结果表明变形参数增强有效势，显著影响无质量标量场的吸收与散射，且焦耳-汤姆逊分析揭示了由压强与温度调控的加热/冷却区，变形参数与黑洞质量增强等焓曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究变形Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞在量子散射（灰体因子）与热力学（Joule-Thomson效应）两方面的物理特性，理解变形参数对时空结构及相应物理过程的影响。

Method: 采用扭曲坐标将Klein-Gordon方程转化为类薛定谔波方程，解析推导有效势；数值/解析分析该势随变形参数、质量、角动量的变化；结合黑洞热力学，计算并分析Joule-Thomson膨胀中的等焓曲线与临界点。

Result: 变形参数增大导致有效势升高，增强对低频波的反射、利于高频波穿透；Schwarzschild极限下有效势亦被显著抬升；Joule-Thomson分析显示变形参数和黑洞质量均使等焓曲线扩展，加热/冷却区域更明显。

Conclusion: 变形参数是调控黑洞量子散射行为与热力学响应的关键自由度，其引入不仅改变势垒结构，也强化了Joule-Thomson冷却能力，为AdS/CFT中变形背景下的黑洞信息与相变研究提供新线索。

Abstract: This paper investigates the greybody factor and Joule-Thomson expansion of deformed Schwarzschild-AdS black hole models. We constructed the Klein-Gordon equation into a Schrödinger-like wave equation using tortoise coordinates and derived the exact effective potential. Also, we analyzed its behavior under varying physical parameters, including deformation parameters, mass and angular momentum. In this case, our results illustrate that increasing the deformation parameter enhances the effective potential, significantly impacting the absorption and scattering of massless scalar fields. It is noted that the Schwarzschild black hole effective potential is greatly increased when the deformation parameter is present. Near the event horizon, we found that waves behave differently, with higher-frequency waves more easily penetrating potential obstacles and lower-frequency waves bouncing off them. Furthermore, we investigated the thermodynamic characteristics of black holes using the Joule-Thomson expansion, detecting isenthalpic curves that indicate heating and cooling zones controlled by pressure and temperature changes. In this context, we tested the influence of deformation black hole parameters; the isenthalpic curves are enhanced by increasing the deformation parameters and the black hole mass.

</details>


### [146] [Novel topological subclasses of a magnetic black hole in causal nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36796)
*Qi-Qi Liang,De-jiang Yin,Yu-Die Wan,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过将黑洞态视为热力学参数空间中的拓扑缺陷，研究了爱因斯坦引力耦合因果非线性电动力学下的静态渐近平直磁黑洞，在正则系综中分析其单视界物理相，并发现一类新的热力学拓扑子类 $\overline{W}^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$ 及其伴随的边界诱导拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞热力学中拓扑结构的本质，特别是将黑洞态建模为热力学参数空间中的拓扑缺陷，以揭示相变与拓扑数变化之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用广义离壳自由能与段一鸣的φ-映射拓扑流理论，在正则系综中分析单视界黑洞的热力学行为，并分类缺陷曲线轮廓及其演化。

Result: 识别出四类典型缺陷曲线，包括新提出的 $\overline{W}^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$ 子类；发现随辅助反温度升高，系统经历两态→三态→一态演化，且因物理下边界进入不稳定的小微黑洞缺陷，导致全局拓扑数由 $W=0$ 变为 $W=-1$，标志热力学拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 物理视界边界在热力学拓扑分类中起关键作用；相同反温度端点行为和绕数序列可对应不同缺陷演化路径；该因果非线性电动力学黑洞族是研究更多缺陷结构与热力学拓扑相变的理想平台。

Abstract: By treating black-hole states as topological defects in thermodynamic parameter space, we investigate a static, asymptotically flat magnetic black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to causal nonlinear electrodynamics. Using the generalized off-shell free energy and Duan's $φ$-mapping topological current theory, we analyze the physical single-horizon sector in the canonical ensemble. We identify four representative defect-curve profiles, including realizations of the conjectured $W^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$ class and $\overline{W}^{1-}$ subclass, as well as a new thermodynamic topological subclass, $\overline{W}^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$. This new subclass features paired generation and annihilation points, with unstable innermost and outermost branches. As the auxiliary inverse temperature increases, the system evolves from two equilibrium states to three and then back to one, with an unstable small-black-hole defect entering through the physical lower boundary and changing the global topological number from $W=0$ to $W=-1$. This boundary-induced change signals a thermodynamic topological phase transition. These results show that the same inverse-temperature endpoint behavior and winding-number sequence from small to large black holes can accompany different defect evolution, highlighting the role of the physical horizon boundary in thermodynamic topological classification. More broadly, this causal nonlinear-electrodynamics black-hole family provides a setting for discovering further defect structures and thermodynamic topological phase transitions.

</details>


### [147] [Realistic neutron stars in the constant-Q branch of f(Q) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36814)
*Debadri Bhattacharjee,Pradip Kumar Chattopadhyay,P. H. R. S. Moraes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称度规平行引力（$f(Q)$理论）框架下研究中子星的流体静力学平衡与全局结构，提出三种新型非线性$ f(Q) $模型，并结合现代物态方程数值求解修正TOV方程，发现不同模型在最大质量和半径预测上呈现互补性，为多信使观测约束非度规性引力提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称度规平行引力（以非度规标量$ Q $为引力媒介）对中子星结构的影响，特别是通过非线性$ f(Q) $模型检验其能否同时满足致密天体质量与半径的观测约束（如GW170817、GW190814），并揭示引力理论参数化与天体观测之间的关联。

Method: 采用度规-联络形式下的常数$ Q $分支（$ Q = Q_* $）$ f(Q) $引力；构建三种新型非线性模型（Born-Infeld型、Hu-Sawicki类比型、Tsujikawa启发型）；结合GPPVA(DD2)和SLy4真实统一物态方程，数值求解修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程；利用Harrison-Zel'dovich-Novikov准则评估稳定性。

Result: Born-Infeld与Hu-Sawicki类比模型能良好复现低质量中子星半径（符合X射线双星与GW170817约束），但最大质量受限（DD2下<2.43 $ M_\odot $，SLy4下<2.06 $ M_\odot $）；Tsujikawa模型显著增强有效引力耦合，支持高达~3.36 $ M_\odot $的中子星，可解释GW190814中2.59 $ M_\odot $致密天体，但高估典型低质量中子星半径。

Conclusion: 在常数$ Q $框架下，最大质量与半径存在关键现象学互补性；多信使观测（引力波+电磁）是区分和约束不同非线性非度规引力模型的有效手段。

Abstract: We investigate the hydrostatic equilibrium and global structure of neutron stars within symmetric teleparallel gravity, where gravitational interactions are mediated by the non-metricity scalar $Q$. Working within the metric-affine formulation, we adopt the regular, constant-$Q$ branch ($Q = Q_*$) of $f(Q)$ gravity, which preserves regularity at the stellar centre while maintaining explicit dependence on the non-linear functional form through $f(Q_*)$ and its derivatives. We propose three novel non-linear $f(Q)$ extensions: (i) a Born-Infeld-inspired model, (ii) a Hu--Sawicki analogue, and (iii) a Tsujikawa-inspired model. Incorporating state-of-the-art realistic unified equations of state, namely GPPVA(DD2) and SLy4, we numerically solve the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations and evaluate stellar stability via the Harrison-Zel'dovich-Novikov criterion. The Born-Infeld and Hu-Sawicki analogues smoothly recover the general relativistic limit as their control parameters increase, predicting stellar radii for low-mass objects ($M < 2.0\,M_\odot$) that match observational constraints from X-ray binaries and GW170817; however, their maximum masses remain capped below $2.43\,M_\odot$ (DD2) and $2.06\,M_\odot$ (SLy4). Conversely, the Tsujikawa-inspired model significantly stiffens the effective gravitational coupling, accommodating massive compact objects up to $\sim 3.36\,M_\odot$ and offering a viable physical origin for the $2.59_{-0.09}^{+0.08}\,M_\odot$ secondary object in GW190814, albeit at the expense of overpredicting radii for typical low-mass neutron stars. These contrasting behaviours demonstrate a key phenomenological complementarity between maximum mass and radius bounds within the constant-$Q$ framework, highlighting the utility of multimessenger observations in constraining non-linear non-metricity gravity.

</details>


### [148] [Primordial black holes from a type-IIB/F theory inflaton potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36863)
*Chitrak Sarkar,Amitava Choudhuri,Buddhadeb Ghosh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在IIB/F型弦理论的大体积场景中，通过稳定所有Kähler模并引入一个小的高斯扰动来构造一个具有超慢滚特性的暴胀势，从而增强曲率功率谱，促进原初黑洞（PBHs）形成，并探讨其可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索原初黑洞（PBHs）的形成机制，特别是利用弦理论框架下的暴胀模型，以解释暗物质候选体及未来引力波探测的可能性。

Method: 基于类型IIB/F弦理论的大体积场景，构建含微扰高斯隆起的Kähler模稳定化暴胀势；数值求解Mukhanov-Sasaki方程得到曲率功率谱；采用Press-Schechter形式计算PBH丰度，并结合微透镜观测限制进行检验。

Result: 在波数k∼1.12×10⁹ Mpc⁻¹处显著增强曲率功率谱，可产生质量约10⁻⁶M⊙的PBHs，其丰度满足当前观测约束；对应霍金温度约2.78×10⁻¹⁵ GeV，蒸发时间约8.5×10³⁸ Gyr；增强信号位于SKA探测灵敏度范围内，暗示标量诱导引力波有望被未来观测验证。

Conclusion: 该弦论暴胀模型能自然实现PBHs的生成，并与当前天文观测兼容，为连接早期宇宙物理与未来引力波天文学提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study the possibility of the formation of primordial black holes (PBHs) using an inflaton potential constructed by stabilizing all Kähler moduli employing both the perturbative and non-perturbative corrections in the large volume scenario in type-IIB/F string theory. We further introduce a small perturbation in one of the Kähler moduli that modifies the existing potential in the field space. The effective correction in the moduli space is made by introducing a localised Gaussian bump, which induces an ultra-slow-roll feature in the inflaton potential during the early cosmological inflation. The evolution of the amplitude of the curvature power spectrum is solved numerically by using the Mukhanov-Sasaki equation, with Bunch-Davies initial conditions. The inclusion of the Gaussian bump produces a sustainable enhancement in the amplitude of curvature power spectra around $k\sim 1.12 \times 10^{9} Mpc^{-1}$ for the formation of PBHs. The abundance of PBHs ($f_{PBH}$) is calculated using the Press-Schechter formalism for different values of critical density contrast and presented in the background of contemporary $f_{PBH}$ bounds and checked for viability with existing microlensing bounds, corresponding to a mass value $10^{-6}M_\odot$ for $δ$ threshold value $δ_c=0.439$. The Hawking temperature and evaporation time are also determined for these black holes and found to be of the order of $2.78\times 10^{-15}$ GeV and $8.5\times10^{38}$ Gyr, respectively. The enhancement of curvature power spectra lies in the sensitivity region of the SKA observations, suggesting an indication that scalar-induced gravity waves may be probed in future observations.

</details>


### [149] [Self-gravitating Schrödinger--Poisson Solitons in Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36923)
*Kei-ichi Maeda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了弯曲时空下自引力Schrödinger-Poisson（SP）孤子的描述方法，通过局域惯性系引入由时空曲率决定的有效潮汐势；将其应用于绕Kerr黑洞作圆轨道运动的孤子，分析其潮汐形变、质量四极矩及引力辐射，并评估其在空间引力波探测器灵敏度范围内的可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自引力玻色子孤子在黑洞时空中的潮汐响应及其引力波信号，以探索其作为暗物质候选体或致密天体物理探针的可能性。

Method: 基于局域惯性系，在弱场与非相对论近似下将背景时空效应纳入SP方程，引入有效潮汐势；针对绕Kerr黑洞的圆轨道孤子，提取静态与时间依赖的四极潮汐分量，定义潮汐可变形性与无量纲Love数，并估算旋转形变孤子产生的引力波特征应变。

Result: 推导出孤子潮汐形变与质量四极矩的关系，给出其引力辐射的特征应变估计，并识别出若干参数区间（如孤子尺寸、轨道半径）下该应变可能落入LISA等空间引力波探测器的灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 建立了研究黑洞时空中自引力玻色子孤子潮汐响应及其引力波特征的系统框架，为未来探测此类新型致密天体提供了理论基础和可观测性判据。

Abstract: We formulate a description of self-gravitating Schrödinger--Poisson (SP) solitons in curved spacetime using a local inertial frame centered on the soliton. In the weak-field and non-relativistic regime, the effect of the background spacetime is incorporated into the SP equations through an effective tidal potential determined by the spacetime curvature. We apply this framework to a soliton moving along a circular equatorial geodesic around a Kerr black hole. The tidal field contains stationary and time-dependent quadrupolar components, which induce a deformation of the soliton and an associated mass quadrupole moment. We formulate the quadrupolar response in terms of the tidal deformability and the dimensionless Love number. We then estimate the gravitational radiation associated with the rotating deformed soliton and evaluate the corresponding characteristic strain for representative soliton sizes and orbital radii. A comparison with representative sensitivity curves of planned space-based gravitational-wave detectors identifies parameter regimes in which the estimated characteristic strain may lie within their sensitivity ranges. Our framework provides a systematic basis for investigating the tidal response of self-gravitating bosonic solitons in black-hole spacetimes and their potential gravitational-wave signatures.

</details>


### [150] [Can One Hear the Absence of a Singularity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37028)
*Matteo Della Rocca,Paolo Pani,Andrea P. Sanna*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则黑洞（即内部无曲率奇点的黑洞）的准正规模谱，发现即使正则化参数极小，其大模态极限下的谱也与史瓦西黑洞谱发生不连续变化，表现为频率实部渐近趋于零而非非零常数，从而为奇点消除提供了谱学印记，并揭示了黑洞谱对广义相对论微小偏离的非微扰不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力理论预期能解决广义相对论预言的曲率奇点问题，产生内部正则的黑洞几何；本文旨在探究此类正则黑洞的准正规模谱是否携带奇点消解的可观测特征。

Method: 通过系统发展单值性方法（monodromy method）进行解析推导，并辅以数值计算予以验证。

Result: 对于一大类正则黑洞（含多个已知解），其大模态极限下准正规模的实部渐近趋于零，而 Schwarzschild 黑洞则趋于非零常数；该差异在正则化参数任意小时仍不连续存在。

Conclusion: 正则黑洞的大模态准正规模谱保留了奇点消解的清晰印记，且表明黑洞谱对广义相对论真空解的任意微小偏离具有非微扰不稳定性（谱不稳定性）。

Abstract: One of the central expectations of quantum gravity is the resolution of the curvature singularities predicted by General Relativity, potentially giving rise to effective black-hole geometries that are regular inside the horizon. We show that, for a very broad class of regular black holes including several known solutions, the quasinormal-mode spectrum in the large-overtone limit differs discontinuously from that of a Schwarzschild black hole, even when the regularization parameter is arbitrarily small. The real part of the quasinormal frequencies vanishes asymptotically, rather than approaching a nonzero constant, as generically occurs in General Relativity and other theories predicting black holes with curvature singularities. The asymptotic black-hole spectrum thus retains a distinctive imprint of singularity resolution. We establish these results analytically through a systematic formulation of the monodromy method and confirm them numerically. We interpret this phenomenon as a manifestation of black-hole spectral instability, whereby arbitrarily small deviations from vacuum General Relativity produce nonperturbative changes in the large-overtone spectrum.

</details>


### [151] [Genesis in Minimally Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37063)
*Alexander Ganz,Paul Martens,Shinji Mukohyama,Pavel Petrov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在II型最小修正引力框架下构建了一个Genesis场景，背景解在过去测地线完备且渐近于闵可夫斯基时空；标量扰动无鬼且无紫外梯度不稳定性，但红外区域存在有限时间的负声速平方；数值模拟显示标量谱近似尺度不变且略呈红倾，张量谱为蓝倾且张量-标量比被强烈压制；高阶相互作用在远古时期被压低至普朗克尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统暴胀模型中初始奇点问题，探索一种无需暴胀、过去完备且避免量子引力效应显著影响的宇宙起源机制。

Method: 基于II型最小修正引力理论构建Genesis背景解，分析其背景演化与线性扰动性质（包括稳定性、功率谱），并数值求解超视界扰动演化，同时考察高阶相互作用在远古极限下的行为。

Result: 获得过去测地线完备的非奇异背景；标量扰动产生近尺度不变且略红倾的功率谱；张量谱蓝倾且r极小；所有非线性相互作用在远古趋于普朗克压制。

Conclusion: 该II型最小修正引力Genesis模型提供了一种自洽、稳定且可观测特征明确的奇点前宇宙起源方案，兼具理论自然性与潜在可检验性。

Abstract: We construct a Genesis scenario within the framework of a type-II minimally modified gravity. The background solution is past geodesically complete and approaches Minkowski spacetime in the asymptotic past. While the Hubble expansion rate grows, the matter sector satisfies the null-energy condition. Scalar perturbations are ghost-free and have no ultraviolet gradient instability, although the squared sound speed $c_\mathcal{R}^2$ becomes negative within a finite infrared region in the Fourier space for a finite time interval. Numerical evolution, including superhorizon growth, yields a nearly scale-invariant, slightly red-tilted scalar spectrum over a broad region of parameter space. The tensor spectrum is blue, with a strongly suppressed tensor-to-scalar ratio. Finally, using the matter fluctuation $δχ$, whose asymptotic normalization is regular, we find that the background-dependent cubic couplings vanish in the far past at fixed nonzero momenta. The surviving matter--graviton and graviton self-interactions are Planck suppressed.

</details>


### [152] [Thermodynamics and phase structure of rotating Hayward-AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37194)
*Qi-Quan Li,Yu Zhang,Hoernisa Iminniyaz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过修正的Newman-Janis算法构造了旋转Hayward-AdS正则黑洞的‘母’黑洞解，并在全相空间中推导其热力学量；引入正则性条件后，发现其霍金温度与表面引力温度一致，且P-V等温线呈现液/气相变特征，相图呈8形或C形，揭示了正则性条件而非球对称性主导该类黑洞的相结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究旋转正则AdS黑洞的热力学相变行为，特别是验证其相结构（如8形/C形）是否源于正则性条件而非球对称性假设。

Method: 采用修正Newman-Janis算法构造旋转‘母’黑洞解；在全参数空间(S,J,q,α,P)中推导热力学量；施加正则性条件M₁=q³/α得到旋转Hayward-AdS正则黑洞的热力学量；分析霍金温度、比热、P-V等温线及G₂-T₂相图。

Result: 获得与表面引力一致的霍金温度；发现小/中/大黑洞三支结构及局部稳定性差异；P-V等温线呈现振荡行为；G₂-T₂相图出现8形（P<P_z，一阶相变）和C形（P_z<P<P_c，零阶相变）两种构型；增大a、q或P均缩小中间黑洞区域。

Conclusion: 旋转Hayward-AdS正则黑洞仍保持8形/C形相结构，表明该结构本质上由正则性条件重构热力学相空间所致，而非依赖于球对称性。

Abstract: We use the modified Newman--Janis algorithm to obtain the rotating ``mother'' black hole solution of the rotating Hayward-AdS black hole. We further derive the fundamental thermodynamic quantities of the ``mother'' black hole in the full unconstrained phase space $(S,J,q,α,P)$, and impose the regularity condition $M_1=q^3/α$ to obtain the thermodynamic quantities of the rotating Hayward-AdS regular black hole. This order of operations gives a Hawking temperature identical to the surface gravity temperature and produces the correct response functions of the regular black hole. For $P<P_c$, the Hawking temperature $T_2$ possesses a local maximum and a local minimum, which divides the black hole into small, intermediate, and large black hole branches, and the $P$--$V$ isotherms display oscillatory behavior characteristic of a liquid/gas phase transition. The small and large black hole branches are locally stable, whereas the intermediate black hole branch has negative heat capacity and is locally unstable. Increasing the rotation parameter $a$, magnetic charge $q$, or pressure $P$ reduces the region of the intermediate branch. The $G_2$--$T_2$ diagram shows that the phase structure, in contrast to the standard swallowtail of the Reissner--Nordström-AdS and Kerr--Newman-AdS black holes, is 8-shaped or C-shaped. These two shapes are divided by the characteristic pressure $P_z$. For $P<P_z$, the curve is 8-shaped, corresponding to a first-order small/large black hole transition. For $P_z<P<P_c$, the curve is C-shaped, corresponding to a zeroth-order transition. These results show that the 8-shaped/C-shaped phase structure characteristic of regular AdS black holes persists under rotation, indicating that it stems from the restructuring of the thermodynamic phase space by the regularity condition rather than from spherical symmetry.

</details>


### [153] [Noether Symmetries in Scalar--Vector--Two-Form Cosmology with a Topological BF Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37278)
*Amin Rezaei Akbarieh,Yusuf Kucukakca*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量场与各向同性Abel 1-形式和2-形式三重态的耦合系统，通过标量依赖的动能函数和常数拓扑BF相互作用建模；在保持齐次约化中拉格朗日乘子（lapse）的前提下，恢复了Friedmann约束，并确定了所有12个物理模式具有正动能项且高频传播速度为光速的参数域；发现一类指数模型支持加速混合解（a∝t²），具非零渐近形式分数和有界归一化线性扰动；利用Noether对称性分析，在平坦minisuperspace坐标下选取二次有效势，导出标量势为双曲平方或指数型（而非幂律型）；根据势坐标的因果性质分为三支：I支含de Sitter极限，II支无法在正则未来完备膨胀轨迹上维持加速，III支给出两形式场均活跃的精确解，晚期膨胀标度为a∝t^{4/3}；通过与无耦合及单场极限对比，区分了形式场贡献与标量驱动加速；BF项仅改变两形式场间的有限时间能量交换，不提供直接负压源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标量场与多重微分形式（1-形式和2-形式）耦合的动力学行为，特别是其在宇宙学背景（如加速膨胀）中的可行性、稳定性（正动能、有界扰动）及对称性结构（Noether守恒量），并厘清拓扑BF项在宇宙演化中的真实物理作用。

Method: 采用广义相对论框架下的齐次各向同性（FLRW）约化，保留lapse变量以严格导出Friedmann约束；分析动能矩阵正定性与高能传播速度；构造指数型模型求解经典背景解；在平坦minisuperspace中设定二次有效势，求解Noether对称性决定方程，反推标量势形式；按势坐标因果结构分类解空间；通过极限情形（无耦合、单场）作对照分析。

Result: 1) 确定了12模式正动能与光速传播的参数域；2) 发现a∝t²加速混合解及其稳定性；3) Noether分析导出标量势必为双曲平方或指数型；4) 解空间分为三支，其中III支给出两形式场共存的a∝t^{4/3}晚期解；5) BF项不产生负压，仅调控形式场间能量交换。

Conclusion: 该耦合模型能支持多种宇宙学演化分支，其动力学特性（如加速机制、稳定性、对称性）由标量势形状与形式场耦合结构共同决定；BF拓扑项虽不驱动加速，但影响形式自由度间能量分配；标量势的非幂律形式（双曲/指数）是Noether对称性的必然结果，凸显几何与对称性对有效宇宙学模型的深层约束。

Abstract: We study a canonical scalar field coupled to isotropic triplets of Abelian one-forms and two-forms through scalar-dependent kinetic functions and a constant topological $BF$ interaction. By retaining the lapse in the homogeneous reduction, we recover the Friedmann constraint and identify a sufficient domain in which all twelve physical modes have positive kinetic terms and luminal high-frequency propagation. A separate exponential model admits an accelerating mixed solution with $a\propto t^2$, nonzero asymptotic form fractions, and bounded normalized linear perturbations. For the Noether analysis, we choose a quadratic effective potential in flat minisuperspace coordinates and solve the zero-boundary determining equations. The canonical scalar potential is hyperbolic-squared or exponential, rather than a power law in the scalar field. The causal character of the potential coordinate separates three branches: Branch I admits a de Sitter limit, Branch II cannot sustain acceleration on regular future-complete expanding trajectories, and Branch III gives an exact solution with both form sectors active and late expansion $a\propto t^{4/3}$. Comparisons with consistent uncoupled and reduced-field limits separate the form contributions from the scalar-driven acceleration. The $BF$ term changes the finite-time exchange between the form sectors but supplies no direct negative-pressure source.

</details>


### [154] [Geodesic dynamics and tidal forces in a quantum-regularized black hole from string T-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37331)
*G. Alencar,Micael S. Oliveira,Diego Sáez-Chillón Gómez,Marcos V. de S. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了受T-对偶性启发的中性和带电正则时空中的测地线动力学和潮汐力效应，揭示了T-对偶尺度ℓ₀与电荷Q如何共同调控黑洞/无视界几何相变、轨道稳定性及有限潮汐行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力效应（以T-对偶正则化为代表）与经典电磁场（电荷Q）如何共同影响强场区域的时空结构、粒子运动与潮汐响应，弥补经典黑洞奇点发散问题。

Method: 分析T-对偶启发的正则时空几何性质；求解测地线有效势并研究圆轨道存在性与稳定性；计算径向自由下落观测者的径向与角向潮汐分量及其极值；数值模拟测地线偏差矢量演化。

Result: T-对偶使中心潮汐力有限化，出现径向与角向同步压缩区；ℓ₀与Q共同决定视界结构与轨道半径；二者均抑制累积径向拉伸，而角向形变对Q不敏感；经典极限被正确恢复。

Conclusion: T-对偶正则化与电荷协同调控强场时空的动力学与潮汐特性，既消除了奇点发散，又展现出丰富的新物理现象，为量子引力效应的可观测特征提供理论线索。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the geodesic dynamics and tidal force effects in neutral and electrically charged regular spacetimes inspired by T-duality. We first analyze the geometric properties of these solutions, including their regular central structure and horizon configurations, and show how the interplay between the T-duality scale $\ell_0$ and the electric charge $Q$ determines the transition between black hole and horizonless geometries. We then study the motion of massive and massless particles through the corresponding effective potentials, examining the existence and stability of circular orbits and the behavior of their characteristic radii. We further investigate the tidal forces experienced by observers in radial free fall, focusing on the behavior of the radial and angular components, their extrema and sign changes, and the location of these characteristic points relative to the horizon structure of the geometry. We show that the T-duality regularization renders the tidal forces finite in the central region and gives rise to different stretching and compression regimes, including a region of simultaneous compression in both the radial and angular directions, while the electric charge modifies the strength and location of these tidal features. Finally, we analyze the evolution of the geodesic deviation vector for two sets of initial conditions. We find that both the T-duality scale and the electric charge tend to suppress the accumulated radial stretching, whereas the angular deformation is considerably less sensitive to the charge. These results show how the quantum regularization induced by T-duality and the presence of electric charge modify the orbital dynamics and tidal response of these geometries in the strong-field regime. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the corresponding classical limits are recovered appropriately.

</details>


### [155] [Quasinormal Ringing of a Schwarzschild Black Hole Surrounded by a King Dark Matter Halo: Higher-Order WKB Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37501)
*Semra Gurtas Dogan,Hassan Hassanabadi,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由King密度剖面描述的暗物质晕环绕的史瓦西黑洞中，标量场和电磁场扰动的准正规模频率。通过改进的WKB方法计算发现，暗物质晕会降低振荡频率和阻尼率，且该效应主要取决于光子球内包含的暗物质质量及零测地线的Lyapunov指数与角频率之比；当将暗物质晕置于黑洞外部时，频率变化受引力红移主导；密度跃变可使阻尼率改变达9%，而连续剖面下偏差小于1.5%；未发现不稳定模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质晕对黑洞准正规模的影响，以期为未来引力波观测提供理论依据，并理解暗物质分布对黑洞时空几何及扰动谱的修正效应。

Method: 采用六阶WKB近似及其Padé改进版本计算准正规模频率；引入反双曲正弦函数重写度规的lapse函数以避免高阶导数数值抵消问题；对比时域演化结果验证精度；考虑暗物质晕部分落入视界内及完全置于黑洞外部（通过Einstein cluster建模）两种情形；分析密度跃变与连续剖面对结果的影响。

Result: 暗物质晕使准正规模实部（振荡频率）和虚部（阻尼率）均降低；实部变化主要由光子球内暗物质质量决定；虚部变化与零测地线Lyapunov指数和角频率之比相关；当晕置于黑洞外时，实部近似为Schwarzschild值乘以晕处引力红移因子；密度跃变可使阻尼率改变约9%；连续剖面下偏差控制在0.5%-1.5%以内；所有检验情形均无不稳定模。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕显著但可控地修改黑洞准正规模，其影响机制清晰可解，且不引发动力学不稳定；结果表明，未来高精度引力波观测或可约束星系中心暗物质分布形态。

Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic perturbations of a Schwarzschild black hole (BH) surrounded by a dark matter halo described by the King density profile. The lapse function can be expressed as $1-2[M+M_{\rm DM}(r)]/r$, where $M_{\rm DM}(r)$ is the halo mass enclosed within radius $r$; thus, the halo affects the geometry through its enclosed-mass function. We compute the quasinormal frequencies using the sixth-order WKB method and its Padé-improved version. Rewriting the lapse function in terms of the inverse hyperbolic sine avoids the severe numerical cancellations that arise when evaluating its high-order derivatives. For the configurations examined, the fundamental frequencies agree with those extracted from time-domain evolution to within $0.2\%$.The halo lowers both the oscillation frequency and the damping rate. The change of the real part is almost entirely accounted for by the halo mass enclosed by the photon sphere, and the additional decrease of the damping rate follows the ratio of the Lyapunov exponent to the angular frequency of the circular null orbit. Since part of the halo lies inside the horizon in this metric, we also place the halo outside the BH by means of an Einstein cluster. The frequencies then decrease by a similar amount, but the real part follows the Schwarzschild value multiplied by the gravitational redshift factor of the halo. A jump in the density at the inner edge of the halo scatters the waves and changes the damping rate by up to about $9\%$. For a continuous density profile, deviations from the redshift relation remain below about $1.5\%$ for $r_{\rm in}=3M$ and below about $0.5\%$ for the cases with $r_{\rm in}\geq4M$. No unstable modes are found within the perturbation sectors and parameter configurations examined.

</details>


### [156] [One Method to Find Them All: Analytic Black Holes beyond Einstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37515)
*Dongjun Li,Jierui Hu,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一框架，用于构建Kerr黑洞在高曲率修正下的稳态轴对称变形解，并给出了六种高曲率相互作用的闭式解析解，无需慢旋转近似或数值拟合，为爱因斯坦引力之外的黑洞几何及强场引力检验提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转黑洞是检验引力理论的有力探针，但在真空广义相对论之外构造其精确几何仍具挑战性。

Method: 发展了从潮汐曲率和物理源出发构造Kerr黑洞稳态轴对称变形的统一框架，并获得六种高曲率相互作用在耦合常数领头阶下的闭式解析解，适用于一般亚极端自旋，不依赖慢旋转展开、数值拟合或模分解。

Result: 所得解通过了场方程、已有逐模构造结果及已知视界物理量解析预测等多重严格检验；发现相互作用的手征性主导了解析结构，该规律可能推广至一大类纯度规高曲率理论。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建爱因斯坦引力之外的黑洞几何及扰动天体物理环境中的黑洞解提供了系统性路径，支撑引力波、黑洞成像与X射线光谱等强场观测检验，以及近极端黑洞的理论研究。

Abstract: Rotating black holes offer powerful probes of gravity, but constructing their geometries beyond vacuum general relativity remains challenging. Here we develop a unified framework for constructing stationary, axisymmetric deformations of Kerr from their tidal curvature and physical sources. As a demonstration, we obtain closed-form solutions for six higher-curvature interactions at leading order in the couplings and for generic subextremal spin, without slow-rotation expansions, numerical fitting, or mode decomposition. The solutions pass stringent checks against the field equations, previous mode-by-mode constructions, and known analytic predictions for horizon quantities. We find that the chirality of the interactions organizes the analytic structure of these solutions, a pattern that likely extends to a large class of purely metric higher-curvature theories. Our framework opens a systematic route to black-hole geometries in beyond-Einstein theories and perturbative astrophysical environments. These geometries provide a foundation for strong-field tests with gravitational waves and other observational channels, such as black-hole images and X-ray spectroscopy, as well as theoretical studies of near-extremal black holes.

</details>


### [157] [Gravitational wave signatures of periodic orbits around a Schwarzschild-like black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37525)
*Mirzabek Alloqulov,Sanjar Shaymatov,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在改进的Schwarzschild黑洞背景下，偏离Schwarzschild几何对粒子轨道运动及相应引力波辐射的影响，通过比较轨道频率、数值模拟粒子运动和计算引力波偏振，分析参数ξ和γ对轨道构型和波形的调制效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究改进Schwarzschild黑洞模型中几何偏离对测试粒子动力学及引力辐射的影响，以识别该黑洞模型的动力学特征。

Method: 基于径向与方位角轨道频率的可通约性确定周期轨道；采用对称无迹质量四极矩方法数值重构粒子运动并计算引力波偏振；与标准Schwarzschild情形对比分析。

Result: 参数ξ和γ仅引起轨道构型及其引力波形的适度变化，但足以作为探测该黑洞模型动力学特征的潜在信号。

Conclusion: 改进Schwarzschild黑洞的几何修正虽小，却可在周期轨道及其引力辐射中留下可观测印记，为检验替代理论提供新途径。

Abstract: We investigate how deviations from the Schwarzschild geometry affect the motion of massive test particles and the gravitational radiation associated with their periodic trajectories around a new improved Schwarzschild black hole. Periodic configurations are determined by examining the commensurability of the radial and azimuthal orbital frequencies. For representative trajectories, we numerically reconstruct the particle motion and calculate corresponding gravitational wave polarizations using the symmetric trace-free mass-quadrupole approach. By comparing these signals with their Schwarzschild counterparts, we assess how the modified spacetime geometry is encoded in the radiation. Our analysis shows that the parameters $ξ$ and $γ$ produce modest changes in the orbital configurations and their associated waveforms, providing a means of investigating the dynamical signatures of this BH model.

</details>


### [158] [Weighing dark matter particles with gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37638)
*Rodrigo Vicente,Francisco Duque,Theophanes K. Karydas,Gianfranco Bertone*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出利用毫赫兹引力波观测器（如LISA）观测极端质量比旋进（EMRI）信号，来测量超轻暗物质粒子质量的方法。通过将波效应主导的吸积与动力学摩擦纳入相对论性旋进波形，联合推断暗物质粒子质量、密度尖峰归一化及双星参数。对约10^5倍太阳质量的中等质量黑洞，可在一个数量级内测定10^{-14}–10^{-11} eV范围的粒子质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为，仅靠引力效应无法确定碰撞less暗物质粒子的质量；本文旨在突破该限制，探索利用EMRI引力波信号探测超轻暗物质粒子质量的新途径。

Method: 将超轻暗物质在大质量黑洞周围形成的波状密度尖峰建模，引入波依赖的吸积率和动力学摩擦效应，并将其整合进相对论性EMRI引力波波形中，再通过贝叶斯参数估计方法对模拟LISA信号进行联合推断。

Result: 针对LISA可观测的低质量端大质量黑洞（M ~ 10^5 M⊙），预测可在约一个数量级精度内测量10^{-14}–10^{-11} eV范围内的超轻暗物质粒子质量。

Conclusion: EMRI引力波观测为超轻暗物质粒子质量提供了新的、可实现的天体物理探针，拓展了暗物质参数空间的可观测性。

Abstract: It is commonly assumed that the mass of dark matter particles cannot be determined from their gravitational effects when dark matter behaves as collisionless matter. Here we show that extreme-mass-ratio inspirals observable by millihertz gravitational-wave observatories such as the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) could measure the mass of ultralight dark matter particles in sufficiently dense dark matter spikes. The separation of scales between the two black holes allows ultralight dark matter to form a collisionless density spike around the massive primary while retaining wave effects in its interaction with the stellar-mass companion. We incorporate wave-dependent accretion and dynamical friction into relativistic inspiral waveforms and jointly infer the dark matter particle mass, spike normalisation and binary parameters from simulated LISA signals. For massive black holes at the low-mass end of the LISA population ($M\sim 10^5 M_\odot$), our forecasts show that particle masses spanning $10^{-14}-10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$ could be measured to within an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [159] [Foldy--Wouthuysen Transformation of the Generalized Dirac Equation in Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27889)
*Sabutay Ugur,Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在对称度规平行引力背景下的广义狄拉克方程的非相对论极限，推导出包含新自旋-引力耦合项的有效哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义狄拉克方程在对称度规平行引力中的低能行为，拓展传统狄拉克理论对非度规性（non-metricity）的响应。

Method: 在重合规范和弱场Schwarzschild度规下，构建广义旋量联络，推导广义狄拉克哈密顿量，并通过Foldy–Wouthuysen变换至1/m²阶，保留引力势及其空间导数的一阶项。

Result: 得到块对角哈密顿量，除常规引力类动能、自旋-轨道、Darwin项外，新增直接自旋-引力、各向异性自旋-动量-引力及潮汐自旋-动量耦合项；并对地球弱场中电子做了量级估计以验证展开截断合理性。

Conclusion: 广义狄拉克方程在对称度规平行引力中自然诱导出自旋、动量与引力场空间变化的新耦合通道，为实验约束广义旋量联络参数提供了有效哈密顿框架。

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of the generalized Dirac equation in a weak, static, and spherically symmetric background of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The underlying generalized spinor connection incorporates the complete Clifford-algebra basis and introduces additional couplings to the non-metricity sector beyond those of the conventional Dirac theory. Working in the coincident gauge and adopting the weak-field Schwarzschild geometry in isotropic coordinates, we derive the corresponding generalized Dirac Hamiltonian and perform successive Foldy--Wouthuysen transformations up to order $1/m^2$, retaining terms to first order in the gravitational potential and its spatial derivatives. The resulting block-diagonal Hamiltonian contains not only the expected gravitational counterparts of the kinetic, spin--orbit, and Darwin interactions, but also additional operator structures generated by the generalized spinor connection. In particular, direct spin--gravity, anisotropic spin--momentum--gravity, and tidal spin--momentum couplings arise naturally from the generalized metric-affine interaction. We further perform an order-of-magnitude analysis for an electron in the Earth's weak gravitational field to justify the adopted truncation of the inverse-mass expansion. These results demonstrate that the generalized Dirac equation in a symmetric teleparallel background gives rise to new low-energy interaction channels involving the fermion spin, momentum, and spatial derivatives of the gravitational field. The resulting effective Hamiltonian provides a framework for exploring phenomenological constraints on the additional couplings entering the generalized spinor connection.

</details>
