### [160] [Field-level modeling of halo and galaxy alignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36027)
*Nickolas Kokron,Mikhail M. Ivanov,James M. Sullivan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于自旋-2场涨落的有效场论（EFT）模型，用于描述星系和暗物质晕形状的本征对齐（IA），包含至三阶的非线性形状算符，并在Quijote和FLAMINGO模拟中精确标定了各阶形状偏置参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系形状的本征对齐是弱引力透镜测量（如宇宙剪切）的主要系统误差源，现有模型（如NLA、TATT）在物理完备性和精度上存在不足，亟需更系统、可扩展的理论框架。

Method: 建立基于大尺度结构示踪物（如星系、暗物质晕）自旋-2形状场涨落的有效场论；纳入Zel'dovich位移的非线性处理；构造至三阶的完备形状算符；在3D场层面建模并用广义最小二乘法进行偏置参数精密标定，推导最优估计量与解析协方差矩阵。

Result: EFT模型在暗物质晕螺旋度场与初始条件的相关性上优于NLA和TATT模型；在Quijote模拟中测得质量依赖的线性、二次、三次形状偏置系数；在FLAMINGO模拟中对LRG、旋涡/椭圆星系显著探测到线性与非线性对齐系数，对发射线星系（ELG）发现非零密度加权潮汐偏置证据。

Conclusion: 基于EFT的本征对齐建模是未来高精度宇宙学形状相关分析的关键，本工作为后续观测样本提供仿真驱动的先验建模基础。

Abstract: We develop an effective field theory (EFT) of intrinsic alignments at the level of spin-2 field fluctuations of large-scale structure tracers, such as the shapes of galaxies and dark matter halos. Our perturbative model includes the non-linear treatment of the Zel'dovich displacement, and contains all necessary shape operators through the third order. We apply our field-level model to the full 3D distribution of galaxy shapes, whose sky projections are a known contaminant of cosmic shear measurements. Our field-level EFT model achieves a better description of the helicity fields of dark matter halo shapes than the standard non-linear alignment (NLA) and tidal torquing + tidal alignment (TATT) models in the literature, as evidenced by improved correlations with the simulations' initial conditions. We perform precision measurements of field-level bias parameters by means of a generalized least-squares problem, for which we derive an optimal estimator and analytic covariance matrices. We measure the full set of linear, quadratic, and cubic shape bias coefficients as a function of halo mass in the $\texttt{Quijote}$ N-body simulations. We then study the intrinsic alignments of galaxies in the $\texttt{FLAMINGO}$ hydrodynamical simulation, focusing on observationally relevant samples: spirals, ellipticals, luminous red galaxies (LRG) and emission-line galaxies (ELG). We detect linear and nonlinear alignment coefficients at high significance for LRG, spiral and elliptical galaxies. We also find evidence for non-zero density-weighted tidal bias coefficient of the simulated ELG. This work supports the notion that EFT-based models of alignments will be paramount in optimally extracting constraints from future shape correlations, and lays the foundation for the development of simulation-based priors for future galaxy shape samples.

</details>


### [161] [Two-dimensional galaxy cluster shapes using lensing data: a simulated analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36039)
*C. Giocoli,M. Meneghetti,G. Despali,L. Moscardini,E. Rasia,G. Lesci,W. Cui,G. Yepes*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟三个不同动力学状态的星系团，评估了弱引力透镜在恢复星系团二维形态方面的可靠性，并比较了弱透镜与强透镜所揭示的形态特征。结果表明，二维弱透镜建模能较准确恢复投影质量取向，而KS重构更倾向于给出偏圆的质量分布；结合弱透镜与强透镜形态分析可有效探查星系团跨尺度结构和动力学状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团形态反映其并合历史与动力学状态，高分辨率弱透镜虽可探测偏离球对称性，但其恢复二维形态的能力尚待系统评估；同时需厘清弱透镜与强透镜在不同尺度上所揭示形态的一致性与差异。

Method: 基于The Three Hundred Project中z=0.22的三个星系团（分别代表松弛、轻微扰动与非松弛态），选取多方向投影（随机及主轴方向），生成大量弱透镜源样本（面密度30 arcmin⁻²，本征椭率弥散σ_ε=0.3）；采用一维NFW/BMO拟合约束质量和浓度，再用二维椭圆NFW模型恢复椭率、位置角和中心；同时对比真实收敛图、Kaiser & Squires重构及强透镜切向临界线所测得的形态。

Result: 恢复参数依赖于投影方向与动力学状态：松弛星系团内外形态一致，扰动系统中心更椭；二维弱透镜建模能稳健恢复质量取向；KS重构能较好恢复位置角但倾向低估椭率；非松弛系统的质心偏移更大。

Conclusion: 联合弱透镜与强透镜形态分析可跨越物理尺度探测星系团结构，是研究其形态演化与动力学状态的有效新途径。

Abstract: Context. Cluster morphology reflects assembly history and dynamical state. High-resolution weak lensing can reveal departures from spherical symmetry, but its ability to recover two-dimensional shapes remains less explored. Aims. We assess how reliably weak lensing recovers projected cluster morphology and its dependence on orientation and dynamical state. We also compare large-scale weak-lensing morphology with that traced by strong lensing near the cluster centre. Methods. We study three clusters at $z=0.22$ from The Three Hundred Project, representing relaxed, mildly disturbed and unrelaxed systems. For each, we consider three random projections and three along the principal axes of the mass tensor. For each projection, we generate 128 weak-lensing catalogues with a source density of 30 arcmin$^{-2}$ and intrinsic ellipticity dispersion $σ_ε=0.3$. We constrain mass and concentration from one-dimensional NFW and BMO fits, then recover ellipticity, position angle and centre using a two-dimensional elliptical NFW model. We also measure morphology from true convergence maps, Kaiser \& Squires reconstructions and strong-lensing tangential critical lines. Results. Recovered parameters depend on orientation and dynamical state. The relaxed cluster has similar inner and large-scale shapes, whereas disturbed systems appear more elliptical centrally. Two-dimensional weak-lensing modelling recovers the projected mass orientation despite source sampling and shape noise. Kaiser \& Squires reconstructions recover the position angle well but favour rounder mass distributions. Centroid offsets are larger in unrelaxed systems. Conclusions. Combining weak- and strong-lensing morphology probes cluster structure across physical scales and offers a promising way to investigate morphology and dynamical state.

</details>


### [162] [Does the Escape Fraction Prescription Shape Reionization History and Morphology?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36046)
*Pulkit Sheoran,Ankita Bera,Sultan Hassan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST紫外光度函数和再电离时期（EoR）观测数据，通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法约束电离光子逃逸分数 f_esc 的三种幂律参数化形式，并发现其随红移减小、与晕质量基本无关；不同 f_esc 模型在全局再电离历史和21 cm功率谱上差异很小（≈10%），表明f_esc的细节物理对大尺度可观测量影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电离光子逃逸分数 f_esc 是再电离时期最关键的未约束参数之一，因高红移际介质不透明而无法直接观测，亟需借助多信使联合建模进行约束。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法，联合拟合JWST紫外光度函数与EoR关键观测量（电离光子发射率、中性氢分数、Thomson光学深度），约束 f_esc(z)、f_esc(M_h) 和 f_esc(z, M_h) 三种幂律模型；再将最优拟合模型输入半数值模拟SimFast21，生成并比较其电离结构与21 cm功率谱。

Result: 数据偏好 f_esc 随红移下降（z=5时≈0.8，z=15时≈0.35），且联合拟合下 f_esc 基本不依赖于暗晕质量；所有模型均能重现观测，全局再电离历史高度一致；在相同中性氢分数下，电离形态和21 cm功率谱差异均≤10%。

Conclusion: 一旦用相同观测校准，f_esc 的具体参数化形式（反映ISM物理）对全局再电离历史和21 cm功率谱影响甚微，其细节差异需借助视线统计（如Lyα/21 cm森林）或再电离时序的精细预报来区分。

Abstract: The escape fraction of ionizing photons, $f_{\rm esc}$, is one of the least constrained quantities of the Epoch of Reionization (EoR), and the opacity of the intergalactic medium prevents its direct measurement at high redshifts. We constrain three power-law parameterizations, $f_{\rm esc}(z)$, $f_{\rm esc}(M_{\rm h})$, and $f_{\rm esc}(z, M_{\rm h})$, against JWST UV luminosity functions and EoR observables (the ionizing emissivity, the neutral hydrogen fraction, and the Thomson optical depth) using Markov Chain Monte Carlo, with priors that allow $f_{\rm esc}$ to either increase or decrease with redshift and halo mass. We then propagate the best-fit models into the semi-numerical simulation SimFast21 and compare their ionization morphology and 21 cm power spectra with those of a constant-$f_{\rm esc}$ model. All models reproduce the observations with nearly identical global reionization histories. The data favor an escape fraction that decreases with increasing redshift, from $\approx 0.8$ at $z = 5$ to $\approx 0.35$ at $z = 15$. The halo-mass-only model prefers $f_{\rm esc}$ increasing with halo mass, but this trend acts as a proxy for redshift evolution. When we vary both dependencies jointly, the data prefer a mass-independent $f_{\rm esc}$. At matched global neutral fraction, the ionization morphology is closely similar for all four prescriptions, and the 21 cm power spectra agree to within $\approx 10\%$. These results suggest that, once calibrated against the same observations, the details of the $f_{\rm esc}$ prescription, which are set by the physics of the interstellar medium, largely average out in both the global observables and the 21 cm power spectrum. Distinguishing them may require statistics beyond the power spectrum, such as sightline statistics of the Ly$α$ and 21 cm forests, or forecasts that exploit their small differences in reionization timing.

</details>


### [163] [ECH0EZ: Estimating the Hubble constant with ZTF SN Ia DR2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36051)
*Konstantin Karchev,Marco Giunta,Raul Jimenez,Roberto Trotta,Licia Verde*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用Zwicky瞬变设施第二次数据释放的772颗Ia型超新星，构建了一个低红移（z<0.06）体积受限、几乎无马尔奎斯特偏差的样本，通过贝叶斯分层模型校准后得到H₀ = 71.6 ± 1.5 km/s/Mpc（或72.3 ± 1.1 km/s/Mpc），结果支持局部距离阶梯测量，并与普朗克结果存在显著张力（2.7σ–4.1σ），表明哈勃常数张力可能源于Ia型超新星或整个距离阶梯之外的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前Ia型超新星数据在哈勃常数H₀测量中存在的异质性和严重选择效应问题，尤其是马尔奎斯特偏差，以更可靠地检验哈勃张力。

Method: 使用ZTF DR2中772颗Ia型超新星（含z<0.06体积受限子样本），修正仪器与标定偏差及红移中的本动速度影响；结合SH0ES的42个造父变星校准器，采用贝叶斯分层模型联合拟合光变曲线、尘埃消光、红移和标定不确定性，并测试多种模型扩展（如本动速度相关性、宿主星系质量步长、双stretch种群等）的稳健性。

Result: 得到H₀ = (71.6 ± 1.5) km/s/Mpc（平坦q₀先验）或(72.3 ± 1.1) km/s/Mpc（加入Pantheon+ Ωₘ先验），统计误差主要受限于校准器数量；结果对多种系统误差和模型扩展具有鲁棒性，并与以往距离阶梯结果一致，但比Planck值高2.7σ–4.1σ。

Conclusion: Ia型超新星距离阶梯本身目前未发现足以解释哈勃张力的系统误差，张力根源更可能来自该方法之外（如早期宇宙物理、系统性未建模效应或新物理）。

Abstract: Type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia) are instrumental for a precise measurement of the Hubble constant ($H_0$) from the local distance ladder, yet the data used hitherto for this purpose are inhomogeneous and subject to severe selection effects. We present the first, preliminary, cosmological analysis of 772 SNe Ia from the Zwicky Transient Facility's second data release: an untargeted single-telescope sample whose volume-limited subset (z<0.06) is largely free of Malmquist bias. We correct the public data for known instrumental and calibration biases and the redshifts for peculiar motions. Using the 42 SH0ES calibrators (their host distances computed from Cepheids and geometric anchors) and a Bayesian hierarchical model of SN Ia standardisation, dust extinction, redshift and calibration uncertainties, we infer $H_0 = (71.6 \pm 1.5)$ km/s/Mpc after marginalising over deceleration with a flat prior (or $(72.3 \pm 1.1)$ km/s/Mpc if a prior on matter density from Pantheon+ is imposed), with the uncertainty dominated by the number of calibrators. We demonstrate robustness to the choice of light-curve and Milky Way dust models and to extensions of the model to include peculiar-velocity correlations, a host-mass step, and two stretch populations. These results are in agreement with previous distance-ladder measurements and $2.7σ$ ($4.1σ$) away from the value obtained from Planck data, suggesting that the resolution of the Hubble tension lies outside of SNe Ia---or the distance ladder entirely.

</details>


### [164] [All Dark Matter as Primordial Black Holes: a Gravitational-Wave Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36177)
*A. Ianniccari,A. J. Iovino,G. Perna,D. Perrone,A. Riotto*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，通过探测轻质量原初黑洞落入高速自转的超大质量黑洞所形成的球对称尖峰时产生的随机引力波背景，来检验暗物质完全由小行星质量原初黑洞构成的假设，并探讨了LISA和DECIGO等未来空间引力波探测器观测该信号的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验暗物质是否完全由小行星质量的原初黑洞构成这一假设。

Method: 利用轻质量原初黑洞在高速自转超大质量黑洞周围形成的球对称尖峰中并合所产生的随机引力波背景进行探测。

Result: 分析表明该引力波信号有望被未来空间引力波观测站LISA和DECIGO探测到。

Conclusion: 该方法为验证原初黑洞作为全部暗物质成分的假说提供了新的可观测途径。

Abstract: We propose a novel test for the hypothesis that the dark matter of the Universe is composed entirely by asteroidal-mass primordial black holes. It relies on detecting the stochastic gravitational-wave background generated by the coalescence onto rapidly spinning supermassive astrophysical black holes of the light primordial black holes accumulated in spherically symmetric spikes. We discuss the scenarios in which the signal can be observed by the future space-based gravitational-wave observatories LISA and DECIGO.

</details>


### [165] [Non-minimal $α$-attractor magnetogenesis with potential dependent couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36228)
*Antonino G. Marino,Tommaso Mengoni*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了由非共形耦合驱动的暴胀磁生成，特别是在大场α-吸引子模型中，通过引入势依赖的电磁耦合函数f(φ)，分析其对电磁放大及最终宇宙磁场强度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暴胀时期如何产生足够强的原初磁场以解释当前观测到的星系际磁场的问题，同时考虑非最小耦合和量子电动力学效应（如Schwinger对产生）的影响。

Method: 基于Starobinsky暴胀模型，解析推导(E)-模型和(T)-模型下的电磁耦合函数；结合φ²R型非最小引力耦合，利用梯度展开形式处理电磁反作用与Schwinger对产生，并进行数值模拟。

Result: 发现非最小耦合参数ξ显著影响电磁场演化与最终磁场振幅；在满足CMB约束的前提下，最小耦合（ξ=0）与正ξ情形可得到当前宇宙磁场强度约10⁻²³ G。

Conclusion: 在所分析的大场α-吸引子暴胀框架下，适当选取电磁耦合函数与非最小耦合强度ξ，可在不违背现有宇宙学观测限制的前提下实现可行的原初磁生成。

Abstract: We investigate inflationary magnetogenesis driven by a non conformal coupling in the case of large-field $α$-attractor inspired models. We introduce potential-dependent electromagnetic coupling functions $f(φ)$ that reproduce the scaling $f\propto a^p$ during slow roll. Starting from Starobinsky inflation, we analytically derive explicit couplings for $(E)$-model and $(T)$-model potentials. We then study how these functions affect electromagnetic amplification in the regime of non-minimal coupling between the inflaton field and the Ricci scalar $φ^2 R$. In particular, we compare different values for the coupling strength $ξ$, accounting for electromagnetic backreaction and Schwinger pair production through the gradient expansion formalism. Our numerical results show that the non-minimal coupling affects the evolution of the electromagnetic sector and the resulting magnetic-field amplitude. We find that, within the analyzed configurations, minimally coupled and $ξ>0$ scenarios are compatible with the adopted CMB constraints, yielding conditional present-day magnetic-field estimates of order $10^{-23}$ G.

</details>


### [166] [Towards Reliably Measuring the Mass-Size Relation of Dark Matter Halos: Impact of projection, miscentering and Eddington bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36531)
*Charlie T. Mpetha,Benedikt Diemer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在观测中隐藏暗物质晕质量-尺寸关系的多种系统性效应，特别是投影效应和亮度散射导致的爱丁顿偏差，并提出了一种简单的弱引力透镜质量校准方法来恢复真实的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管溅回半径（splashback radius）是暗物质晕的物理边界，但观测中尚未验证其与200倍平均物质密度半径之比随质量减小的理论预期，需探究隐藏该关系的观测效应。

Method: 通过将真实的星系团搜寻流程应用于纯暗物质数值模拟，构建模拟观测样本，量化投影效应、亮度散射引起的爱丁顿偏差及中心偏移对平均密度和透镜剖面的影响。

Result: 发现投影效应与亮度散射共同导致显著的质量-可观测量关系偏差及全尺度密度剖面压制；该效应质量依赖且与搜寻算法和巡天特性耦合；简单弱透镜质量校准可较好恢复真实质量-尺寸关系；严重中心偏移才会系统性偏置尺寸测量，但任何偏移均增大测量不确定性。

Conclusion: 观测中质量-尺寸关系被复杂系统性效应掩盖，需前向建模；而弱透镜质量校准是一种有效且实用的缓解策略。

Abstract: In the conventional spherical overdensity definition of a dark matter halo, masses and radii, e.g. $M_{200{\rm m}}$ and $r_{200{\rm m}}$, are equivalent. However, halos do exhibit a physical boundary at the splashback radius $r_{\rm sp}$, which manifests as a sharp drop in density. Splashback radii are now routinely measured in stacked weak lensing profiles, but the expected trend of decreasing $r_{\rm sp}/r_{200{\rm m}}$ with mass has not been verified in observed galaxy groups or clusters. In this work, we show that several observational effects conceal the predicted mass-size relation. In particular, we quantify the impact of systematics on the mean density and lensing profiles of mock-observed halo samples, which are created by applying a realistic cluster-finding procedure to dark matter-only simulations. We reproduce the known impact of projection: preferentially observing systems in overdense regions. But, differing from previous works, we show that the same projection effect, combined with scatter in observed luminosities, causes a significant Eddington bias in the derived mass-observable relation and a complex suppression in mean density profiles over all radial scales. The magnitude of this effect is mass-dependent and coupled to the cluster-finding procedure and survey properties, making it challenging to model simply and motivating full forward-modeling of observed group and cluster samples. However, we demonstrate that a simple mitigation approach, effectively a weak lensing mass calibration, does well in approximately recovering the true mass-size relation. Finally, we show that only severe miscentering can bias the measurement of the halo size, but any miscentering can increase the measurement uncertainty.

</details>


### [167] [Warm inflation beyond the Gaussian limit: primordial black holes and induced gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36591)
*Mayukh R. Gangopadhyay,Nilanjana Kumar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暖通胀模型中热辐射浴对原初涨落的影响，特别是记忆时间和强度分布如何影响原初黑洞（PBH）形成与诱导引力波（IGW）信号，并指出PBH对涨落高阶统计（如峰度）更敏感，而IGW主要依赖功率谱，因此二者可协同用于区分不同物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分暖通胀中热浴对原初涨落的记忆时间（影响功率谱形状）和强度分布（影响极端涨落），以理解原初黑洞与诱导引力波的产生机制差异。

Method: 构建一个受暖通胀启发的简单模型，其中独立随机强度的‘踢’叠加形成窄峰功率谱；分析不同峰度（kurtosis）下原初黑洞丰度与诱导引力波振幅的关系，并比较峰值计数、谱宽变化等修正效应。

Result: 在固定引力波振幅下，峰度仅0.1–0.3的小范围变化即可使原初黑洞形成率提升10至10⁶倍（尤其对稀有大质量PBH）；改变计数方式或展宽谱最多仅降低该增强因子约4倍；功率谱变化同时影响PBH与IGW，而峰度仅影响PBH。

Conclusion: 原初黑洞与诱导引力波构成互补探针：前者对涨落高阶统计（如峰度）高度敏感，后者主要响应功率谱；联合观测可有效区分暖通胀中热浴的不同微观特性。

Abstract: In warm inflation, the primordial fluctuations are driven by random kicks from a hot radiation bath. Two features of these kicks matter: how long the bath remembers them, which shapes the power spectrum, and how their strengths are spread, which controls the rare, extreme fluctuations. In this paper, we investigate how primordial black holes and the accompanying induced gravitational waves can be used to discriminate between these two features. We use a simple model inspired by warm inflation, in which independent kicks of random strength add up to a narrow peak in the power spectrum. At leading order, the induced gravitational wave depends only on the power spectrum and is therefore insensitive to how the kicks are distributed. However, the black holes are very sensitive to these rare large fluctuations. Hence, two baths can yield the same gravitational wave signal while forming black holes at very different rates. A modest kurtosis range of 0.1 to 0.3 enhances black-hole formation by factors of ten to a million at fixed gravitational-wave amplitude, the larger factors for the rarer black holes. Counting peaks rather than all points, or widening the spectrum, lowers the enhancement by at most a factor of about four. In general a change in the power affects both phenomena, while the kurtosis affects only the black hole production.

</details>


### [168] [Relativistic N-body simulations with mass-varying neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36752)
*Øyvind Christiansen,Julian Adamek,Mainak Baidya,Vitor da Fonseca,Eric V. Linder,David F. Mota*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了中微子质量随宇宙时间变化的模型，其中中微子与作为暗能量的精质场耦合，并通过全动力学、相对论性的N体和格点模拟研究结构形成，发现中微子团块具有高度动态性和自旋特性，并探讨了有效暗能量可能出现幻影穿越的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙学数据对中微子质量的限制弱于中微子振荡实验结果，从而激发了对中微子质量随宇宙时间演化的研究兴趣。

Method: 构建中微子与精质场（quintessence）耦合的模型，采用全动力学、相对论性N体与格点模拟，包含标量场的非线性演化及弱场度规展开。

Result: 模拟中普遍出现中微子团块，且这些团块具有高度动态性和自旋；若干模型中有效暗能量（含标量场非线性效应对宇宙膨胀的影响）可呈现幻影穿越行为；相对论模拟框架有助于通过观测中微子与暗能量性质、以及引力势滑移（源于各向异性应力）来区分该模型与LCDM模型。

Conclusion: 质量可变中微子模型展现出丰富现象学，值得进一步探索并发展相关模拟技术。

Abstract: Recent cosmological data provide weak hints for smaller masses than what is required by neutrino-oscillation experiments, motivating investigations into variation of the neutrino masses with cosmic time. Here we study a model where the neutrinos couple to a quintessence field that acts as dark energy. We compute structure formation using fully dynamical and relativistic N-body and lattice simulations, with nonlinear field evolution and a weak-field metric expansion. Similar to previous studies, we find the generic appearance of neutrino lumps, but observe them to be highly dynamical and spinning. We study several models where the effective dark energy, including nonlinear effects of the scalar field on the cosmic expansion, can show phantom crossing. The relativistic physics available in our simulation framework can help distinguish such models from LCDM, through observations of neutrino and dark energy properties, and slip between the gravitational potentials due to anisotropic stress. The rich phenomenology offered by mass-varying neutrinos motivates further exploration and development of the simulation techniques.

</details>


### [169] [Cosmological constraints on single-field inflation models from ACT/SPT data and observations at sub-Mpc scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37383)
*Qing-Yang Wang,Jing-Zhi Zhou,Fei-Yu Chen,Zhi-Chao Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了能在亚兆秒差距尺度上产生大曲率扰动，同时与多尺度宇宙学观测一致的单场暴胀模型，提出了三种可行的暴胀势能形式，并通过CMB、BAO、PBH、PTA和CMB畸变等观测数据对其参数空间进行了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在亚兆秒差距尺度上增强原初扰动与满足ACT/SPT测得的标量谱指数ns之间的张力问题。

Method: 提出三类暴胀势能（隆起/凹陷修正型、阶跃型、双拐点型），计算其原初功率谱，并利用CMB、BAO、PBH、PTA和CMB畸变等多尺度观测数据约束参数空间。

Result: 部分模型可同时满足ACT/SPT约束与当前PTA观测，但兼顾PBH丰度限制仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 所提出的三类单场暴胀模型在理论上可行，且部分参数区域与当前多尺度宇宙学观测兼容，为解释PTA信号提供了新思路，但需进一步协调PBH约束。

Abstract: We investigate single-field inflation models that can produce large curvature perturbations at sub-Mpc scales while remaining consistent with cosmological observations across multiple regimes. We highlight the tension between generating enhanced small-scale perturbations and maintaining agreement with the scalar spectral index $n_s$ measured by ACT and SPT, which disfavors several conventional single-field inflation models. To address this issue, we propose three viable classes of inflationary potentials: bump/dip-modified, step-like, and double-inflection-point. Each can sufficiently amplify primordial perturbations while predicting $n_s$ that lie within the $2σ$ range of current observational bounds. In addition, we examine the primordial power spectra predicted by these models and constrain their parameter spaces using cosmological observations at different scales, including CMB, BAO, PBH, PTA, and CMB-distortion. Our results show that some of the models considered can simultaneously satisfy the ACT/SPT constraints and account for the current PTA observations, although doing so while respecting the constraints on the PBH abundance remains challenging.

</details>


### [170] [Getting Ready For Analysing Rubin Data : CLMM v2, The Updated LSST-DESC Cluster Weak Lensing Mass Modeling Library For Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37455)
*Michel Aguena,Céline Combet,Shenming Fu,Constantin Payerne,Marina Ricci,Camille Avestruz,Eduardo Barroso,de Oliveira Caio,Zhou Conghao,Hsin Fan,Calum Murray,Anna Niemiec,Nikolina Sarcevic,Tae-hyeon Shin,Rance Solomon,Radhakrishnan Srinivasan,Sunayama Tomomi,Yuanyuan Zhang,Joe Zuntz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CLMM v2.0代码库，用于从弱引力透镜效应中约束星系团质量，专为Vera C. Rubin天文台LSST数据设计，支持堆叠分析、WL多极矩等功能，并已应用于模拟与实测数据验证，具备开源、文档完善、示例丰富等特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对Vera C. Rubin天文台即将发布的LSST数据，需可靠、可扩展的弱引力透镜（WL）分析工具来开展星系团质量测量与宇宙学研究。

Method: 开发并升级Python开源库CLMM至v2.0版本，集成弱透镜建模与数据处理模块，新增面向LSST的堆叠分析、WL多极矩计算等功能，并在LSST-DESC模拟数据、前期巡天及Rubin验证数据上进行测试。

Result: CLMM v2.0已成功应用于多种星系团弱透镜分析，包括模拟数据、前期巡天和Rubin验证数据，具备支撑首批LSST星系团宇宙学分析的能力。

Conclusion: CLMM v2.0是一个功能完备、经过验证、开源易用的弱透镜分析工具，为LSST时代的星系团质量测量与宇宙学研究提供了关键软件基础设施。

Abstract: The Dark Energy Science Collaboration has prepared a multitude of pipelines to process the upcoming Vera C. Rubin Observatory data and perform different scientific analyses. Here, we present the methodology to constrain the mass information of galaxy clusters from weak lensing, which is encompassed by the v2.0 release of the Cluster Lensing Mass Modeling (CLMM) code. This Python library provides the ingredients for both the modeling and data reduction of lensing observables, and the improvements in the new version were customized to handle the first WL analyses of LSST clusters, including, e.g., stacking functionalities and WL multipoles. This new version of CLMM has allowed various cluster analyses using LSST-DESC simulated data, precursor survey and Rubin commissioning and survey validation data, and is ready to be used for the first cluster cosmology analyses. CLMM is an open-source software package collaboratively written in Python, providing multiple example notebooks, including a set that can reproduce all figures in this work, and extensive documentation, publicly available at https://lsstdesc.org/CLMM/.

</details>


### [171] [A self-supervised machine learning approach to identify instrumental systematics in 21cm Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37596)
*Ajith Sampath*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督卷积自编码器，利用射电干涉仪漂移扫描观测中仪器状态恒定、天空旋转变化的特性，将可见度数据的隐空间分解为仅含仪器系统误差和仅含天空信号的两部分，成功高精度预测未知仪器谱纹幅度（R²=0.9975），并验证仪器隐向量不含可测天空信号，为21cm强度映射中的系统误差建模与前景清除提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电干涉仪存在仪器系统误差（如波束宽度谱纹），会将强前景泄漏至信号主导模式，阻碍宇宙学21cm信号（特别是BAO）的探测；现有方法依赖前向建模，而本文旨在探索无需前向模型的自监督学习方案以实现仪器-天空分离。

Method: 基于漂移扫描中仪器状态不变、天空随时间变化的物理约束，构建卷积自编码器，强制其隐空间分为两个子空间：一个编码仪器系统状态（如谱纹幅度），另一个编码天空信号；通过模拟数据训练，并用线性探针评估仪器状态预测性能及天空信号污染程度。

Result: 对15种未见过的仪器状态，仪器系统状态预测R²达0.9975，远优于随机编码器基线（R²=-3.55）；仪器隐向量经总功率天空代理检验，未检测到天空信号；天空与仪器隐向量呈部分解耦但未完全分离，天空隐向量纯度是当前主要挑战。

Conclusion: 该自监督模型能有效识别并表征仪器系统误差，为ML驱动的21cm前景清除流程提供了可行路径；完全的仪器-天空解耦是下一步自然目标。

Abstract: The primordial sound waves in the baryon-photon plasma are imprinted on the large-scale galaxy distribution as Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (BAO), serving as a cosmological standard ruler to constrain dark energy and the Hubble parameter. The upcoming 21cm intensity-mapping arrays are designed to map the 21cm emission across large volumes and measure the BAO scale. However, radio interferometers suffer from instrumental systematics that can hinder detection of the cosmological 21cm signal by leaking bright foregrounds into the signal-dominated modes. This work aims to demonstrate that a self-supervised model can predict the instrument systematic state (here, the amplitude of a spectral ripple in the beam width) in interferometric visibilities and potentially disentangle the instrument from the sky without a forward model. Based on the fact that an instrument stays the same as the sky rotates in a drift-scan observation, and that the same sky is observed under different instrument states, we train a convolutional autoencoder to compress the information in the simulated visibilities and separate this information into instrument-only and sky-only latent vectors. The result shows excellent prediction of the unknown instrument systematic for 15 held-out instrument states with $R^2 = 0.9975$ with a linear probe, against $R^2 = -3.55$ for a random-encoder baseline. The instrument-latent carries no detectable sky signal, as measured with a total-power sky proxy, for any held-out test data. The sky-latent vector and instrument-latent vector show partial separation but not complete disentanglement, identifying sky-latent purity as the primary open problem. The results show the ability of the model to recognize the systematic and show a promising path towards an ML-based foreground cleaning pipeline. Full instrument-sky disentanglement constitutes the natural next step.

</details>


### [172] [Cosmological Constraints on Interacting Dark Matter with SPT-3G D1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37604)
*Cynthia Trendafilova,Fei Ge,Ali Rida Khalife,Christian L. Reichardt*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用最新的CMB（SPT-3G D1、Planck、ACT DR6）和BAO（DESI）数据，对暗物质-重子散射、暗物质-暗辐射相互作用及暗物质湮灭三种暗区模型进行限制，在95%置信水平下给出了更严格的参数上限，并指出小尺度CMB透镜数据对提升前两类模型约束起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过宇宙学观测（如CMB和BAO）探测暗物质非平凡相互作用，提供地面实验之外的暗物质性质研究途径。

Method: 结合SPT-3G D1 CMB温度/极化各向异性数据、D1 MUSE透镜数据，以及Planck、ACT DR6和DESI BAO数据，对三类暗物质相互作用模型进行贝叶斯参数估计与限制。

Result: 在95%置信水平下得到：σ_DM-b < 4.6×10⁻²⁶ cm²；Γ_0,nadm < 5.2×10⁻⁸ Mpc⁻¹；p_ann < 3.7×10⁻²⁸ cm³/s/GeV；其中前两者精度提升主要得益于SPT-3G D1小尺度透镜测量。

Conclusion: 当前多组宇宙学数据已能对多种暗物质相互作用模型施加强约束，但暗物质湮灭与再电离光学深度存在简并；结合DESI等新数据时，参数限制可能因试图调和数据间张力而看似收紧；未来更高精度CMB与BAO观测将进一步厘清该张力并提升暗物质相互作用检验能力。

Abstract: Models of the dark sector in which the dark matter participates in nontrivial interactions can create detectable signatures in cosmological observables, providing non-terrestrial means of probing the nature of dark matter. In this work, we test dark matter-baryon scattering, dark matter-dark radiation interactions, and dark matter annihilation using measurements of the CMB and BAO. We leverage the latest SPT-3G D1 CMB measurements of the primary anisotropies, alongside the D1 MUSE lensing data, to set up-to-date bounds on these models. From the combination of Planck, ACT DR6, and SPT-3G D1 data, we constrain the velocity-independent dark matter-baryon scattering cross-section to $σ_\mathrm{DM-b} < 4.6\times10^{-26}~\mathrm{cm^2}$, the dark matter-dark radiation momentum transfer rate to $Γ_\mathrm{0,nadm}<5.2\times10^{-8}\,\text{Mpc}^{-1}$, and the effective dark matter annihilation parameter, quantifying the fractional thermally averaged cross-section, to $p_\mathrm{ann}<3.7\times10^{-28}~\mathrm{cm^3/s/GeV}$, all at the 95% confidence level. The improved sensitivity to the first two models is in particular driven by the precision measurements of CMB lensing at small scales provided by SPT-3G D1 MUSE lensing data. For annihilating dark matter, due to the degeneracy of the effect with the optical depth to reionization, we observe a non-trivial relationship between the upper bound on $p_\mathrm{ann}$ and the large-scale polarization information. When combining CMB data with the latest measurements from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), upper bounds on extended model parameters can appear to tighten as the model space attempts to accommodate the known discrepancy between these data. Future CMB and BAO measurements will provide better clarity on reconciling this discrepancy and enhance our ability to test dark matter interactions.

</details>


### [173] [Improving cosmological distance measurements at high redshift using Lyman-Alpha forest BAO reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37664)
*Boryana Hadzhiyska,Roger de Belsunce*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将标准Zel'dovich重构方法扩展到Lyman-alpha森林的新方法，以校正非线性结构形成对BAO信号的展宽和偏移效应，并在AbacusSummit模拟上验证了其有效性，显著提升了DESI等巡天对宇宙膨胀历史测量的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性结构形成会展宽并偏移Lyman-alpha森林中BAO峰，影响DESI对宇宙膨胀历史的精确测量，需发展适用于Lyman-alpha森林的重构方法。

Method: 将Zel'dovich重构推广至Lyman-alpha森林；使用AbacusSummit N体模拟生成理想化森林模拟；引入线性控制变量压制样本方差；在周期性全采样盒子及DESI类观测掩模下测试重构效果，并比较直接由森林或外部示踪体（如LBGs、LAEs）估计位移场的两种策略。

Result: 在全采样盒子中，重构使径向BAO阻尼尺度减半，α∥和α⊥测量误差分别降低约25%和15%，径向偏移从+0.3%降至+0.10±0.07%；在DESI类掩模下，两种位移场估计方式均使α∥和α⊥误差分别降低约27%和10%；FOM提升因子达1.65–1.76。

Conclusion: 该扩展Zel'dovich重构方法可有效校正Lyman-alpha森林BAO的非线性畸变，显著提升DESI及未来高密度巡天对宇宙学参数的约束能力，且具备实际观测应用可行性。

Abstract: The Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) measures baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) in the Lyman-$α$ (Lyman-alpha) forest and provides a robust probe of the expansion history in the matter-dominated era. Yet, non-linear structure formation broadens and shifts the BAO peak. To undo these effects, we extend standard Zel'dovich reconstruction, routinely applied to galaxy surveys, to the Lyman-alpha forest. We validate our methodology on large-volume idealized Lyman-alpha forest mocks painted on top of the \textsc{AbacusSummit} $N$-body simulations, using linear control variates to suppress sample variance and thereby reduce the error bars by a factor of up to $\approx \sqrt{50}$. In fully sampled periodic boxes, reconstruction halves the radial BAO damping scale and reduces the uncertainties on the radial ($\apar$) and transverse ($\aperp$) scaling parameters by $\approx25\%$ and $\approx15\%$, respectively. It also reduces the non-linear shift from $Δ\apar=+0.3\%$ to $+0.10\pm0.07\%$, while $\aperp$ remains unbiased. To make this approach readily applicable to observational data, we demonstrate the impact of a survey mask with a DESI-like density of $50\,\mathrm{deg}^{-2}$. For this mask, we compare two approaches to estimate the displacement field: either directly from the Lyman-alpha forest or using an external tracer with a number density of $\bar n \simeq 10^{-3}\,h^3\mathrm{Mpc}^{-3}$, such as Lyman-break galaxies and Lyman-$α$ emitters---both key targets for DESI-II. Both approaches give comparable gains and reduce the uncertainties on $\apar$ and $\aperp$ by $\approx 27\%$ and $\approx 10\%$, respectively, with mild improvements for futuristic high-density surveys with $160\,\mathrm{deg}^{-2}$. Correspondingly, the figure of merit improves by factors of $1.66$ for fully sampled boxes, $1.65$ for a DESI-like, and $1.76$ for a high-density survey.

</details>


### [174] [Axion Inflation: Perturbative control in the strong backreaction regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02498)
*Valerie Domcke,Alexandros Papageorgiou,Marco Peloso,Stefan Sandner*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究轴子暴胀中的强反作用区域，其中阿贝尔规范场通过伪标量相互作用产生的摩擦与哈勃摩擦相当；通过一阶梯度展开和一环in-in计算，发现大耦合β下存在一个扩展的温和反作用区域，并指出CMB非高斯性对轴子-规范场耦合与哈勃参数乘积设定了普适上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强反作用区域在轴子暴胀中具有重要现象学意义，但因规范场非线性动力学而难以用微扰方法研究，需量化微扰可控性。

Method: 采用两种方法：(i) 包含轴子梯度的一阶梯度展开形式；(ii) 一环in-in计算，以直接评估谱反作用对微扰控制的影响。

Result: 发现在大耦合β下存在一个扩展的温和反作用区域，粒子产生参数ξ较大且近似恒定；并导出CMB非高斯性给出的普适约束βH/M_p < 1.4×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 强反作用区域虽具挑战性，但仍可在一定参数范围内实现微扰可控；CMB非高斯性为轴子暴胀模型提供了关键、模型无关的耦合上限。

Abstract: We study the strong backreaction regime in axion inflation, in which the friction from Abelian gauge fields generated via the pseudo-scalar interaction $\fracβ{4 M_p} φF {\tilde F}$ is comparable to Hubble friction. The non-linear dynamics associated with the gauge fields render this regime phenomenologically particularly interesting, but also notoriously difficult to study with perturbative methods. Quantifying the perturbative control through the direct impact on the spectral backreaction using (i) a first-order gradient-expansion formalism including axion gradients, and (ii) a one-loop in-in calculation, we discover an extended mild backreaction regime at large couplings $β$, with a relatively large, nearly constant particle production parameter $ξ$. In passing, we point out that CMB non-Gaussianity bounds impose a general upper limit on the product of axion gauge field coupling and Hubble parameter during inflation, $βH/M_p < 1.4\cdot 10^{-3}$, independently of the choice of the axion potential.

</details>
